### [1] [3D-BBDA: Backpropagation-Free Black-Box Domain Adaptation for Robust 3D Point Cloud Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03588)
*Shivangi Rai,Kunal Jangid,Vaibhav Kumar,Vinod K Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 3D-BBDA：一种无需反向传播的黑盒域适应框架，仅利用源模型输出将 3D 点云分类器适配到无标签目标域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 点云分类器在传感器变化、环境差异和采集伪影导致的域偏移下性能下降；现有适应方法常需源数据或源模型内部参数，受隐私、许可和安全约束难以部署。

Method: 该方法进行伪标签引导的基于原型的对齐，并用加权预测模块融合源 logits 与对齐 logits 以降低噪声伪标签敏感性；当伪标签覆盖不完整时，从无标签目标预测构建高斯扰动原型先验，作为缺失或不可靠类别原型的回退。

Result: 在四个损坏 3D 基准上稳定优于强基线。ModelNet-40C 上，3D-BBDA 平均错误率为 PointNet 32.08%、GDANet 29.62%，优于 Source-only（35.88%/34.03%）和 BFTT3D（33.78%/32.36%）。

Conclusion: 3D-BBDA 仅需单次前向、无梯度更新，适合实时且隐私保护的可迁移 3D 识别流水线部署。

Abstract: Robust 3D point-cloud recognition is critical for robotics, mapping, and urban sensing, yet point-cloud classifiers often suffer performance drops under domain shifts caused by sensor changes, environmental variation, and acquisition artifacts. Many existing adaptation methods are difficult to deploy in practice because they require access to source data or the internal parameters of the source model, which may be restricted by privacy, licensing, or security constraints. We present 3D-BBDA, a backpropagation-free black-box domain adaptation framework that adapts a source-trained predictor to unlabeled target data using only black-box outputs. Our method performs pseudo-label guided prototype-based alignment and employs a weighted prediction module to fuse source logits with alignment logits, reducing sensitivity to noisy pseudo-labels. To stabilize prototype memory when pseudo-label coverage is incomplete, we construct a Gaussian-perturbed prototype prior from unlabeled target predictions and use it as a fallback for missing or unreliable class prototypes during alignment. Experiments on four corrupted 3D benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines. On ModelNet-40C, 3D-BBDA achieves mean errors of 32.08% (PointNet) and 29.62% (GDANet), outperforming Source-only (35.88% / 34.03%) and BFTT3D (33.78% / 32.36%). 3D-BBDA requires only a single forward pass with no gradient updates making it suitable for real-time, privacy-preserving deployment in transferable 3D recognition pipelines. Project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [2] [Evaluating Depth-Augmented Visual Features for Cross-Dataset Lunar Traversability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03430)
*Jan Baturo,Bartosz Ptak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建基于稀疏成对标注的弱监督月面可通行性估计框架，并评估深度、表面法向量及组合几何描述子对视觉特征的增强效果；结果显示朴素几何增强无稳定收益，训练源域选择才是跨域迁移质量的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面自主移动受通信延迟和视线遮挡限制，连续遥操作不可行；深度学习方法虽用于可通行性估计，但地球类比环境与真实月面存在域差距，且真实月面可通行性标签稀缺，完全监督训练困难。

Method: 采用弱监督框架，在稀疏成对标注上训练；将深度衍生几何信息（深度、表面法向量、组合几何描述子）与视觉特征结合；在两个地球类比数据集和真实嫦娥PCAM数据集上进行域内与跨域评估，并在资源受限边缘设备上运行。

Result: 与预期相反，朴素几何增强在域内和跨域设置中均未稳定优于强视觉基线，其影响相对于随机种子间方差较小；训练源域成为迁移质量的主导因素，域相关源域训练的模型迁移到真实月面图像的效果显著优于几何融合对单一模型的改进。

Conclusion: 研究提供了受控基线，提示未来月面感知系统应优先关注域相关训练数据和稳健评估，而非简单几何融合；并为设计更具表达力的多模态融合架构奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous mobility is essential for sustained lunar surface operations, as communication delays and line-of-sight occlusions limit continuous teleoperation. Although recent traversability estimation methods increasingly rely on deep learning, the domain gap between terrestrial analogue environments and real lunar terrain can limit their generalisation, and real lunar traversability labels are scarce, making fully supervised training difficult. To address these challenges, we build a weakly supervised framework trained on sparse pairwise annotations and study whether augmenting visual features with depth-derived geometric information: depth, surface normals, and combined geometric descriptors. We evaluate the models in both in-domain and cross-domain settings using two terrestrial analogue datasets and the real lunar Chang'E PCAM dataset. Contrary to expectations, we find that naive geometric augmentation yields no consistent improvement over a strong visual-only baseline in either setting, with effects small relative to seed-to-seed variance. Instead, the choice of training source emerges as the dominant factor in transfer quality: models trained on domain-relevant sources transfer substantially better to real lunar imagery than the geometric fusion strategy improves any single model. Our evaluation runs onboard a resource-constrained edge device and provides a controlled baseline for designing more expressive multimodal fusion architectures in future lunar perception systems.

</details>


### [3] [MoSoLoco: Monocular 3D Human Localization through Visual Latent Features and Social Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03189)
*Yasamin Borhani,Javad Khoramdel,Yang Gao,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSoLoco 是一种逐帧单目 3D 人体定位器，复用姿态检测器内部视觉特征与 2D 骨架，并可选融合同帧稀疏多人上下文，在 JRDB/NuScenes 上提升定位精度且保持高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航与运动预测需要从单目相机估计人的度量 3D 位置；基于轻量姿态的定位器适合算力受限硬件，但通常丢弃姿态估计器计算出的丰富视觉表示。

Method: 提出 MoSoLoco：无需额外 RGB 或深度编码器，将姿态检测器的内部视觉特征与检测到的 2D 骨架结合进行逐帧单目 3D 人体定位；并引入稀疏社会交互模块，利用同一图像中邻近检测结果细化个体位置估计。

Result: 在 JRDB 和 NuScenes 上，复用姿态检测器表示可改善定位，JRDB 最佳误差相对最强关键点基线降低约 23%；稀疏多人上下文可进一步细化；受控轨迹预测实验显示下游收益；完整流程适合在 Jetson AGX Xavier 上高效推理。

Conclusion: 复用姿态检测器内部特征并结合稀疏同帧多人上下文，可在不增加额外视觉/深度编码器的前提下提升单目 3D 人体定位，并兼顾下游任务收益与边缘设备效率。

Abstract: Estimating the metric 3D position of people from a monocular camera is important for robot navigation and motion prediction. Lightweight pose-based localizers are attractive for robotic hardware with limited computational resources because they operate on compact 2D keypoints. However, they typically discard the richer visual representations computed by the pose estimator. We introduce MoSoLoco, a per-frame monocular 3D human localizer that combines the pose detector's internal visual features with the detected 2D skeleton, without requiring an additional RGB or depth encoder. MoSoLoco can further incorporate sparse same-frame multi-person context to refine individual position estimates. Experiments on JRDB and NuScenes show that reusing pose-detector representations improves localization, reducing the best JRDB error by approximately 23 percent relative to the strongest evaluated keypoint-based baseline. A sparse social-interaction module incorporates nearby detections from the same image for further refinement. Controlled trajectory-forecasting experiments show downstream benefits, while the complete pipeline remains suitable for efficient Jetson AGX Xavier inference.

</details>


### [4] [Scene-Adaptive Readout and Decoding in Diffusion Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02076)
*Seungjun Yu,Jiho Choi,Inha Kang,Hojun Choi,Seungwook Kim,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对扩散式视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在每个去噪阶段重复执行骨干导致的高推理开销，作者发现轨迹读出存在“深度-解码解耦”和场景差异，提出场景自适应读出与解码框架，在保持或提升规划质量的同时显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散驱动VLA推理成本高，且不同场景是否需要相同解码计算尚不明确；需要区分轨迹何时已可读、还需多少进一步细化。

Method: 分析不同网络深度与解码进度下的轨迹读出，发现后层表示在首次前向扫描后即可支持有用轨迹读出，而前层表示更受益于继续解码，且最佳读出层与解码深度随场景变化。据此提出场景条件读出MoE，在单次骨干前向后自适应加权组合各后层轨迹预测，并用自适应解码策略仅在场景需要时执行额外原生细化。

Result: 在两个扩散驾驶VLA及Waymo E2E、nuScenes基准上评估：验证RFS最高提升0.56，测试性能亦提升；Waymo E2E端到端最高加速6.0倍，nuScenes上持续降低轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 场景级读出与解码计算分配能够避免不必要的解码，同时提升规划质量与推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion driving vision-language-action models repeatedly execute the backbone at every denoising stage, incurring substantial inference cost, while it remains unclear whether all scenes require the same decoding computation. To distinguish when a trajectory becomes readable from how much further refinement it requires, we analyze trajectory readout across network depth and decoding progress. We identify a depth-decoding dissociation in which late-layer representations already support useful trajectory readout after the first forward sweep, whereas early-layer representations benefit more from continued decoding. We further find that both the preferred readout layer and decoding depth vary across scenes. Based on these observations, we propose a scene-adaptive readout and decoding framework. A scene-conditioned readout MoE adaptively weights and combines trajectory predictions from layer-specific late-layer representations after a single backbone sweep, while an adaptive decoding policy applies additional native refinement only when required by the scene. We evaluate our framework on two diffusion driving VLAs across the Waymo E2E and nuScenes benchmarks. Our method improves validation RFS by up to 0.56 while also improving test performance, and achieves an end-to-end speedup of up to $6.0\times$ on Waymo E2E, while consistently reducing trajectory error on nuScenes. These results demonstrate that scene-level readout and decoding allocation improves planning quality while avoiding unnecessary decoding. Our code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [5] [4D Triangle Splatting Reconstruction of Dynamic Endoscopic Scenes from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02034)
*Laura Salort-Benejam,Stamatia Giannarou,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自监督三角面片溅射框架SurgicalTS，用于单目内窥镜视频的动态4D场景重建，通过规范化三角面片与形变网络联合优化，实现软组织形变和器械-组织交互建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜动态重建受非刚性组织形变、光照变化、遮挡和未知相机轨迹影响；可靠4D重建可提升微创手术可视化、诊断准确性和术中引导。

Method: 以规范化三角面片集合表示场景，与形变网络联合优化；引入基于四元数的形变模型、改进颜色参数化、时空先验及重定义初始化策略，以提升遮挡下完整性和物理一致性，采用自监督学习。

Result: 在多种术式的活体内窥镜视频上，SurgicalTS成功建模软组织形变与工具-组织交互，持续优于现有方法，并减少训练时间，同时保持有竞争力的几何与光度精度。

Conclusion: 基于四元数且具物理一致性的形变建模可有效支持稳健、高效的内窥镜场景4D重建。

Abstract: Robust 4D reconstruction of deformable endoscopic scenes from monocular videos remains challenging due to non-rigid tissue deformation, illumination changes, occlusions, and unknown camera trajectories. Reliable dynamic reconstruction of endoscopies could significantly enhance visualization, diagnostic accuracy and intraoperative guidance of minimally invasive procedures. We propose SurgicalTS, a self-supervised triangle-splatting framework for dynamic endoscopic scene reconstruction from static monocular cameras. The scene is represented by a canonical set of triangles jointly optimized with a deformation network. We introduce a quaternion-based deformation model, an improved color parametrization, spatio-temporal priors, and a redefined initialization strategy that improves completeness under occlusions. Extensive evaluation on challenging in-vivo endoscopy videos from diverse procedures shows that SurgicalTS successfully models soft-tissue deformations and tool-tissue interactions over time, while consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Our method also reduces training times while maintaining competitive geometric and photometric accuracy results, highlighting the effectiveness of quaternion-based deformation modelling with physical consistency for robust and efficient 4D reconstruction of endoscopic scenes.

</details>


### [6] [GatedVLA with Camera-Based Emergency Braking: AlpaSim E2E Closed Loop Challenge Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01663)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Naoki Takada,Makoto Kawano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GatedVLA 是面向 PAI-AV 赛道的 VLA 驾驶策略：以 Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B 为视觉语言主干，结合逐层 flow-matching 动作专家输出 2.5 秒加速度与曲率控制，并在交叉注意力上施加软视觉掩码；部署时加入规则式 AEB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升视觉-语言-动作驾驶策略在开环与闭环场景中的表现，尤其关注左转误差与部署安全性，并评估软视觉掩码和 AEB 的实际增益。

Method: 将驾驶预训练的 Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B VLM 与逐层 flow-matching 动作专家配对，输出 2.5 s 时域的加速度和曲率控制；对动作专家交叉注意力施加软视觉掩码。闭环部署时额外加入规则式自动紧急制动 AEB 模块。

Result: 与同训练基线相比，掩码策略整体开环误差相当，但降低左转误差。在相同 150 个场景、每场景两次 rollout 的本地闭环评测中，视觉掩码将平均场景分从 0.479 提升至 0.517（配对差 +0.038，95% bootstrap CI [-0.018, +0.093]，胜/负 37/26，符号检验 p=0.21）。部署加入 AEB 后平均分进一步升至 0.599（+0.082，CI [+0.023, +0.143]），有责碰撞场景平均数从 34.0 降至 11.0。

Conclusion: 软视觉掩码在左转和闭环得分上呈正向趋势，但闭环提升未达统计显著；规则式 AEB 带来显著闭环得分提升并大幅减少有责碰撞，是更明确的部署安全增益。

Abstract: This report presents GatedVLA, our entry for the Physical AI AV (PAI-AV) track. Our learned vision-language-action (VLA) policy pairs the driving-pretrained Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B vision-language model with a layer-wise flow-matching action expert that outputs a 2.5 s horizon of acceleration and curvature controls, augmented with a soft visual mask applied to the expert's cross-attention. Compared to an identically trained baseline, the masked policy achieves comparable overall open-loop error while reducing error on left turns. In local closed-loop evaluation across the same 150 scenes (two rollouts per scene), the visual mask increases the mean scene score from 0.479 to 0.517 (paired difference +0.038, 95% bootstrap CI [-0.018, +0.093], wins/losses 37/26, sign test p = 0.21). Incorporating a rule-based automatic emergency braking (AEB) module at deployment further increases the mean score to 0.599 (+0.082, CI [+0.023, +0.143]) and reduces the mean number of scenes with at-fault collisions from 34.0 to 11.0.

</details>


### [7] [UA-Depth: Uncertainty-Aware Depth Restoration for Material-Agnostic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01645)
*Rui Cao,Jia-Hui Pan,Yin Zhang,Qiwei Meng,Jiapeng Li,Gang Wang,Runsong Zhu,Wei Chen,Chi-Wing Fu,Jiangliu Wang,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知深度修复框架UA-Depth，迭代联合优化深度与像素级不确定性，并在零样本多数据集基准及下游6D位姿估计与抓取合成中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商品RGB-D相机在透明、反射、半透明等非朗伯物体上易产生空洞、结构化噪声和深度偏置；现有修复方法多只输出单张深度图，对噪声区域与可靠区域同等对待，忽略区域不确定性。

Method: UA-Depth迭代共优化深度和像素级不确定性，利用不确定性门控特征抑制不可靠线索以稳定更新；引入冻结深度基础模型作为先验初始化缺失或不可靠深度；采用两阶段MAD加权对数深度尺度归一化，降低相对深度到度量深度对齐中的异常值偏置。

Result: 构建覆盖多场景、多材质、多传感器的八数据集零样本基准；UA-Depth在代表性基线中取得最佳平均排名，在透明与反射物体主导的数据集上提升尤其显著；利用预测不确定性还改善了类别级6D位姿估计和6D抓取合成。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的深度修复能有效提升非朗伯场景下的零样本深度恢复质量，并将不确定性用于下游机器人感知与操作任务，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires accurate metric depth, yet commodity RGB-D cameras often fail on non-Lambertian objects such as transparent, reflective, and translucent materials, producing holes, structured noise, and biased depth. Most restoration methods produce only a single depth map, treating noisy and reliable regions uniformly for downstream tasks while ignoring regional uncertainty. We present UA-Depth, an uncertainty-aware depth restoration framework that iteratively co-refines depth and pixel-wise uncertainty, and uses uncertainty-gated features to suppress unreliable cues for stable updates. To strengthen zero-shot behavior, UA-Depth leverages a frozen depth foundation model as a prior to initialize missing or unreliable depth, and applies two-stage MAD-weighted log-depth scale normalization to reduce outlier bias in relative-to-metric alignment. We introduce an eight-dataset zero-shot benchmark across diverse scenes, materials, and sensors. Across these benchmarks, UA-Depth achieves the best mean rank among representative baselines, with particularly strong gains on datasets dominated by transparent and reflective objects. Beyond depth metrics, we demonstrate downstream improvements in category-level 6D pose estimation and 6D grasp synthesis by exploiting the predicted uncertainty. \emph{Code, models, and data for training and evaluation will be released upon publication.}

</details>


### [8] [DriftingPlanner: Drifting Generative Models for One-Step Trajectory Planning in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01283)
*Ziming Liu,Yizhan Qu,Leichen Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftingPlanner 将 drifting 生成模型引入端到端轨迹规划：仅用 drifting loss 训练单步生成器，并通过成对反噪声解码与平均消除单正样本导致的壳层偏差，在 NAVSIM navtest 上取得 93.75 PDMS 的 SOTA，单步推理接近十步扩散/流匹配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐标回归规划快但会把多种合理机动混合成一条可能不属于任何机动的轨迹；扩散和流匹配能建模轨迹分布但需多步推理。Drifting 模型可将迭代移入训练实现单步生成，但原方法面向类条件图像生成，每类有大量正样本；规划中每个场景是独立条件，跨场景池化会错误吸引，按场景计算又只有专家轨迹一个正样本，互斥会使生成轨迹停留在其周围壳层并偏离真值。

Method: 提出 DriftingPlanner，完全不使用坐标回归项，仅以 drifting loss 训练轨迹规划器。按场景计算吸引-排斥场；推理时去除壳层：将每个噪声向量与其相反向量组成反噪声对并在一次批处理中解码，对每对输出取平均以抵消相对壳层中心的线性位移。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 上达到 93.75 PDMS 的最新最好水平；其 drifting 解码器在单步推理下与最佳扩散或流匹配设置仅差 0.13 PDMS，而后者需要十步推理。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 drifting 模型在单正样本规划场景中的壳层偏差问题，实现了无需坐标回归、单步推理且强性能的轨迹规划，缩小了单步生成与多步扩散/流匹配方法之间的差距。

Abstract: End-to-end planners decode the ego trajectory either by coordinate regression, which is fast but blends plausible maneuvers into one trajectory that may follow none of them, or by diffusion and flow matching, which model the trajectory distribution but need several inference steps. Drifting models move this iteration into training: a one-step generator learns to follow an attraction--repulsion field between generated and ground-truth samples. Drifting was developed for class-conditional image generation, where every class supplies many positive samples. In planning, every scene is its own condition. Pooling scenes would attract the generated trajectories of one scene to the ground truth of unrelated scenes, whereas computing the field per scene leaves a single positive, the expert trajectory. Mutual repulsion then holds the generated trajectories on a shell around it, so any single generated trajectory deviates from the ground truth. We present DriftingPlanner, a trajectory planner trained with the drifting loss alone, without any coordinate regression term. It computes the field per scene and removes the shell at inference: it decodes every noise vector together with its negation in one batched pass, and averaging each antithetic pair cancels the linear part of the displacement from the shell center. On NAVSIM navtest, DriftingPlanner reaches a state-of-the-art 93.75 PDMS, and with single-step inference its drifting decoder comes within 0.13 PDMS of the best diffusion or flow matching setting, which needs ten inference steps.

</details>


### [9] [SceneBound: How Much Should a Streaming 3D Reconstruction Model Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00756)
*Ziming Wang,Chuyue Gong,Chutian Wang,Jingmin Zhu,Hongliang Cao,Yuheng He,Xiuyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBound 是一种训练无关的流式三维重建关键帧写入规则：仅当预测相机位姿与所有已存关键帧的距离至少为固定半径时才写入新帧，从而在控制内存/状态漂移的同时提升轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式三维重建模型需将历史帧写入循环状态、快速权重或 KV cache，但每次写入会改变后续相机坐标系或占用 GPU 显存；现有训练无关门控规则依赖当前帧信号，且其写入频率/预算对平均精度影响显著。

Method: 提出 SceneBound：利用预测相机位姿做几何覆盖判断，仅当新相机位姿距每个已存关键帧至少固定半径时写入；关键帧数量与访问位姿集合的覆盖数保持常数因子关系，并在大场景中当解码首帧再次暴露漂移时关闭子图。

Result: 在四个已发布模型和三类记忆类型上使用同一半径；在 CUT3R 的循环状态与 StreamVGGT 的 KV cache 中，单次遍历和九次遍历场景下均取得所评估训练无关规则中最低平均轨迹误差，关键帧仅增长 3% 到 6%。

Conclusion: SceneBound 通过位姿覆盖约束实现训练无关的关键帧写入，能在不依赖当前帧信号的情况下控制写入预算与状态漂移，并在多种流式三维重建架构中一致提升轨迹精度。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction models keep past video frames in a recurrent state, fast weights from test-time training, or a key-value (KV) cache. Each written frame shifts the coordinate frame of later cameras or occupies GPU memory. Training-free rules decide what to write from a signal of the current frame. How much they write accounts for their mean accuracy: spending the update budget of a gate at uniformly spaced frames, with no signal, matches nine published gates, and the budget bounds the drift of the state wherever the writes fall. We propose SceneBound, a training-free rule that writes a frame only when its predicted camera pose lies at least a fixed radius from every stored keyframe. Its keyframe count stays within a constant factor of the covering number of the visited poses, the fewest views that keep a keyframe near every camera, so the count grows with the places visited and stays flat when the camera returns. In large scenes, SceneBound closes a submap once decoding its first frame again reveals drift. SceneBound uses one radius across four released models and three memory types. In the recurrent state of CUT3R and the KV cache of StreamVGGT, it achieves the lowest mean trajectory error among the published training-free rules we evaluate, in one pass and over nine passes of a scene, where its keyframes grow by 3% to 6%.

</details>


### [10] [3D Neural Rendering for Automated Ultrasonic Laser Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00527)
*Thomas Pierson,Matthew Mitchell,Kira Perna,Anand Iyer,Eric Flynn,Adam Wachtor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作结合六自由度机器人操控与复数NeRF神经渲染，从稀疏激光多普勒测振扫描重建三维零件稳态表面波场，用于缺陷检测与表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造零件部署前需要检测缺陷和裂纹；现有LDV扫描难以直接获得复杂三维构件的完整表面波场。

Method: 用搭载压电换能器的UFactory xArm6机械臂在已知姿态下将试件呈现给固定LDV，获得复数二维扫描，姿态由机械臂运动学解析获得而非图像位姿估计；提出complex-nerfacto，将标准NeRF三通道RGB输出改为两通道实部/虚部速度输出，保留复数测量且不量化；以ZNCC对焊接铝圆柱和焊接T型支架重建与实测扫描进行评估。

Result: 在留出视角下，复数管线平均ZNCC为0.60，而基于同一数据渲染图像训练的RGB nerfacto基线为0.28；重建表面波场支持复杂构件缺陷检测与表征。

Conclusion: 机器人LDV扫描结合complex-nerfacto可有效重建复数表面波场并服务缺陷检测，但从稀疏LDV扫描重建几何仍是实际部署的主要障碍。

Abstract: Laser doppler vibrometer (LDV) scans can be used to detect defects and cracks in manufactured parts before they are deployed. This work presents a method for reconstructing the steady-state surface wavefield of a three-dimensional part by combining automated robotic manipulation with neural rendering. A six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) robotic manipulator (UFactory xArm6) with an embedded piezoelectric transducer presented an acoustically excited test article to a stationary LDV at a sequence of known orientations, yielding a set of complex-valued 2D scans with poses derived analytically from the arm kinematics rather than from image-based pose estimation. These scans were used to train a novel complex-nerfacto model, a modified Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) pipeline that replaces the standard three-channel RGB output head with a two-channel output. Complex-nerfacto represents the real and imaginary velocity components of a wavefield, preserving the complex measurement without quantization. Reconstructions of a welded aluminum cylinder and a welded T-bracket were evaluated against measured scans using Z-normalized cross-correlation (ZNCC). The complex-valued pipeline achieved a mean ZNCC of 0.60 at held-out viewing angles, compared with 0.28 for a baseline RGB nerfacto model trained on rendered images of the same data. The resulting surface wavefield reconstructions supported defect detection and characterization on complex components. We identify geometry reconstruction from sparse LDV scans as the remaining obstacle to practical deployment.

</details>


### [11] [A Straight Line Is Not a Camera Pose: Auditing Short-Clip Pose Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00482)
*Xinyi Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示标准ATE的轨迹对齐会导致无图像匀速直线在Sintel上优于多数模型，且位置对齐忽略相机朝向；提出将对齐旋转拟合到预测朝向的pose-anchored ATE，以极小开销更好反映重投影几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATE是SLAM与前馈3D重建中常用的相机运动精度指标，但其对齐方式可能奖励简单运动并忽略朝向，导致低ATE不一定代表更好的几何重建；需要诊断ATE的偏差并给出修正指标。

Method: 分析ATE对齐的两个性质：任意匀速预测会被映射到真实轨迹的最佳拟合直线，ATE因此等于该直线的回归残差；用残差信噪比SNR预测将预测轨迹移向匀速拟合是否会降低ATE。指出仅位置对齐对相机朝向不敏感，将轨迹相对朝向旋转30度可保持ATE不变。提出pose-anchored ATE：在对齐中把旋转拟合到预测相机朝向，仅需改一个函数，耗时0.17 ms/clip（ATE为0.11 ms）。

Result: 在Sintel上，无图像匀速直线的平均ATE低于11个近期模型中10个官方代码复跑结果。SNR准则预测1056个model-clip对中95%、792个留出对中98%、1034个ScanNet对中97%的ATE变化方向，并区分Sintel（SNR中位数0.86）与TUM、ScanNet、全长Bonn和Wild-SLAM（2.7至27），后者拉直轨迹会提高所有模型平均ATE。位置对齐下，将轨迹相对朝向旋转30度不改变ATE，却在Sintel、TUM、ScanNet主设置中恶化所有模型的静态点重投影；Sintel上按平均ATE第二的直线在几何平均重投影上最差。Pose-anchored ATE在Sintel上将每个无图像参考排在每个模型之下，能跟踪旋转和拉直下的重投影误差（Spearman 0.61 vs. ATE 0.32），基本保持模型排序，并在旋转与参考测试上与10帧平移RPE及DTE相当。

Conclusion: 标准ATE因对齐方式可能误导模型评估；pose-anchored ATE以很低计算成本增强了对相机朝向和几何质量的敏感性，是更可靠的轨迹评估指标，代码、预测和结果将公开。

Abstract: The absolute trajectory error (ATE) is the standard measure of camera-motion accuracy, from SLAM to feed-forward 3D reconstruction. On the common Sintel protocol, a constant-velocity straight line using no images has a lower mean ATE than 10 of 11 recent models re-run with official code. Two properties of ATE's alignment explain this. First, it maps any constant-velocity prediction onto the best-fitting line through the true path, so the line scores the path's regression residual. A residual signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) criterion predicts when moving a prediction towards its constant-velocity fit lowers ATE. It gives the outcome for 95% of 1056 model-clip pairs, 98% of 792 held-out pairs and 97% of 1034 ScanNet pairs, and it separates Sintel (median SNR 0.86) from TUM, ScanNet and full-length Bonn and Wild-SLAM (2.7 to 27), where straightening raises every model's mean ATE. Second, position-only alignment ignores where cameras look: rotating a trajectory by 30 degrees against its orientations leaves ATE unchanged, yet worsens the reprojection of static points for every model on Sintel, TUM and ScanNet in the main setting, and the line, second by mean ATE on Sintel, places them worst by geometric mean: a lower ATE need not mean better geometry. Fitting the alignment's rotation to the predicted orientations gives pose-anchored ATE, a one-function change in ATE's units costing 0.17 ms per clip (ATE: 0.11 ms). It ranks every image-free reference below every model on Sintel, tracks the reprojection error under rotation and straightening, where ATE does not (Spearman 0.61 vs. 0.32), largely preserves the models' order, and ties 10-frame translational relative pose error (RPE) and the discernible trajectory error (DTE) on our rotation and reference tests. Code, predictions and results will be released.

</details>

### [12] [When appearance and motion disagree: evaluating class--motion consistency against disguised uncrewed surface vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00367)
*Jon Reifschneider*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文评估海上目标防御中计算机视觉与航迹检查的脆弱性，并提出基于AIS航迹的混合重放协议用于对抗性攻击与防御方法的对比测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上资产防御系统需在足够预警时间内区分接近的良性船只与无人水面艇（USV）。岸基摄像机视觉系统可区分敌意与良性目标，但可能遭受对抗性船体补丁或物理伪装攻击，因此需要评估这些攻击及相应防御。

Method: 作者评估对抗性船体补丁与物理伪装对检测的影响，并开发一种基于AIS航迹的混合重放协议：将记录的良性运动与直接或离轴攻击航迹结合，用于可重复的防御评估。

Result: 对抗性船体补丁在最多29%的尝试中抑制检测，但从未导致误分类为良性；物理伪装可绕过摄像机检查和雷达存在检查。对标注为休闲船只、在4海里内直接接近的攻击，资产相对接近率检查在15–20节时报警率为94–95%，而仅航迹速度检查为12%；两种检查在离轴接近时互补，可将攻击者限制在双阈值以下并延长到达时间。任一检查报警的整体留出误报率为4.9%，休闲交通上为12.3%。

Conclusion: 基于重放的评估协议支持防御方法的比较性测试与评估；接近率与速度等航迹检查可与视觉检查互补，提高预警能力但需考虑误报率。

Abstract: Defense systems for maritime assets must distinguish approaching benign craft from uncrewed surface vessels (USVs) with sufficient warning to act. Shore cameras using computer vision can separate hostile vessels from benign traffic, but these systems may be attacked using an adversarial hull patch or physical disguise. We evaluate adversarial hull patches and find they suppress detection in up to 29% of attempts but never cause misclassification as benign. Physical disguise, however, can bypass both a camera check and a radar presence check. To evaluate defenses, we develop a hybrid replay protocol from automatic identification system (AIS) tracks that combines recorded benign motion with direct or off-axis attack run-ins. We find that for recreational-labeled vessels attacking via direct run-in within 4 nautical miles, an asset-relative closing-rate check flags 94-95% at 15-20 knots, compared with 12% for a track-only speed check. The checks are complementary on off-axis approaches, constraining the attacker to remain below both thresholds and extending the time required to reach the asset. Flagging on either check has a 4.9% held-out false-alarm rate overall and 12.3% on recreational traffic. The replay-based evaluation protocol supports comparative test and evaluation of defense methods.

</details>

### [13] [Gibbs Variational Last Layers for Long-Horizon Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02891)
*Paul Brunzema,Henrik Hose,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从Gibbs变分推断视角重新审视变分贝叶斯最后层（VBLL），将后验推断与训练损失解耦，并引入多步训练损失以提升动力系统长期概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VBLL能以接近确定性的成本提供可处理不确定性，但现有训练仅用单步似然目标，难以保证多步自回归展开的可靠性；因此需要直接针对多步预测质量进行优化。

Method: 基于Gibbs变分推断解耦后验推断与损失选择，构建递进式多步训练损失族，逐步引入长期结构、基于连续排序概率分数（CRPS）的鲁棒性，以及在完整自回归rollout上训练，同时保留VBLL的简洁性。

Result: 在示意性合成环境、多种混沌动力系统和真实数据上，多步且基于CRPS的目标显著提升长期预测精度与分布拟合，并持续优于单步似然训练。

Conclusion: 多步训练可释放VBLL在长期概率预测中的潜力，为基于模型的决策提供更可靠的预测。 

Abstract: Accurate long-horizon probabilistic predictions of dynamical systems are a prerequisite for model-based decision-making. Variational Bayesian last layers (VBLLs) are an attractive model class for this setting, offering tractable uncertainty at near-deterministic cost, yet they are trained exclusively with single-step likelihood objectives that may be insufficient for reliable multi-step rollouts. We revisit VBLLs through the lens of Gibbs variational inference, which decouples posterior inference from the choice of training loss. Building on this perspective, we introduce a family of multi-step training losses that progressively incorporate long-horizon structure, robustness via continuous ranked probability scores (CRPS), and training on full autoregressive rollouts. Our approach retains the simplicity of VBLLs while directly optimizing for multi-step predictive quality. Across illustrative synthetic environments, a diverse suite of chaotic dynamical systems, and real-world data, we show that multi-step, CRPS-based objectives substantially improve long-horizon accuracy and distributional fit, consistently outperforming single-step likelihood training.

</details>


### [14] [Beyond Validation Loss: Coverage and Reliability Dynamics in Robot Policy Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01565)
*Zhanyi Sun,Max Simchowitz,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用闭环 pass@k 评估生成式机器人策略预训练，揭示其对后续 posttraining 的影响，并给出离线选点与生成式数据增强两种改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练指标如验证损失和平均任务成功率（pass@1）无法完整反映策略是否既适合直接部署，又有利于后续强化学习或监督微调等 posttraining。

Method: 研究闭环 pass@k，即同一初始条件下采样 k 次至少一次成功的概率；pass@1 衡量平均成功率，大 k 的 pass@k 衡量重复采样下成功是否仍可达。并在多种 posttraining 配方下比较不同检查点。

Result: 大 k pass@k 能揭示 pass@1 未捕获的 posttraining 差异；pass@1 相近但大 k pass@k 不同的检查点，其 posttraining 表现不同；监督预训练中 pass@k 在不同检查点达到峰值，体现覆盖度与可靠性权衡。基于此提出离线检查点选择准则和生成式数据增强，后者在保持大 k pass@k 的同时维持或提升 pass@1，并提升覆盖度-可靠性 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: 评估、检查点选择和训练流程应显式考虑 posttraining，而不能仅依赖传统预训练指标。

Abstract: In modern robot learning, generative robot policies are first pretrained through supervised learning from demonstrations, then refined through reinforcement learning, supervised finetuning, or other forms of posttraining. Yet conventional pretraining metrics, such as validation loss and average task success, provide an incomplete account of which policies support both effective direct deployment and subsequent posttraining. In this work, we study closed-loop pass@k, which evaluates the probability that k rollouts from the same initial condition attain at least one success. Thus, pass@1 measures mean task success; large-k pass@k measures whether success remains reachable under repeated sampling. Across a wide range of posttraining recipes, we show that large-k pass@k reveals posttraining differences not captured by pass@1. In particular, checkpoints with similar pass@1 but different large-k pass@k have different posttraining performance. Moreover, we show pass@k peaks at different checkpoints during supervised pretraining, revealing a coverage-reliability tradeoff over the course of training. Based on these observations, we propose two practical interventions: (1) a purely offline checkpoint-selection criterion that balances coverage and reliability without closed-loop evaluation, and (2) a generative data augmentation procedure that expands supervision beyond the finite demonstration set, preserving large-k pass@k while maintaining or improving pass@1 and raising the observed coverage-reliability Pareto frontier. Together, our results motivate evaluation, checkpoint selection, and training procedures that explicitly account for posttraining. Blog post, visualizations, and code are available at this https URL .

</details>

### [15] [Spectral and Conformal: Neural Network Methods for Calibrated Trajectory Prediction and Control-Point Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00231)
*Jasmine Ratchford,Raffaele Romagnoli*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种结合谱骨干神经网络、改进保形分位数回归与区间算术的轨迹可行性/安全性评估框架，在树莓派Zero 2 W上实现四旋翼闭环LQR轨迹推理3.45 ms、全流程9.81 ms（约100 Hz）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统需快速评估期望轨迹的可行性与安全性；数值积分计算量大，而快速数据驱动代理缺乏数值方法的形式化保证。

Method: （1）用带谱骨干的神经网络学习轨迹及分位数带，并内置初始条件与稳定性约束；（2）采用改进的保形分位数回归提供校准的轨迹级联合覆盖；（3）用区间算术将无噪声覆盖保证扩展到控制点；随后重构网络用于推理。

Result: 四旋翼在闭环线性二次调节器下，轨迹推理时间为3.45 ms（Raspberry Pi Zero 2 W）；包含轨迹推理的总流程时间为9.81 ms，约100 Hz，与典型控制频率一致。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持形式化覆盖保证的同时实现毫秒级轨迹评估，适用于自主系统实时控制中的轨迹可行性与安全性分析。

Abstract: Autonomous systems that navigate their environment need methods to quickly assess the feasibility and safety of desired trajectories. Traditional solutions that rely on numerical integration can be computationally heavy, while fast data-driven surrogates lack the formal guarantees that numerical approaches provide. This work demonstrates a framework that uses (1) a neural network with a spectral backbone to learn trajectories and quantile bands with built-in constraints (initial conditions and stability); (2) a modified conformal quantile regression to provide calibrated, joint trajectory-level coverage; and (3) interval arithmetic to extend noise-free coverage guarantees to control points. After refactoring the network for inference, the trajectory inference time for a quadrotor under a closed-loop linear quadratic regulator is 3.45~ms on a Raspberry Pi Zero 2 W. The total pipeline time, including trajectory inference time, is 9.81~ms, or approximately 100~Hz, consistent with typical control rates.

</details>

### [16] [Catenary Visibility: A Geometric Framework for Planning with Tethered Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00324)
*B. Ábrego,J.M. Díaz-Báñez,S. Fernández,D. Flores-Peñaloza,V. Sánchez-Canales*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 论文提出“悬链线可见性”概念，用于地面机器人与系留无人机组成的系统，并给出面向障碍环境的可见性感知规划几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系留无人机可为地面机器人持续供电并显著扩展作业范围，但需判断在给定三维目标和障碍下，地面机器人可部署在哪些位置，以及对应缆长是否可行。

Method: 将缆绳建模为长度有界的可伸长悬链线；先求解二维子问题：障碍、目标和地面机器人运动线均限制在单一竖直平面内，计算运动线上全部可行部署点及每个点的可行缆长范围，并处理给定地面位置的碰撞-free缆长范围查询；再将方法推广到三维，针对一类受限障碍给出三维解。

Result: 提出了计算可行部署点集（即从三维目标点悬链线可见的地面点集合）的几何算法；二维情形可完整给出运动线上的可行发射点及对应可行缆长区间，并支持位置查询；三维情形在受限障碍类别下得到推广解。

Conclusion: 该工作为系留火星车式/母舰式机器人系统提供了可见性感知规划的几何基础，将悬链线约束、避障与缆长可行性统一到部署位置计算中。

Abstract: This paper introduces \emph{catenary visibility}, a new geometric visibility concept for a robotic system consisting of a ground robot tethered to an aerial drone. The tether is modeled as an extensible catenary curve with bounded length, providing sustained power and significantly extending operational range. We define a point on the ground plane as \emph{catenary-visible} from a target point in 3D if there exists a collision-free catenary connecting them, given a set of obstacles. This paper introduces a geometric framework for visibility-aware planning of a tethered marsupial system, addressing the computational challenge of determining the feasible deployment locus: the collection of points in the ground plane that are catenary-visible from a given target in 3D space. We begin by solving the 2D subproblem, where the obstacles, target, and the ground vehicle's line of motion are all constrained to a single vertical plane. Our algorithm for this case computes the complete set of feasible launch points along the motion line and simultaneously determines the range of viable cable lengths for each. We also consider the query problem of finding all collision-free length ranges for a given location of the ground robot. Building upon this planar solution, we then generalize the approach to three dimensions, deriving a 3D solution for a constrained class of obstacles.

</details>

### [17] [Defining Autonomous Weapon Systems: A schematic framework for refined purpose-driven definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02916)
*Björn Lundgren,Nathan G. Wood*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出自主武器系统（AWS）定义存在严重分歧，主张无需追求单一普适定义，而应建立可对同一概念家族内不同AWS进行分类的定义框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献提出多种AWS定义，学者、政府和国际监管机构之间存在核心分歧；单一界定难以覆盖AWS的多样性，也无法预见未来AWS类型。

Method: 作者评述并批判主流AWS定义类别，说明可对广泛接受的定义进行变体调整，从而构建一个易于修改的定义图式（schema），用于分类不同AWS概念。

Result: 提出一种定义性框架而非唯一本质定义，该框架能够在同一概念家族内容纳并区分不同种类的AWS，并具备面向未来系统的可扩展性。

Conclusion: AWS研究与治理应转向可修改、可扩展的分类框架，以促进概念澄清、类型比较及监管规则的适用。

Abstract: The literature on autonomous weapon systems (AWS) includes many proposed definitions for these (types of) systems, with core disagreements between scholars, governments, and international regulatory bodies. In this article, we present and critique prevailing (classes of) definitions, arguing that what is needed is not a singular definition, but rather a definitional framework which allows one to classify different sorts of AWS within the same conceptual family. Recognizing we cannot foresee all future AWS, we show how variations on a broadly accepted definition can be used to produce a definitional schema which can be easily modified in order to classify various concepts belonging to the class of AWS.

</details>

### [18] [Route-Guided CBF-QP Repair for Safe Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01713)
*Chan Park,Sunwoo Hong,Kirak Kim,Yoonjeong Park,Alexander W. Olson,Ali Pesaranghader*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Route-Guided CBF-QP Repair 后处理修复模块，利用 VLA 预测的短时域动作块检测被障碍阻塞的标称轨迹，并生成绕障的进入-退出-回归引导，将路径作为 CBF-QP 的软引导项，同时保持 CBF 安全约束为硬约束，以提升富障碍操作场景中的安全性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型能生成面向任务的机器人动作，但在富障碍操作场景中不保证执行安全；现有 CBF-QP 安全过滤器虽可将不安全动作局部投影到安全集，却可能使机器人偏离学习到的任务轨迹，且 VLA 策略缺乏可靠的恢复方向。

Method: 提出 Route-Guided CBF-QP Repair 后处理修复模块：使用 VLA 预测的短时域动作块检测被阻塞的标称轨迹，围绕障碍生成进入-退出-回归引导路径；将选定路径作为软引导项加入 CBF-QP 目标函数，同时保留 CBF 安全约束为硬约束。

Result: 在 SafeLIBERO 上，与 AEGIS 相比，该方法在碰撞避免、任务成功率和 SafeSuccess 指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将 VLA 短时域动作预测与 CBF-QP 安全过滤相结合，可在不破坏硬安全约束的前提下为安全修复提供任务相关的恢复方向，从而改善富障碍操作中的安全执行与任务完成表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can generate task-oriented robot actions, but they do not guarantee safe execution in obstacle-rich manipulation scenes. Although CBF-QP safety filters can locally project unsafe actions into a safe set, such correction may push the robot away from the learned task trajectory and leave the VLA policy without a reliable recovery direction. We propose Route-Guided CBF-QP Repair, a post-hoc repair module that uses the short-horizon action chunk predicted by the VLA to detect blocked nominal trajectories and generate entry-exit-rejoin guidance around obstacles. The selected route is added to the CBF-QP objective as a soft guidance term, while the CBF safety constraint remains hard. On SafeLIBERO, our method improves collision avoidance, task success, and SafeSuccess compared with AEGIS. Our project page is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [19] [CalVLA: Shaping Native Confidence in Vision-Language-Action Policies with Outcome Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00394)
*Boniface Chukwuemeka Godwin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CalVLA 利用成功/失败轨迹塑造 VLA 原生动作 token 置信度，使 OpenVLA-7B 的置信度可用于任务失败判别；在 LIBERO-10 子集上失败判别 AUROC 提升至约 0.739，但任务成功率略降，未证明控制或安全收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否直接从视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略自身的动作概率/动作 token 置信度中推断任务失败，从而避免额外失败检测器。

Method: 提出 CalVLA：用成功与失败 rollout 及 Action-Confidence Alignment（ACal）平方 margin-ranking 损失校准原生动作 token 置信度；通过低秩适配器更新 OpenVLA-7B，不引入辅助失败检测器。训练/比较时将同一任务内归一化已用时间相近的观测配对，但这些观测不一定具有等价物理进度。

Result: 在来自 500 个 LIBERO-10 配置的留出测试集上，校准后置信度的联合失败判别 AUROC 为 0.7392±0.0480（3 个随机种子，平均 130 与 260 步得分）。冻结策略探针为 0.6146–0.6436，但其相对线性探针的优势统计上不显著。同一置信度汇总下，AUROC 从适配前 0.4485 升至适配后 0.7327。平均任务成功率从 58% 降至 55%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 CalVLA 改善了失败判别能力，但未改善控制性能，也未建立安全性收益。

Abstract: Can a vision-language-action (VLA) policy signal likely task failure from its own action probabilities? We introduce CalVLA, which shapes native action-token confidence using successful and failed rollouts and an Action-Confidence Alignment (ACal) squared margin-ranking loss. Low-rank adapters update OpenVLA-7B without an auxiliary failure detector. Observations are paired within tasks at similar normalized elapsed times, not necessarily equivalent physical progress. On a held-out test split from 500 LIBERO-10 configurations, adapted confidence achieves a joint failure-discrimination AUROC of $0.7392\pm0.0480$ over three seeds, averaging scores at 130 and 260 steps. Frozen-policy probes reach 0.6146--0.6436, but superiority over the linear probe is statistically inconclusive. For the same confidence summary, AUROC rises from 0.4485 before adaptation to 0.7327 after adaptation. Mean task success decreases from 58% to 55%. These results show improved failure discrimination, not improved control or established safety benefits.

</details>

### [20] [From Concepts to Actual Causes: Toward Closing the Concept-Malfunction Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00155)
*Gihyuk Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该立场论文指出，机制可解释性（MI）虽能揭示机器人基础模型学到的概念，但不足以定位具体事故的内部致因；作者用实际因果框架形式化故障隔离问题，分析现有MI方法的缺陷，并提出四项开放研究任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型事故后的分析需要回答一个具体因果问题：哪些内部组件导致了此次失败、影响程度如何。现有MI主要建立一般性因果主张，解释策略学习了哪些概念，但“知道概念被编码”并不能说明某次具体事故的实际原因，因此存在概念解释与故障归因之间的鸿沟。

Method: 论文将故障隔离置于实际因果框架中，把故障隔离查询形式化为由被干预内部组件、反事实取值、 contingency（伴随条件/背景条件）和读出（read-out）构成的元组，并给出该查询良定义的条件；据此审视当前MI方法，并用玩具示例说明其不足可能导致冲突判定。

Result: 作者识别出现有MI方法的关键缺陷：假定固定的contingency，且只读出即时效应，使备份机制/替代路径掩盖实际原因；在玩具示例中，这些缺陷可导致相互冲突的故障归因结论。

Conclusion: 基于良定义条件，论文提出四项开放研究任务，以期弥合机制可解释性与具体事故故障隔离之间的差距，推动机器人基础模型的事后因果归因。

Abstract: Post-incident analysis of a robot foundation model poses a singular causal question: which internal components caused this failure, and to what degree? Mechanistic interpretability (MI), however, establishes general causal claims by decoding the concepts a policy has learned, yet knowing which concepts are encoded is insufficient for identifying the actual cause of a specific incident. In this position paper, we analyze this concept-malfunction gap by posing fault isolation in the framework of actual causality. Specifically, we formalize a fault-isolation query as a tuple of intervened internal components, counterfactual values, contingencies, and read-outs, and lay out conditions for it to be well-specified. Through this lens, we identify shortcomings of current MI approaches: they assume fixed contingencies and read out only immediate effects, letting backups obscure actual causes. We demonstrate on a toy example that these shortcomings can lead to conflicting verdicts. Lastly, based on the well-specification conditions, we outline four open research tasks toward closing the gap.

</details>


### [21] [Recursive Self-Improvement of Exoskeleton Assistance Policies through Human-in-the-Loop Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01581)
*Muyuan Ma,Shuo Jiang,Lijun Han,Xinpan Meng,Zhenghua Ma,Xiuze Xia,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于人类反馈的外骨骼辅助策略自主演化框架，由智能体生成可执行控制代码，并形成“生成—反馈—评估—自主改进”闭环；四种大模型在单次自主改进后均实现有效辅助，但面对突发情况的自适应性仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少外骨骼辅助策略设计对人工设计和大量参数调优的依赖，并利用人类主观反馈、运动表现和肌电测量自动识别策略缺陷与改进控制代码。

Method: 智能体自主生成可执行的外骨骼辅助控制代码，在人体实验中结合用户主观反馈、运动性能和肌肉激活测量进行评估；根据评估结果自主识别策略不足并修订控制代码，形成闭环迭代。使用GPT、DeepSeek、GLM和Kimi进行对比评估。

Result: 不同模型初始辅助性能存在差异，但在测试条件下经过一次自主改进迭代后四种模型均实现有效辅助；三项任务中肱二头肌和肱三头肌肌肉激活水平相较无外骨骼条件分别降低29.4%和35.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了基于人类反馈的LLM智能体自主生成与改进外骨骼辅助策略的可行性，可显著降低部分肌肉激活；但对突发情况的自适应性有限，后续需提升鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: To reduce reliance on manual design and extensive parameter tuning, a framework for the autonomous evolution of exoskeleton assistance policies through human feedback is proposed. Assistance policies are autonomously generated as executable control code by an intelligent agent and evaluated in experiments with human participants. Subjective user feedback, movement performance, and muscle activation measurements are incorporated into policy assessment. Based on these assessments, policy deficiencies are autonomously identified and the control code is revised by the agent for subsequent trials. A closed loop of policy generation, human feedback, evaluation, and autonomous refinement is thereby established. The framework is evaluated using GPT, DeepSeek, GLM, and Kimi, and initial assistance performance and refinement outcomes are compared across models. Despite differences in initial performance, effective assistance is achieved with all four models after a single autonomous refinement iteration under the tested conditions. Across three tasks, biceps and triceps muscle activation levels are reduced by 29.4% and 35.2%, respectively, compared with those measured without an exoskeleton. However, limited adaptability to unexpected situations is observed, indicating that further improvements in robustness and safety are required.

</details>


### [22] [Democratizing Robot-Use Agents: A Unified Harness with Open Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01560)
*Jiarui Yuan,Yuxin Zuo,Xiaoyu Huang,Wenbo Zhang,Kwanyoung Park,Junlin Yang,Tianxiang Zhang,Ruoqu Chen,Yutao Ouyang,Bingxiang He,Xingtai Lv,Hongjin Wu,Jiajun Liu,Jiahao Zhao,Tianwei Luo,Haozhan Li,Cheng Qian,Jiaxin Ge,Huan-ang Gao,Kaisen Yang,Jiaqi Deng,Qixin Xu,Siqiao Huang,Bowen Zhou,Mengdi Xu,Zhiyuan Liu,Pieter Abbeel,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 uni-robot-agent：一个支持继续训练、面向开放权重机器人使用代理（RUA）的统一评测与训练框架。开箱即用的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 在无特权输入下取得 RoboDojo 31.39，超过已发表的 GPT-6 Astra-RoboProbe 28.97，并在 19 个真实任务上具竞争力；分析表明有效机器人使用与四项通用能力相关，未来应联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RUA 已展现有前景的零样本操作能力，但现有研究依赖前沿闭源模型，限制了对 RUA 的进一步训练与开放研究。

Method: 构建 uni-robot-agent 统一 harness，支持竞争性开放权重机器人使用代理及后续训练；在无特权输入条件下评测，并跨模型代际、模型家族和模型规模分析 RUA 能力与四项通用能力的关系。

Result: 开箱即用的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 取得 RoboDojo Score 31.39，超过已发表的 GPT-6 Astra-RoboProbe 28.97，并优于多个 VLA/WAM 基线；在 19 个真实世界任务上表现有竞争力。

Conclusion: 有效机器人使用可归结为四项通用能力，未来 RUA 训练应联合发展这些能力；开放权重统一框架有助于推动 RUA 研究民主化与可复现训练。

Abstract: Robot-use Agents (RUAs) have shown promising zero-shot manipulation capabilities, but reliance on frontier, proprietary models limits research into further training. To democratize RUA research, we introduce uni-robot-agent, a unified harness for competitive open-weight robot use that supports further training. Without privileged inputs, an out-of-the-box Qwen3.8-Flash-Next achieves a RoboDojo Score of 31.39, surpassing the published GPT-6 Astra-RoboProbe result of 28.97 and many VLA/WAM baselines, and performs competitively on 19 real-world tasks. Our analysis across model generations, families, and sizes links effective robot use to four general capabilities, suggesting that future RUA training should develop them jointly.

</details>


### [23] [$TF^2$-Steering: Action Refinement with Tactile Forecasts and Feedback via Velocity-Field Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01483)
*Yiming Chen,Xiaofei Wei,Chun Gu,Wei Xiao,Peidong Liu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TF^2-Steering：在流匹配动作专家中学习残差转向，并用触觉混合专家融合触觉预测与实时反馈，从而将触觉有效引入视觉主导的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉在插拔、灵巧操作等接触丰富任务中至关重要，但现有机器人策略以视觉为主；由于触觉数据有限且接触事件稀疏，直接融合触觉信号常被忽略，甚至破坏预训练策略能力并导致性能下降。

Method: 该方法通过学习的残差转向修正流匹配动作专家的速度场，采用触觉混合专家架构整合触觉预测和实时触觉反馈，并设计三阶段训练流程，使触觉信号在保留视觉基础策略强动作生成能力的同时，纠正必要时重定向动作生成过程。

Result: 在六个仿真和六个真实机器人操作任务中验证有效性；平均仿真成功率比最强基线高 10.7 个百分点，真实世界平均进度成功率高 9.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: TF^2-Steering 能有效将触觉反馈用于接触丰富操作，在不牺牲视觉预训练策略能力的情况下提升仿真与真实任务表现。

Abstract: Tactile sensing plays a crucial role in everyday human interaction. Consider inserting a plug into a tight socket, vision alone provides limited guidance during the final insertion stage, and we instead rely on both anticipated contact and realized tactile feedback to ajust the insertion motion. The same capability is equally important for robots, particularly in dexterous manipulation tasks. Yet many contemporary robot policies remain vision-dominant. This visual bias, compounded by limited tactile data and sparse contact events, can cause directly incorporated tactile signals to be ignored or even compromise the policy's pretrained capabilities, yielding degrading performance as observed in our experiments. We present $TF^2$-Steering, which refines actions via learned residual steering of the flow-matching action expert's velocity field, using a tactile mixture-of-experts architecture to integrate both tactile forecasts and real-time tactile feedback. Together with specific designed three stages training recipe, $TF^2$-Steering enables tactile signals to correct and, when necessary, redirect the flow-matching action-generation process, while building on the strong action-generation capacity of the vision-based foundation policy. Extensive experiments across six simulated and six real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of $TF^2$-Steering. It outperforms the strongest evaluated baselines by 10.7 percentage points in average simulation success rate and 9.1 percentage points in average real-world progress success rate. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [24] [Dual Path Following for an Aerial Manipulator Under State and Input Constraints via Transverse Feedback Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01442)
*Hamza Tariq,Adeel Akhtar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究四旋翼携带三自由度机械臂的“双路径跟随”问题：组合系统质心需沿飞行器路径运动，末端执行器需沿另一条路径运动，同时满足关节限位并避开奇异位形。作者提出基于横向反馈线性化的二次规划控制器，仅放宽末端沿路径速度，并在Drake中验证其能保持两条路径不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼与机械臂机械耦合，单一控制器必须同时处理质心路径和末端路径；现有级联加安全滤波器方法在速度参考冲突时可能放弃路径约束，因此需要一种能显式处理约束与双路径不变性的控制方法。

Method: 以组合系统质心坐标和末端执行器路径坐标为输出，并对推力作动态扩展，使系统具有无内部动态的规范形；刻画规范形失效状态。将横向反馈线性化控制器构造为二次规划，仅松弛末端执行器沿其路径的速度，把安全与执行器限制作为不等式约束；该规划可化为标量问题并具有闭式解。

Result: 若二次规划始终严格可行，则反馈局部Lipschitz，两条路径均不变且吸引，所有约束满足。在Drake物理引擎中，当两条速度参考冲突时，控制器通过减慢末端执行器解决冲突且两条路径保持不变；相比之下，级联加安全滤波器会离开两条路径。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将横向反馈线性化与仅松弛末端路径速度的闭式二次规划结合，可在机械耦合四旋翼-机械臂系统上实现双路径跟随并显式满足安全、关节与执行器约束，优于会破坏路径不变性的级联安全滤波方案。

Abstract: This paper studies dual path following for a quadrotor carrying a three-degree-of-freedom arm. The center of mass of the combined system must follow a vehicle path and the end effector must follow a second path, while the arm respects its joint limits and avoids its singular postures. The vehicle and the arm are mechanically coupled, so one controller must handle both paths. With the center of mass and the end-effector path coordinates as outputs, and a dynamic extension of the thrust, the system admits a normal form without internal dynamics. We characterize the states where this normal form fails. We formulate the transverse feedback linearizing controller as a quadratic program that relaxes only the speed of the end effector along its path and imposes safety and actuator limits as inequality constraints. The program reduces to a scalar problem with a closed-form solution. If it remains strictly feasible for all time, the feedback is locally Lipschitz, both paths are invariant and attractive, and every constraint holds. In the physics engine Drake, the controller resolves a conflict between the two speed references by slowing the end effector while both paths remain invariant. By contrast, a cascade with a safety filter leaves both paths. Code and animations are available at this https URL

</details>


### [25] [Adaptive Surface Cleaning under Uncertain Cleaning Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01437)
*Ruichen Li,Rachel Holladay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将自适应表面清洁建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出基于信念的规划框架，通过在线更新的局部清洁响应模型自适应调整清洁位置、运动、力与次数，并在栅格仿真中验证闭环适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面污染对清洁的响应在空间上异质，且通常只有在实际交互后才能观测，因此机器人不仅要决定“清洁哪里”，还要根据交互反馈决定“如何清洁”。

Method: 构建POMDP以处理隐藏的局部清洁响应；维护关于潜在局部清洁响应模型的信念，利用已观测清洁结果进行更新，并据此规划后续清洁运动、接触力与清洁遍数，形成闭环自适应规划。

Result: 初步栅格世界仿真表明，闭环规划器可随表面状态演化调整空间分解（清洁区域划分）和清洁动作。

Conclusion: 该工作为污染响应不确定条件下的自适应表面清洁提供了信念感知的闭环规划思路；但结果尚属初步仿真验证，仍需在更真实场景与物理平台上评估。

Abstract: Surface cleaning requires robots to adapt both where they clean and how they interact with contamination whose response to cleaning may vary spatially and remain unobservable prior to interaction. We formulate adaptive surface cleaning as a partially observable Markov decision process and propose a belief-aware planning framework that uses observed cleaning outcomes to update a latent local cleaning-response model and adapt subsequent cleaning motions, forces, and passes. Preliminary grid-world simulations demonstrate the resulting closed-loop planner adapting its spatial decomposition and cleaning actions as the surface evolves.

</details>


### [26] [Serving Agentic Robot Fleets: Workload Characterization, KV-Cache Thrashing, and Admission Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01404)
*Sida He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文刻画并回放了由视觉语言“大脑”、工具调用框架和VLA策略组成的机器人智能体舰队，揭示动作截止时间与大脑KV缓存驻留之间的耦合资源瓶颈，并给出容量规划与准入控制原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人智能体舰队时，大脑需反复访问长且增长的KV缓存，VLA需在严格控制截止时间内执行动作块；两类资源约束相互耦合，现有系统缺乏对持续负载下容量与准入策略的量化理解。

Method: 收集GPT-6-Astra、RPent和pi0.5生成的80个LIBERO-PRO回合（1,862个大脑轮次、1,582个VLA推理块），在H800 GPU上分别剖析大脑与VLA两层，并将智能体程序在vLLM服务引擎上回放。

Result: 大脑上下文达215k tokens，Astra请求中位耗时7.78 s，而环境步间隔仅1.0 s；0.25 s动作块截止时间下单VLA GPU可满足单机器人1%饥饿目标，1.0 s截止时间下实测容量至少32。持续大脑回放中，并发从20升至32使吞吐从约2.4降至0.6 requests/s，重复prefill挤占generation；静态活跃回合上限恢复至2.24–2.25 requests/s，并将完整轨迹程序吞吐从1.30提升至5.00–5.55 episodes/min。

Conclusion: 测量揭示了动作截止时间与大脑KV驻留之间随负载变化的瓶颈转移，为持续运行的机器人智能体舰队提供了容量规划与准入控制的实用原则。

Abstract: Agentic robots combine a vision-language brain, a tool-using harness, and a vision-language-action (VLA) policy. Serving a fleet of these agents introduces two coupled resource constraints: the brain repeatedly revisits long, growing key-value (KV) cache states, while the VLA executes action chunks under tight control deadlines. We characterize 80 LIBERO-PRO episodes generated by GPT-6-Astra, RPent, and pi0.5 (1,862 brain turns and 1,582 VLA inference chunks), profile both layers on H800 graphics processing units (GPUs), and replay the resulting agent programs on a vLLM serving engine. Brain contexts reach 215k tokens; the median Astra request takes 7.78 s, while the intervening environment-step gap is only 1.0 s. Under a 0.25 s action-chunk deadline, one VLA GPU meets the 1% starvation target for one robot; under a 1.0 s deadline, its measured capacity reaches at least 32. In sustained brain replay, increasing concurrency from 20 to 32 robots reduces throughput from about 2.4 to 0.6 requests/s as repetitive prefill displaces generation. A static active-episode cap restores 2.24-2.25 requests/s and raises completed trace-program throughput from 1.30 to 5.00-5.55 episodes/min. Our measurements expose the workload-dependent shift between action deadlines and brain KV residency, and establish practical principles for capacity planning and admission in sustained agentic robot fleets.

</details>


### [27] [Same Path, Less Time: Training-Free Fast Execution of Vision-Language-Action Models under Inference Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01390)
*Fuyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Path-preserving Time Scaling（PTS），一种无需训练的冻结动作分块策略执行层，通过路径层与时间层同时消除推理停顿和慢速演示带来的吞吐损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 部署中，机器人因等待下一动作块推理而停顿，又因模仿慢速谨慎演示而移动过慢；单独修复任一问题的方法难以叠加，因为异步执行与加速都会改变计划进度与实际运动之间的对应关系。

Method: PTS 将机器人运动视为参考路径的时间重参数化，路径仅随新观测变化。路径层对每次重规划进行确定性一步解码，并从推理期间已消耗的进度进入；时间层累加计划增量，在夹爪运动时保持机械臂 1x，并按执行器线性范围和延迟下动作块可行性限制速率。作者还分析错位进入与随机采样对续接误差带来的偏差和方差，并推导加速收益的盈亏平衡成功率。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上对两个冻结 VLA 注入推理延迟，PTS 相比最强可部署基线将任务吞吐分别提升 33%（pi0.5）和 104%（SmolVLA），成功率匹配或更优，在六个模型-套件组合中均最佳，并超过零延迟 1x 执行；与同样一步解码器的异步 1x 执行相比，仅时间层就分别提升 28% 和 38%。在 LIBERO-Dynamic 上，PTS 相比停等执行提升 38%，与异步 1x 执行相当。

Conclusion: PTS 作为冻结动作分块策略的训练无关执行层，可在保持或提升成功率的同时显著提高部署吞吐，并统一处理异步执行与加速执行对计划-执行对应关系的耦合影响。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions, yet deployed robots waste time in two ways that are independent of model capability: they halt while the next chunk is inferred, and they move at the speed of slow, cautious demonstrations. Existing remedies remove one loss each, and stacking them fails, because asynchrony and speed-up each change the correspondence between planned progress and executed motion. We present Path-preserving Time Scaling (PTS), a training-free execution layer for frozen action-chunking policies built on one principle: the robot's motion is a time reparameterization of a reference path, and the path changes only in response to new observations. A path layer decodes every replan deterministically in one step and enters it at the progress consumed during inference. A time layer sums the planned increments, keeps the arm at 1x while the gripper moves, and caps the rate by the actuator's linear range and by chunk feasibility under latency. We show that misaligned entry and stochastic sampling add a bias and a variance to the continuation error, and derive a break-even success rate that states when speeding up pays off. On LIBERO with two frozen VLAs under injected inference latency, PTS raises task throughput over the strongest deployable baseline by 33% for pi0.5 and 104% for SmolVLA while matching or improving the success rate, is best on all six model-suite combinations, and exceeds zero-latency 1x execution. Against asynchronous 1x execution with the same one-step decoder, the time layer alone adds 28% and 38%. On LIBERO-Dynamic it improves on stop-and-wait execution by 38% and is comparable to asynchronous 1x execution.

</details>


### [28] [Programmable Droplets as Active Matter in a Reconfigurable Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01277)
*Ivan Shmarov,Abhishek Sharma,Michael Jirasek,Kristine Laws,Leroy Cronin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出闭环机器人实验平台，实时表征并动态编程多组分水包油液滴的自主行为，引入“持久性”和“多样性”两个描述符，揭示二者存在行为权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接非生命物质与生命物质需要可重构环境与具备自主运动、适应行为和持续动力学的合成系统。自推进液滴可作为研究类生命行为的最小平台，但缺少同时量化持久性与适应性、并支持闭环重配置的指标。

Method: 构建自主闭环机器人平台，实时分析液滴行为并迭代选择配方；探索含与不含外部pH扰动的两个化学组成空间。提出持久性与多样性描述符：持久性通过速度—形状复杂度空间中的时间动力学映射并识别区分活跃/非活跃液滴的持久域；多样性刻画自发或环境诱导的运动变化及液滴在扰动下重定向或适应的倾向。

Result: 持久性与多样性受行为权衡约束：高持久性限制多样性，而高多样性降低持久活动。

Conclusion: 研究提供了量化框架以导航和编程活性液滴的类生命行为，并表明闭环实验可揭示复杂活性物质中的组织原则。

Abstract: Exploring systems that bridge the gap between non living and living matter requires a reconfigurable environment coupled to synthetic systems capable of autonomous motion, adaptive behaviour and persistent dynamics. Self propelled droplets provide a minimal platform for such life like behaviour, but quantitative measures capturing both persistence and adaptability to enable closed loop reconfiguration remain lacking. Here we introduce an autonomous closed loop robotic platform to characterise and dynamically program the autonomous behaviour of multicomponent oil in water droplets. We explored two chemical composition spaces, with and without external pH perturbations, analysing droplet behaviour in real time and iteratively selecting formulations. To quantify this behaviour, we introduce two descriptors, persistence and diversity. Persistence is quantified by mapping temporal dynamics in a speed shape complexity space, where we identify a persistence domain that distinguishes active from inactive droplets. Diversity captures spontaneous or environmentally induced variation in motion and the propensity of droplets to reorient or adapt under perturbation. We show that persistence and diversity are constrained by a behavioural trade off: high persistence limits diversity, whereas high diversity reduces persistent activity. Our results provide a quantitative framework for navigating and programming life like behaviours in active droplets and show how closed-loop experimentation can uncover organising principles in complex active matter.

</details>


### [29] [Shades of Success: Evaluating Manipulation Quality from Object-Centric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01250)
*Lorena Maria Genua,Sarvesh Prajapati,Ananya Trivedi,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPECTRA 是一个以物体为中心的操作执行质量量化框架，通过从仿真示教中学习相对位姿约束并计算 Task Quality Index Q∈[0,1]，实现比二值成功标签更细粒度的评估，并可用于硬件策略训练的示教筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值成功标签只能确认任务是否完成，无法区分不同几何精度下的成功执行；因此需要一种能量化刚体操作执行质量、并可作为机器人操作任务参考的评估方法。

Method: SPECTRA 从少量仿真 oracle demonstrations 中提取 progress-aligned relative poses，识别哪些相对位姿方向受到约束及其 admissible variations，并以此建立任务参考；对未见执行，将超出允许变异的偏差汇总为 Task Quality Index Q∈[0,1]。

Result: 在七个仿真任务中，每任务使用30条示教时约束恢复准确率达94%；在三种学习策略的 rollouts 上，SPECTRA 分数与人类判断的 pooled Spearman 相关性为全体 rollouts p=0.81、成功 rollouts p=0.61，均优于所有基线；在硬件上，用仿真学到的任务参考选择最高分示教训练 diffusion policy，探索性结果显示成功率高于随机选择示教。

Conclusion: SPECTRA 提供了对象中心、细粒度且可迁移的操作质量评估，不仅能区分不同几何精度的成功执行，还能通过示教筛选改善真实硬件策略训练。

Abstract: As learned manipulation policies become increasingly capable of solving practical manipulation tasks, assessing how well a task is performed becomes a fundamental challenge. While binary labels confirm task success, they cannot distinguish successful executions with different levels of geometric precision. To address this, we introduce SPECTRA, an object-centric framework for quantifying execution quality in manipulation of rigid objects. From a small set of oracle demonstrations generated in simulation, SPECTRA represents object interactions through progress-aligned relative poses. SPECTRA identifies which relative-pose directions are constrained and their admissible variations from demonstrations. These constraints establish a reference for evaluating robotic manipulation tasks. To score an unseen execution, deviations beyond the admissible variation are summarized by a Task Quality Index Q in [0,1]. Across seven simulated tasks, SPECTRA recovers constraints with 94% accuracy using 30 demonstrations per task. On rollouts from three learned policies, its scores achieve pooled Spearman correlations with human judgments of p=0.81 over all rollouts and p=0.61 among successful rollouts, exceeding all baselines in both subsets. Using the task reference learned in simulation, we use SPECTRA to select the highest-scoring demonstrations for training a diffusion policy on hardware. Exploratory results show higher success rate than training on randomly selected demonstrations.

</details>


### [30] [Differentiable Iterative Learning Control for Black-Box Position-Commanded Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01220)
*Mohammed Al-Ghaili,Yakoop Qasim,Hassan Almubarak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Diff-ILC：一种在线双层框架，在物理执行之间自适应约束ILC更新参数，用于低层控制器不可访问的位置指令机械臂高精度重复跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置指令机械臂的低层控制器不可访问，高精度重复跟踪困难；约束模型型ILC通常需要大量人工调参。

Method: Diff-ILC先由成对平滑扰动一次性辨识闭环微分模型并固定；每次试验用约束二次规划计算可行指令修正，以真实锚定的下一试验预测定义任务级目标；通过对局部有效KKT系统隐式微分获得超梯度，从而无需额外机器人执行即可多次更新计算参数。

Result: 在6自由度DOBOT CR3的未见平面和三维轨迹上，物理试验收敛显著快于固定参数模型型ILC；四条主要轨迹仅两次试验间更新后，平均关节向量RMSE和笛卡尔RMSE分别降低78.8%和76.4%。

Conclusion: Diff-ILC可在线自动调节约束ILC参数且不增加机器人执行次数，有效提升低层控制器不可访问的位置指令机械臂的重复跟踪收敛速度与精度。

Abstract: High-accuracy repetitive tracking is challenging for position-commanded manipulators whose low-level controllers are inaccessible, while constrained model-based iterative learning control (ILC) can require substantial manual parameter tuning. We present Differentiable Iterative Learning Control (Diff-ILC), an online bilevel framework that adapts the parameters of a constrained ILC update between physical robot executions. A closed-loop differential model is identified once from paired smooth perturbations and subsequently kept fixed. At each trial, a constrained quadratic program computes a feasible command correction, while a real-anchored next-trial prediction defines a task-level objective. Hypergradients are obtained by implicitly differentiating the locally active KKT system, enabling multiple computational parameter updates without additional robot executions. Experiments on a 6-DoF DOBOT CR3 across unseen planar and three-dimensional trajectories show substantially faster physical-trial convergence than fixed-parameter model-based ILC. Across the four primary trajectories, after only two trial-to-trial updates, Diff-ILC achieves 78.8% and 76.4% lower mean joint-vector and Cartesian RMSE, respectively, than fixed-parameter model-based ILC.

</details>


### [31] [Vision-Language Models Are Joint Denoisers in Robotic Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01215)
*Xicheng Gong,Wenxuan Song,Chengze Liu,Qiwei Li,Qize Yu,Jiaolong Yang,Haoang Li,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JADE 提出一种联合动作去噪框架，使预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）与扩散动作专家在每个去噪步共同细化动作：VLM 读取含噪动作潜变量、视觉观测和语言指令，并通过门控交叉注意力动态引导动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式 VLA 通常将 VLM 与动作专家级联，但仅让 VLM 提供静态条件，限制了预训练感知与推理能力对动作生成的引导，尤其在分布外场景中表现不足。

Method: JADE 利用结构化动作潜变量，让 VLM 在每一步去噪中结合当前含噪动作、视觉观测和语言指令进行上下文建模，并通过门控交叉注意力指导动作专家；同时监督 VLM 从含噪潜变量恢复干净动作潜变量，以提供直接的动作学习信号并耦合感知与控制。联合去噪支持更少采样步数，配合自适应步长调度平衡任务性能与推理延迟。

Result: 在 RoboDojo 上，JADE 平均成功率达到 12.67%，为所评估的基于 π0.5 方法中最高，并与近期大规模预训练的 VLA 和 WAM 基础模型具有竞争力；在 RoboTwin 2.0 上性能提升，并在四个真实世界双臂任务上相较 π0.5 平均成功率提升 20.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: JADE 通过让 VLM 参与联合动作去噪，将预训练感知推理与动作生成更紧密耦合，在提升控制性能和分布外适应性的同时，通过少步采样与自适应调度控制额外推理成本，是一种有效且具竞争力的 VLA 动作生成方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) models cascade pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) with diffusion action experts, but typically restrict the VLM to providing static conditioning. This separation limits how much pretrained perception and reasoning can guide action generation, especially in out-of-distribution settings. We introduce JADE, a Joint Action DEnoiser that enables the VLM and action expert to jointly refine actions. Our key insight is that structured action latents allow the VLM to read the current noisy action together with visual observations and language instructions. At every denoising step, the VLM contextualizes the noisy action and guides the action expert through gated cross-attention, so that its guidance evolves with the action being generated. We further supervise the VLM to recover clean action latents from noisy ones, which gives it a direct action-learning signal and couples perception with control. Additionally, joint denoising enables JADE to generate actions with fewer sampling steps, helping offset the additional cost of VLM participation. An adaptive step-size schedule further balances task performance and inference latency. On RoboDojo, JADE achieves an average success rate of 12.67%, the highest among the evaluated $\pi_{0.5}$-based methods, and is competitive with recent VLA and WAM foundation models pretrained at scale. It also improves performance on RoboTwin 2.0 and raises the average success rate across four real-world bimanual tasks by 20.8 percentage points compared with $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Agile Humanoid Dodgeball with Onboard Monocular Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01197)
*Feng Tian,Yulin An,Chenhao Li,Matthias Heyrman,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一个面向人形机器人的集成式机载单目视觉躲避球系统，将视觉威胁评估、时序球状态估计与学习式全身控制结合，实现动态来球下的躲避、恢复与目标重捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在共享且动态的环境中运行时，必须在保持平衡的同时快速响应高速接近的物体；论文以躲避球作为受控测试任务，研究视觉威胁评估与全身躲避、恢复及目标重捕获的耦合能力。

Method: 系统集成机载单目感知、球状态与威胁标志的时序估计以及学习式全身控制。球检测器提供边界框历史，时序估计器预测相对球位置和二值威胁标志；独立的躲避/恢复与搜索/跟踪策略共享该接口并组合以支持重复遭遇。训练采用三阶段课程：先用特权球状态训练控制，再从稳定策略 rollout 启动估计器监督，最后用估计器输出替换 actor 的特权输入；通过解析投影和感知随机化生成类检测器训练观测而无需图像渲染。

Result: 在受控仿真中按接近角分箱测试，每个速度—距离条件 1000 次投掷；5.0 m、6 m/s 发射时躲避成功率为 97.8%–100.0%，2.5 m、10 m/s 发射时为 33.2%–40.8%。在实体 Unitree G1 上，超过 15 次试验的受控单次投掷成功率超过 85%；硬件序列展示了全方位目标重捕获后再次躲避。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该集成式机载单目视觉系统能够在高动态运动条件下完成人形机器人躲避球任务，并支持躲避后的恢复、目标重捕获与重复遭遇。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in shared, dynamic environments must respond to rapidly approaching objects while maintaining balance. We study dodgeball as a controlled test of this capability, coupling visual threat assessment with whole-body evasion, recovery, and target reacquisition. We develop a system that integrates onboard monocular perception, temporal ball-state and threat-flag estimation, and learned whole-body control. A ball detector supplies bounding-box histories, and a temporal estimator predicts relative ball position and a binary threat flag. Separate dodge/recovery and search/track policies share this interface and are composed to support repeated encounters. A three-stage curriculum first trains control with privileged ball states, then starts estimator supervision from stable policy rollouts, and finally replaces the actor's privileged inputs with estimator outputs. Analytic projection and perception randomization provide detector-like training observations without image rendering. Across approach-angle bins in controlled simulation, dodge success ranges from 97.8--100.0% for 5.0 m launches at 6 m/s to 33.2--40.8% for 2.5 m launches at 10 m/s, with 1000 throws per speed--distance condition. On a physical Unitree G1, controlled single-throw success exceeds 85% over more than 15 trials. A hardware sequence demonstrates full-azimuth target reacquisition followed by another dodge. These results demonstrate an integrated onboard-monocular-vision system under highly dynamic motions.

</details>


### [33] [ClothDojo: Learned Data Generation and Benchmarking for Bimanual Garment Flattening and Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01190)
*Zilong Huang,Zipeng Ye,Caicheng Wang,Yuchen Xie,Jiaxiang Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ClothDojo 是一个用于服装展平与折叠的仿真平台，可大规模生成自适应操作轨迹并在闭环中评估策略；其利用 NOCS 纹理向预训练 VLA 模型提供规范表面位置线索，微调后自动生成并筛选成功轨迹，再用随机外观重渲染以训练 RGB 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装展平和折叠要求机器人在操作中识别服装结构，并随布料形变调整动作；现有研究需要跨多样服装和初始状态的大规模自适应轨迹，以及闭环策略评估手段。

Method: ClothDojo 将每件服装的规范网格坐标编码为 NOCS 纹理，通过预训练视觉-语言-动作模型的视觉接口提供规范表面位置线索；基于人类示范和纠正微调得到数据生成策略，自主生成仿真操作轨迹，并仅保留满足任务特定成功标准的 rollout；随后在随机采样的外观下重渲染保留轨迹，为不接收 NOCS 的下游策略提供 RGB 观测。

Result: 在两个任务上，该策略在数百件服装上生成近 8000 条成功轨迹；基于这些生成轨迹训练的 RGB 策略在所有评估的服装划分和外观设置下均优于直接基于原始人类示范与纠正训练的策略；多策略模型评估显示整体性能和外观鲁棒性因模型和任务而异，而对未见服装的泛化仍是共同挑战。

Conclusion: ClothDojo 提供了可扩展的服装操作轨迹生成与闭环评估平台，NOCS 引导的数据生成策略能有效产出下游 RGB 策略可用的大规模成功数据，但跨未见服装的泛化仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Garment flattening and folding require robots to identify garment structure during manipulation and adapt their actions as the cloth deforms. We present ClothDojo, a simulation platform that generates adaptive manipulation trajectories at scale across diverse garments and initial states and evaluates policies in closed loop. ClothDojo encodes each garment's canonical mesh coordinates as an NOCS texture, providing canonical surface-position cues through the visual interface of a pretrained vision-language-action model. We fine-tune the model on human demonstrations and corrections to obtain a data-generation policy. The policy autonomously generates simulated manipulation trajectories, and we retain rollouts that satisfy task-specific success criteria. Each retained trajectory is re-rendered under a randomly sampled appearance to provide RGB observations for downstream policies that do not receive NOCS. Across both tasks, the policy produces nearly 8,000 successful trajectories over hundreds of garments. RGB policies trained on these generated trajectories outperform those trained directly on the original human demonstrations and corrections across all evaluated garment splits and appearance settings. Evaluations across multiple policy models show that overall performance and appearance robustness vary across models and tasks, while generalization to unseen garments remains a common challenge.

</details>


### [34] [Auriga Technical Report: A Task-Agnostic Agent Harness for Zero-Shot Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01112)
*Chengcheng Wang,Yuheng Shi,Siyu Xu,Yutong Wang,Jinjing Zhao,Chenghao Xia,Jinqi Liao,Jiankun Peng,Ligong Bi,Yuechuan Ma,Jiaqi Liu,Wenbo Wang,Zijian Wang,Yuemin Wu,Minjing Dong,Xiu Su,Tao Huang,Jianyuan Guo,Chengbin Du,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Auriga 是与具体任务无关的智能体 harness（agent harness，指承载智能体决策与执行的运行框架），它借助显式任务信念、直接工具调用或多步 Python 程序、校准 3D 测量、以手指为参考的双臂控制、单一机器人准入网关以及执行期间的容差检查，在零样本操作中缩小了 68.3% 的规划-执行差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能够理解指令并规划操作任务，但直接作为机器人策略时表现不佳。在 RoboDojo 上，高层任务决策与物理执行之间存在明显的规划-执行差距：移除机械臂执行后，GPT-6 Astra 的成功率从参考控制接口下的 22.5% 升至 76.2%，说明模型自身具备较强任务决策能力，瓶颈在于执行环节。

Method: Auriga 让模型在显式任务信念上完成全部任务决策，并通过直接工具调用或合成的多步 Python 程序执行动作；harness 则提供校准的 3D 测量、以手指为参考的双臂控制，以及面向机器人的单一准入网关。运动前，模型在仿真暂停时检查可视化的轨迹预览；执行期间，harness 校验模型声明的阶段容差与抓取边界，一旦发生偏离就停止机器人，并返回测量到的偏差，以便在原地恢复。该 harness 经过 13 次跨任务能力迭代开发，随后冻结用于评估。

Result: 在 RoboDojo 排行榜上，Auriga 以 57.7% 的成功率（64.0 分）排名第一，在相同模型下成功率超过参考 harness 的两倍；在场景随机化条件下仍居第一，而学习型策略最多下降 33.9%。在 RoboCasa365 上，同一冻结 harness 达到 73.2% 的成功率，在复合任务上比 VLA 辅助智能体高出最多 23.1%。在零样本操作中，它缩小了 68.3% 的规划-执行差距，且无需目标任务的试验或重置。

Conclusion: 结果表明，把模型的高层任务决策与 harness 提供的精确测量、以手指为参考的控制和以执行容差为准的校验及恢复机制解耦，是弥合视觉语言模型规划-执行差距的有效途径；这种组合无需目标任务调优，就能在多个基准上稳定超越学习型策略与 VLA 辅助智能体。

Abstract: Vision-language models can interpret instructions and plan manipulation tasks, yet struggle when acting directly as robot policies. On RoboDojo, a large planning-execution gap separates high-level task decisions from physical execution: removing arm execution raises GPT-6 Astra's success rate from 22.5% under the reference control interface to 76.2%. We present Auriga, a task-agnostic agent harness that closes 68.3% of this gap in zero-shot manipulation without target-task trials or resets. In Auriga, the model makes all task decisions over an explicit task belief and acts through either direct tool calls or synthesized multi-step Python programs, while the harness supplies calibrated 3D measurements, finger-referenced dual-arm control, and a single admission gateway to the robot. Before motion, the model inspects a visual trajectory preview while simulation is paused; during execution, the harness checks declared stage tolerances and grasp bounds, stopping the robot on divergence and returning the measured discrepancy for in-place recovery. Developed across 13 cross-task capability iterations and frozen for evaluation, Auriga ranks first on the RoboDojo leaderboard with a 57.7% success rate (64.0 Score), more than doubling the reference harness on the same model, and remains first under scene randomization while learned policies drop by up to 33.9%. On RoboCasa365, the same frozen harness reaches a 73.2% success rate, outperforming VLA-assisted agents by up to 23.1% on composite tasks. Project page: this https URL Code: this https URL

</details>


### [35] [Robust Safety Assurance for Robotic Swarms Using Decentralized Collision Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01081)
*Grant Phillips,Hoang-Dung Tran,Justin M. Bradley*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在真实户外无人机集群上部署去中心化运行时安全验证与避碰算法，基于140次户外飞行（含6次8机集群飞行）评估性能并迭代改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息物理系统集群验证多停留在数值仿真或高度可控、无噪声室内环境，真实部署中算法假设与系统实现细节的匹配程度会显著影响方法有效性。

Method: 面向尺寸、重量和功耗受限的无人机集群，修改、增强并分析一种基于可达性的去中心化避碰方法；使用开源 safety_checker 库，在户外飞行中采集验证性能、资源利用和时序数据，并随实验推进改进避碰表现。

Result: 该方法能够实时高效验证多无人机安全性并帮助各智能体采取安全避碰动作；每个智能体验证未来4秒安全状态平均耗时约500–520 ms，其中可达集广播时间占主导，约300–500 ms。

Conclusion: 研究表明去中心化运行时验证可在真实无人机集群中有效运行，同时揭示了真实信息物理系统部署中的关键挑战，并为相关研究提供实践指导。

Abstract: In this paper we present the methodologies, results, and analysis used to deploy a decentralized safety verification algorithm on a robotic swarm to prevent collisions at runtime in an outdoor environment. Typically, approaches toward verification of swarms of cyber-physical systems (CPS) are evaluated solely in numerical simulations or in highly controlled, noiseless indoor environments. Our efforts to implement collision avoidance on a real swarm system outdoors have revealed that the underlying algorithmic assumptions juxtaposed against the implementation details of the target system are critical factors in a method's efficacy in the real world. Here we modify, enhance, and analyze a decentralized reachability-based method for collision avoidance on a size, weight, and power-constrained uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) swarm with the objective of illustrating the challenges of real-world CPS deployments as well as provide guidance for researchers in this space. Verification performance, resource utilization, and timing data were gathered over the course of 140 total outdoor vehicle flights, including 6 swarm flights composed of 8 UAVs, and used to improve collision-avoidance performance as experimentation progressed. Our extensive experiments show that the proposed decentralized runtime verification approach can efficiently verify the safety of multiple UAVs in real-time and help all agents take safe actions to avoid collisions. On average, each agent, using our open-source safety_checker library, takes approximately 500-520 ms to verify its safety for the next 4 s into the future, in which the reachable set broadcast time dominates the total verification time (~300-500 ms).

</details>


### [36] [Pixel: A Co-Designed Full-Stack Robot System for Learning General-Purpose Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03648)
*Tony Tao,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pixel 是一个面向通用移动操作的全栈协同设计系统，将低成本轮式人形硬件、数据采集接口、全身控制与策略学习统一集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动操作系统的硬件、数据、控制与学习常独立开发，导致组件间不匹配并限制整体能力，因此需要协同设计的一体化系统。

Method: Pixel 集成低成本轮式人形机器人（人类对齐运动学与柔顺夹爪）、多种遥操作与无机器人人类数据采集接口、统一全身控制器，以及跨人类和机器人示范的通用策略学习流程；并采用分层学习栈实现语言条件视觉运动控制和长时程任务执行。

Result: 作者在硬件、数据采集和策略学习方面评估系统，并展示了需要灵巧性、主动感知、导航和全身协调的移动操作行为。

Conclusion: Pixel 旨在为研究机器人硬件、数据、控制与学习如何协同设计提供一个集成且可扩展的基础平台。

Abstract: General-purpose mobile manipulation requires a combination of capable robot hardware, scalable demonstration collection, expressive whole-body control, and effective policy learning. Yet these components are often developed independently, creating mismatches that limit the capabilities of the complete system. We present Pixel, a co-designed full-stack system for learning general-purpose mobile manipulation. Pixel combines a low-cost wheeled humanoid with human-aligned kinematics and compliant grippers, multiple teleoperation and robot-free human data-collection interfaces, a unified whole-body controller, and a common policy learning pipeline across human and robot demonstrations. A hierarchical learning stack further enables language-conditioned visuomotor control and long-horizon task execution. We evaluate the system across hardware, data collection, and policy learning, and demonstrate mobile-manipulation behaviors requiring dexterity, active perception, navigation, and whole-body coordination. We hope that Pixel provides an integrated and extensible foundation for studying how robot hardware, data, control, and learning can be designed together for robot learning. More results at this https URL

</details>


### [37] [Pixel-UMI: Learning Environment-Aware Whole-Body Manipulation from Sensorized Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03647)
*Jason Jingzhou Liu,Tony Tao,Deepak Pathak,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Pixel-UMI，从带传感器的人类演示中学习环境感知的全身移动操作策略，通过环境感知重定向、重定向误差感知掩码和 DAgger 人类纠错提升跨具身迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可为移动操作提供可扩展数据，但人与机器人在具身形态、运动学与视角上的差异会阻碍策略学习与部署。

Method: 构建 Pixel-UMI 流程：首先提出 Environment-Aware Retargeting（EAR），结合重建场景几何与演示的头部、夹爪轨迹确定机器人姿态，并生成碰撞感知的关节与基座目标；通过延迟校准的动作分配将轨迹转换为动作。针对运动学差异导致的残余位姿误差和人机视角不匹配，提出 Retargeting-Error-Aware Masking（REAM），在训练中利用重定向误差降低部署时对不匹配视角的依赖。最后，由于估计动作难以充分表达接触丰富交互，采用 DAgger 收集在线人类纠正以改进策略。

Result: 在涵盖关节物体操作、可变形物体处理、受限空间到达、全身避障和接触丰富双臂协调的五项挑战任务上评估，Pixel-UMI 达到 87.0% 的平均任务进度。

Conclusion: Pixel-UMI 表明，结合环境感知重定向、误差感知训练掩码与在线人类纠正，可有效利用人类演示学习环境感知的全身移动操作策略，并缓解跨具身差异带来的视角与运动学不匹配问题。

Abstract: Sensorized human demonstrations offer scalable data for mobile manipulation, but embodiment differences complicate policy learning. We present Pixel-UMI, a pipeline for learning environment-aware whole-body policies from human data. We first introduce Environment-Aware Retargeting (EAR), which combines reconstructed scene geometry with demonstrated head and gripper trajectories to determine robot posture and generate collision-aware joint and base targets. Delay-calibrated action assignment converts these trajectories into actions. However, kinematic differences can leave residual pose errors, creating viewpoint mismatches between human demonstrations and robot observations.
