We therefore introduce Retargeting-Error-Aware Masking (REAM), which uses these errors during training to reduce reliance on mismatched views at deployment. Finally, because estimated actions cannot fully capture contact-rich interactions, we collect on-policy human corrections through DAgger to improve performance. We evaluate Pixel-UMI on five challenging tasks spanning articulated-object manipulation, deformable-object handling, constrained-space reaching, whole-body collision avoidance, and contact-rich bimanual coordination, achieving 87.0% average task progress. Video results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [38] [D&A: Decoupling Description and Assessment Enables VLMs as Zero-Shot Robotic Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03622)
*Marius Memmel,Shawn Yin,Urvi Rutia,Angad Wadhwa,Eldon Li,Gavin Angeloff,Ankit Goyal,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox,Fabio Ramos,Anqi Li,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出纯提示式的 D&A（Describe & Assess）框架，将 VLM 的视频任务进度估计解耦为客观场景动态描述与基于显式评分规则的评估，从而校准零样本进度估计并缓解确认偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 视频任务进度估计常因隐含成功假设或相对时间排序设计而产生确认偏差，在失败执行中幻觉出进度；直接提示前沿模型会退化为二值成功检测器，难以刻画非线性和细粒度失败；后训练方法虽可缓解，但依赖昂贵真实机器人数据且需对每个基座模型进行高计算量后训练。

Method: D&A 先让 VLM 生成任务 grounding 的客观文本化场景动态描述，再在显式 rubrics 下进行评估；通过强制模型在打分前合成绝对成功的显式文本定义，将评价锚定到外部全局标准而非视频内部时间线。

Result: 实验表明该方法可校准零样本估计、缓解确认偏差，并提供细粒度部分进度指标，既能准确惩罚失败执行，又能正确插值成功过程；由于纯提示式设计，D&A 可跨任务、具身和基准泛化，匹配或超过后训练奖励模型，其奖励估计还能支持高效下游强化学习。

Conclusion: D&A 以无需额外训练和真实机器人数据的提示式方案，实现了更可靠、细粒度且可泛化的 VLM 视频任务进度估计，并可作为下游强化学习的有效奖励信号。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) hold promise for zero-shot video task-progress estimation in robot learning, yet existing frameworks frequently suffer from confirmation bias, hallucinating progress in failed executions due to implicit success assumptions or relative temporal ranking designs. Naive prompting of frontier models collapses into a binary success detector that fails to capture nuanced, non-linear progress and failures. While post-training approaches can mitigate this through explicit training, they demand expensive real-world robotics data and require computationally intensive post-training for every individual base model. To resolve these limitations, we introduce D&A (Describe & Assess): our approach decouples progress estimation into an objective, task-grounded textual description of scene dynamics followed by an assessment phase using explicit rubrics. By compelling the VLM to synthesize an explicit, text-based definition of absolute success before scoring, the framework anchors evaluations to an external, global criterion rather than the video's internal timeline. Empirical results demonstrate that our method calibrates zero-shot estimates and mitigates confirmation bias, providing fine-grained, partial progress metrics that accurately penalize failed executions while correctly interpolating successful runs. Being purely prompt-based, D&A generalizes across tasks, embodiments, and benchmarks---matching or surpassing post-trained reward models---and its reward estimates enable efficient downstream reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [39] [Seeing Through the Illusion: Test-Time Scaling for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03585)
*Cheikh Ahmed,Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Milad Yazdani,Zirui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WISE，一种在执行前对世界动作模型（WAM）采样出的多个 rollout 进行评分与选择的方法，在保持 WAM 冻结的情况下提升闭环任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 会联合生成动作与想象未来，但预测出的未来看似合理，并不代表其共同生成的动作能够真正实现该未来；因此需要在执行前筛选候选动作。

Method: WISE 对每个采样 rollout 从三方面评分：动作有效性与时间规律性、所提动作与想象运动之间的逆动力学一致性、以及指令条件下的任务进展；随后执行最高分候选，且不更新 WAM。实验中每个决策采样四个候选。

Result: 在 RoboLab 上使用 Cosmos 3 Edge 时，闭环成功率从 6.0% 提升到 24.0%；在 LIBERO 上使用 DreamZero 时，平均成功率从 75.1% 提升到 78.3%；总体分别比现有最佳选择器高 4.0 和 0.9 个百分点。消融与一致性分析表明，三项评分对候选的排序不同，其中任务进展在融合决策中权重最大。

Conclusion: 执行前的多候选评分选择能有效提升 WAM 的闭环表现；任务进展是关键选择信号，且不同评分维度具有互补性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) jointly generate actions and imagined futures, but a plausible prediction need not be realized by its co-generated action. We introduce WISE (\emph{World Action Inference Scaling through Evaluation}), a pre-execution selector that scores each sampled rollout on action validity and temporal regularity, inverse-dynamics consistency between the proposed action and the imagined motion, and instruction-conditioned task progress, and executes the highest-scoring candidate while keeping the WAM frozen. With four candidates per decision, WISE raises closed-loop success from 6.0\% to 24.0\% on RoboLab with Cosmos~3 Edge and from 75.1\% to 78.3\% mean success on LIBERO with DreamZero, and exceeds the best existing selector in aggregate by 4.0 and 0.9 points. Component ablations and agreement analyses show that the three scores rank candidates differently and that task progress carries the largest weight in the fused decision.

</details>


### [40] [TAMPAgent: Agentic Belief-Space Planning for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03584)
*Utkarsh A. Mishra,Alperen Degirmenci,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu,Tucker Hermans,Caelan Garrett*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TAMPAgent，将LVLM的开放世界推理能力与TAMP的结构化约束满足相结合，用于部分可观测环境下的开放世界机器人移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM智能体依赖直接工具调用，难以处理几何不确定性，通常局限于完全可观测的桌面环境；TAMP虽能处理部分可观测长时程任务，但计算成本高且依赖封闭世界假设。

Method: 为LVLM智能体引入基于可扩展感知、规划与控制工具API的信念空间规划模型。智能体先生成由参数化观测与执行动作组成的部分任务计划，再用经典搜索算法补全；随后提取几何约束，通过工具API绑定可行参数值，生成可执行运动计划，并在执行中持续更新信念历史，交错观测与操作形成闭环。

Result: 在Rainbow Y1和Unitree G1平台上的复杂家庭移动操作任务中，TAMPAgent显著优于直接工具调用的智能体基线。

Conclusion: 将TAMP约束满足集成到智能体规划闭环中，对非结构化、部分可观测环境下的机器人移动操作至关重要。

Abstract: Open-world robotic mobile manipulation requires acting in physical environments with only partial information. While recent Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM) agents show strong open-world reasoning capabilities, existing architectures that rely on direct tool calling struggle with geometric uncertainty and thus are limited to fully-observable tabletop environments. In contrast, Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) systems can solve long-horizon tasks under partial observability but incur high computational costs and require closed worlds. We propose \textbf{TAMPAgent}, an agentic planning framework that unifies the reasoning of LVLMs with the structured constraint satisfaction of TAMP systems. We endow an LVLM agent with a belief-space planning model operating over an extensible API of perception, planning, and control tools. The agent formulates a partial task plan composed of parameterized observation and execution actions, which is subsequently completed using classical search algorithms. After extracting the plan's geometric constraints, the agent leverages its tool API to bind valid parameter values, satisfying feasibility requirements and producing an executable motion plan. Upon execution, the agent continuously updates its belief history and repeats this closed-loop process by interleaving observation and manipulation actions. Evaluated across complex household mobile manipulation tasks on Rainbow Y1 and Unitree G1 platforms, TAMPAgent significantly outperforms direct tool-calling agentic baselines, demonstrating that integrating TAMP constraint satisfaction into agentic planning loops is essential for unstructured, partially observable environments.

</details>


### [41] [Mediating Human-Swarm Interactions Through a Robot Helper: A Shepherding-Inspired Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03548)
*Arihant Yadav,Sophie Jörg,Eakta Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在VR牧羊任务中比较低层遥操作与高自主运动基元界面，发现通过中介机器人引导不可寻址群体时，作用于集体形状的运动基元表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人-群体控制和人-群体交互方法常假设群体成员可寻址，但野生动物群、鱼群等“不可治理”群体不满足该假设，受牧羊启发需要研究有效交互方式。

Method: 在虚拟现实牧羊任务中，参与者通过中介机器人助手引导大规模群体，比较低层遥操作与较高自主性的运动基元界面，并改变群体凝聚力动态。

Result: 作用于集体形状（如基于边界的控制）的运动基元界面提高了任务表现，并降低了感知工作负荷。

Conclusion: 对于不可寻址的大规模群体，采用作用于集体形状的高自主运动基元界面比低层遥操作更有效，为人-群体交互设计提供了依据。

Abstract: This work investigates human-swarm interaction (HSI) for steering large collectives through an intermediary robot helper. While prior robot-swarm control and HSI techniques commonly assume swarm members are addressable, these assumptions often fail for ``ungovernable'' swarms such as wildlife flocks and schools of fish. Motivated by pastoral shepherding, we compare low-level teleoperation to a higher-autonomy motion-primitives interface in a virtual reality (VR) shepherding task under varied swarm cohesion dynamics. Results indicate that motion primitives that operate on collective shape (e.g., boundary-based control) improve task performance and reduce perceived workload.

</details>


### [42] [PanoTac: A Dense Three-Axis Tactile Sensor for Robust Touch-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03520)
*Binglin,Wang,Xiaole,Jiang,Zicheng Teng,Xinqi Liu,Yunzhu Li,Lerrel Pinto,Zichen Jeff Cui,Binghao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出PanoTac，一种低成本、柔顺的触觉指尖，可进行密集三轴触觉感知（12×32×3，含法向与双剪切分量），并以60 Hz运行、单指尖成本低于50美元；将其接入视觉-触觉策略后，在试管准备、舀取谷物和倒盐任务中显著优于纯视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要同时获取接触几何与交互力信息：舀取谷物时接触模式有助于推断勺子的朝向，抓取中传递的阻力则指导运动。现有方案难以以低成本同时提供这些互补感知能力，因此需要一种可密集感知法向和剪切信息的柔性触觉指尖。

Method: 设计PanoTac低成本柔顺触觉指尖，每个指尖输出12×32×3触觉表示，每个taxel包含一个法向z和两个剪切x、y响应分量，运行频率60 Hz，成本低于50美元/指尖。作者对传感器进行表征，量化响应重复性以及跨传感器实例和接触位置的一致性，并将其集成到机器人视觉-触觉流水线中。

Result: 在试管准备、谷物舀取和盐倾倒任务中，基于PanoTac的策略平均比纯视觉基线高46.7个百分点。消融实验表明，移除空间接触信息或剪切反馈中的任一项，都会降低任务套件上的整体性能。

Conclusion: PanoTac能够以低成本提供密集三轴触觉反馈，补充接触几何与力信息，并有效提升接触丰富机器人操作任务的策略表现；空间接触信息和剪切反馈对性能均至关重要。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation benefits from tactile feedback that captures both detailed contact geometry and interaction forces. When scooping grains, contact patterns help infer spoon orientation, while resistance transmitted through the grasp guides motion. To provide these complementary sensing capabilities, we present PanoTac, a low-cost, compliant tactile fingertip designed for dense three-axis tactile sensing. Each fingertip provides a $12 \times 32 \times 3$ tactile representation, with one normal ($z$) and two shear ($x$, $y$) response components at each taxel. The sensor operates at 60 Hz and costs less than $50 per fingertip. Sensor characterization quantifies response repeatability and consistency across sensor instances and contact locations. We integrate PanoTac into a robotic system with a visuo-tactile pipeline. Across test-tube preparation, grain scooping, and salt pouring, the resulting policies outperform vision-only baselines by an average of 46.7 percentage points. Ablations show that removing either spatial contact information or shear feedback reduces overall performance across the task suite. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [43] [When Robots Outweigh Objects: How Inertia Mismatch Shapes Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03501)
*Younghyo Park,Antonia Bronars,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过仿真实验研究机器人臂与操作物体之间的惯性不匹配是否会对现代机器人学习（尤其是行为克隆）造成隐藏成本，并探索在不更换硬件的情况下降低接触惯性的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人臂相比日常物体往往非常重，接触时存在显著惯性不匹配；尽管强化学习和行为克隆已展示出广泛的操作能力，但尚不清楚高惯性是否会加剧数据缩放成本或降低学习性能上限。

Method: 在受控仿真中系统改变机器人质量和物体质量，比较强化学习与行为克隆在不同质量组合下的表现；进一步在机器人臂重量不变但电机通过齿轮传动反映更多惯性的条件下测试行为克隆；并评估两种降低现有机械臂接触惯性的方式：数据采集前选择低惯性工作空间，或引导示教者采用任务的低惯性解。

Result: 强化学习在广泛质量组合下均能成功；行为克隆在较轻机器人操作较重物体时学习到更可靠策略，增加数据可缩小但与高惯性情形间的差距仍未完全消除；当电机通过齿轮传动反映更多惯性时，即使臂重不变，行为克隆性能也会下降；两种低惯性示教策略下，低惯性示教被更可靠地模仿。

Conclusion: 高惯性会对行为克隆施加隐藏成本并影响其数据缩放与性能上限；在不更换硬件的前提下，通过选择低惯性工作空间或引导低惯性示教解，可提升示教数据被可靠模仿的程度。

Abstract: Conventional robot arms can be extremely heavy compared with the everyday objects they manipulate, creating a significant inertia mismatch at contact. Even a small accidental bump from a heavy arm can knock a light object out of place. Modern learning methods have nevertheless demonstrated a wide range of successful manipulation behaviors on these platforms, suggesting that even extreme inertia mismatch can be overcome with sufficient data. However, it remains unclear whether high inertia imposes a hidden cost on learning, potentially worsening scaling with data or lowering the performance ceiling of modern robot learning methods. We investigate this question through controlled simulation experiments that vary robot and object masses. We find that reinforcement learning succeeds across a broad range of mass combinations, while behavior cloning learns more reliable policies when a lighter robot manipulates a heavier object, and more data narrows the resulting gap without closing it. Behavior cloning likewise degrades on a simulated robot arm as its motors reflect more inertia through their gearing, even though its weight stays the same. Finally, because new hardware is rarely an option, we explore ways to lower contact inertia on the arm one already has, either by choosing a lower-inertia workspace before collecting data or by steering the demonstrator toward lower-inertia solutions of the task. In both simulated cases, the lower-inertia demonstrations are imitated more reliably. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [44] [SNAP: Semantic Navigation Among Pedestrians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03498)
*Jakob Wolfram,Pascal Roth,Fan Yang,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的机器人导航策略，从单张语义标注鸟瞰图（BEV）中联合推理语义地形与动态障碍物，实现拥挤城市环境中兼顾行人避让与社会规范的导航，并可零样本迁移到足式机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将人群避让与语义路径规划分开处理，难以同时满足行人避让和靠人行道通行等社会规范；同时依赖显式行人状态估计会增加复杂度。

Method: 将语义地形信息和动态障碍物统一表示在单张语义标注BEV地图中，使用强化学习训练导航策略进行联合推理，不依赖显式人体状态估计。

Result: 该策略优于现有的人群导航和语义感知基线方法，并能零样本迁移到足式机器人，在高度动态的真实场景中实现符合社会规范的导航。

Conclusion: 研究表明，从统一语义BEV表示中联合学习地形与动态障碍推理，可有效实现拥挤城市环境下的社会合规机器人导航，并具备良好的真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: Navigating crowded urban environments requires robots to avoid pedestrians while adhering to social norms such as remaining on sidewalks. Existing approaches typically address either crowd avoidance or semantic path planning in isolation. We propose a reinforcement learning (RL) navigation policy that jointly reasons about semantic terrain and dynamic obstacles from a single semantically annotated bird's-eye-view (BEV) map, without relying on explicit human-state estimation. Our policy outperforms established crowd-navigation and semantic-aware baselines and transfers zero-shot to a legged robot, demonstrating socially compliant navigation in highly dynamic real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [45] [Delay-Certified Readiness Barriers for Overactuated Multirotor Allocation Without Lipschitz Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03490)
*Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种采样数据下的就绪度（readiness）保持滤波器：利用每个转子对自身力矩单调且闭式的响应结构，构造不确定切换时间下可达转速的廉价盒约束，并在预先已知的角点处检验最低就绪度，从而无需Lipschitz常数即可保证到下一采样时刻不低于下界，并提供后备力矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过驱动多旋翼的同一力旋量可由多组转速实现，但某些组合会使车辆无法快速改变力旋量（如抗阵风）。现有readiness barrier仅在连续时间下保持就绪度高于下界；在采样命令和速度控制器延迟变化时，可用鲁棒界不足1 ms，难以提供实际保证。

Method: 利用转子对自身力矩的单调闭式响应，将不确定切换时间下的可达转速集合表示为可廉价计算的盒；最低就绪度在该盒的某个预先已知角点取得。由此得到不依赖Lipschitz常数的检验：判断当前命令能否在下一采样前保持就绪度高于下界；并给出后备力矩，使车辆保持在已认证的计划上。

Result: 在六旋翼仿真中，面对2--40 ms延迟和±2 ms抖动，该滤波器从未违反就绪度下界。在10±2 ms时，其在设计包络内与延迟补偿预测器相当；超出包络时跟踪误差增加2--30%，而单独使用预测器在16次任务中有2至16次违反下界。

Conclusion: 该方法将连续时间的readiness保证扩展到采样命令与变化延迟/抖动场景，以一定跟踪性能代价换取可认证的快速力旋量调节能力，并避免对Lipschitz常数的依赖。

Abstract: Overactuated multirotors can produce the same wrench with many rotor-speed combinations, some of which leave the vehicle unable to change its wrench quickly, e.g., to reject a gust. Readiness barriers keep this ability, the readiness, above a floor, but only in continuous time; with sampled commands and varying speed-controller delays, the available robustness bound covers less than one millisecond. We remove this limit by exploiting the structure of the problem: each rotor responds monotonically and in closed form to its own torque, so the speeds reachable under uncertain switching times lie in a cheaply computed box, and the lowest readiness over that box sits at a corner known in advance. This yields a Lipschitz-free test that a command keeps the readiness above its floor until the next sample, plus a fallback torque that keeps the vehicle on a certified plan. In hexarotor simulations with 2--40~ms delays and $\pm2$~ms jitter, the filter never violated the floor. At $10\pm2$~ms, it matched a delay-compensating predictor within the design envelope and cost 2--30\% more tracking error beyond it, where the predictor alone violated the floor in 2 to 16 of 16 missions.

</details>


### [46] [Exploring Proximity and Tactile Sensing for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03489)
*Eric T. Chang,Bianca Aumann,Zhanpeng He,Mohamed Elnoor,Thomas Liang,Matei Ciocarlie,Nima Fazeli,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ToFTouch多模态触觉传感器，将三个4×4分辨率多区域飞行时间（ToF）传感器与基于光学的压力传感阵列结合，并安装于平行夹爪上，用于接触前感知与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预触觉感知可在机器人与物体接触前获取信息，使机器人能够提前预测并响应接触，从而改善抓取与操作性能。

Method: 设计并集成ToFTouch传感器：三个多区域ToF传感器提供接近/距离信息，光学压力传感阵列提供接触压力信息；将其安装在平行夹爪上，在预抓取调整、杂乱环境中物品提取、狭窄空间插入三类任务中验证。

Result: 预抓取调整中，ToF反馈使夹爪在接近过程中提前闭合，循环时间降低7.4%；物品提取中，在偏离标称抓取点的±2 cm深度偏移条件下均成功，包括基线方法失败的条件下，标准差低于0.4 cm；密集杂乱插入中，分析了插入轨迹过程中ToFTouch提供的接近与触觉信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多区域ToF传感在机器人操作环境中具有实用价值，可支持接触前感知、预抓取调整、杂乱提取和紧密空间插入等任务。

Abstract: Pre-touch sensing, or sensing information about an object before contact, is an attractive modality for robot hands as it can allow a robot to anticipate and respond to a contact before it happens. In this work, we introduce ToFTouch, a multimodal tactile sensor combining three multizone (4$\times$4 resolution) time-of-flight sensors with an optics-based pressure sensing array. We mount this sensor on a parallel jaw gripper and demonstrate its utility on three distinct tasks: (i) pre-grasp adjustment, (ii) item extraction from clutter, and (iii) item insertion into tight spaces. For pre-grasp adjustment, ToF feedback reduced cycle time by 7.4$\%$ by enabling anticipatory gripper closure during approach. For extraction, ToF sensing enabled successful retrieval across all tested depth offsets ($\pm$2 cm) deviating from the nominal pick point, including conditions where baseline approaches failed, with standard deviations below 0.4 cm. For insertion into dense clutter, we explore the proximity and tactile information provided from ToFTouch during an insertion trajectory. The results demonstrate the utility of multizone ToF sensing in a manipulation environment.

</details>


### [47] [Clinician-AI Collaboration in Endovascular Neurointerventions: In-Vitro User Study of Copilot Assistance Modes for Robotic Navigation Across the Autonomy Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03446)
*Nikola Fischer,Harry Robertshaw,S.M.Hadi Sadati,Xingyu Chen,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,Michael Adofo Kwakye,Sara Sciacca,Naga Kandasamy,Parthiban Balasundaram,Sahan Guruge,Charles Cummins,Joe Leyon,Nasr Abdelsalam,Matthew Benger,Andrew Falzon,Alejandro Granados,Christos Bergeles,Thomas Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出Copilot Assistance Modes（CAMs）框架，系统比较从手动到全自主的机器人血管内导航模式，发现 clinician-AI 共享自主在真实体外环境中显著优于全自主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械取栓是急性缺血性卒中的有效治疗，但受神经介入专家可及性限制；远程机器人和AI辅助系统有潜力，但 clinicians 如何与不同自主级别交互、自主导航在真实环境中的表现仍不清楚。

Method: 提出覆盖手动操作到全自主的 clinician-AI 协作框架CAMs；开展首个比较 clinician-AI 协作策略的体外用户研究，11名专家和2名学生在一患者特异性血管体模中评估6种导航模式，结合客观性能指标与主观负荷和可用性评估；并对自主性能进行 in silico 和 in vitro 评估以研究 sim-to-real 迁移。

Result: 手动导航总体表现最佳（成功率100%）。全自主导航在仿真中成功率为81%，但体外降至23%。多种 clinician-AI 共享自主模式成功率达92-97%，约为全自主的4倍，并与直接机器人控制（95%）相当，表明 clinician-AI 协作可缓解 sim-to-real 域偏移。AI驱动方法中，用户导向任务委托和旋转共享控制在高性能与临床接受度之间平衡最佳。

Conclusion: 对于可靠的机器人神经介入导航，当前更重要的可能不是单纯提高自主级别，而是实现有效的 clinician-AI 协作与控制权分配。

Abstract: Mechanical thrombectomy (MT) is an effective treatment for acute ischemic stroke, yet access remains limited by the availability of neurointerventional expertise. Telerobotic and AI-assisted systems may help address this challenge, but it remains unclear how clinicians interact with different levels of autonomy and how autonomous navigation systems perform in realistic environments. This work introduces Copilot Assistance Modes (CAMs), a framework for clinician-AI collaboration in robotic endovascular navigation spanning the autonomy spectrum from manual operation to full autonomy. In the first in-vitro user study comparing clinician-AI collaboration strategies, 11 experts and two students evaluated six navigation modes in a patient-specific vascular phantom. Objective performance metrics were combined with subjective assessments, including workload and usability. Autonomous performance was additionally evaluated both in silico and in vitro to investigate sim-to-real transfer. Hands-on manual navigation achieved the strongest overall performance (100% success). While fully autonomous navigation achieved 81% success in simulation, performance dropped to 23% in vitro. In contrast, several clinician-AI shared-autonomy modes achieved success rates of 92-97%, approximately four times higher than full autonomy and comparable to direct robotic control (95%), demonstrating mitigation of sim-to-real domain shift through clinician-AI collaboration. Among the evaluated AI-driven approaches, user-directed task delegation and rotational shared control demonstrated the most favorable balance between performance and clinician acceptance. These findings suggest that effective clinician-AI collaboration and allocation of control authority may currently be more important for reliable robotic neurointerventional navigation than increasing autonomy alone.

</details>


### [48] [Toward Uncertainty-Aware, Physics-Grounded Human World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03428)
*Yufei Zhang,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文论证将不确定性感知的3D人体重建组件KNOWN与基于学习动力学的物理预测组件PhysMoP耦合，可分别构成人类世界模型的状态信念与动力学预测两半，并给出接口、三种不确定性注入机制和在野外视频上的评测协议；本文不报告新实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人周围行动时，需要关于人的世界模型：既要估计人体身体状态及其不确定性，又要预测该状态如何演化。作者认为已有两个组件恰好对应这两部分，因此需要明确如何将它们耦合起来。

Method: KNOWN实时重建3D人体并估计偶然不确定性与认知不确定性；其偶然项识别不可靠观测，认知项识别训练数据低密度区域输入，并用认知不确定性驱动细化。PhysMoP通过求解带学习动力学项的欧拉-拉格朗日方程预测未来身体构型。本文定义二者接口，提出KNOWN不确定性进入PhysMoP的三种机制，并指定基于野外视频的评测协议。

Result: 组件已有结果：KNOWN达11.6 ms/帧，认知不确定性驱动细化将低密度输入误差从81.3 mm降至70.3 mm；PhysMoP将80 ms预测误差在Human3.6M上从9.6 mm降至2.1 mm，在3DPW上从12.1 mm降至0.7 mm。本文本身未报告新的实验结果。

Conclusion: 论文提出的是一个耦合框架与研究议程：将不确定性感知的人体状态估计与物理启发的动力学预测连接起来，以构建机器人所需的人类世界模型；其有效性仍需按所提出的野外视频协议进行实证验证。

Abstract: A robot that acts around people needs a world model of the human: a belief over the person's body state and a dynamics model that predicts where that state goes next. This abstract argues that two published components form exactly these two halves, and specifies how to couple them. KNOWN [1] reconstructs the 3D human body while estimating both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty in real time (11.6 ms per frame); its aleatoric term identifies unreliable observations, its epistemic term identifies inputs from low-density regions of the training data, and refinement driven by the epistemic term reduces the error on such inputs from 81.3 to 70.3 mm. PhysMoP [2] predicts future body configuration by solving the Euler-Lagrange equations of the body with learned dynamics terms, reducing the 80 ms prediction error from 9.6 to 2.1 mm on Human3.6M and from 12.1 to 0.7 mm on 3DPW. We define the interface between the two, propose three mechanisms by which KNOWN's uncertainty enters PhysMoP, and specify an evaluation protocol on in-the-wild video. No new experimental results are reported here.

</details>


### [49] [Myoelectric Control for Upper-Limb Exoskeletons: A Decoder-Shaper-Controller Framework, Design Guidelines, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03418)
*Davide Costanzi,Eldison Dimo,Emanuele Feola,Mario Sedilesu,Francesco Pascucci,Andrea Calanca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述提出用于上肢外骨骼连续肌电控制的“解码器-整形器-控制器”功能框架，并据此系统审视代表性方法、开放问题与设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌电控制系统可实现 wearable robot 的自主控制，但意图解码、辅助整形与底层执行器控制常未被明确区分，导致各模块贡献难以评估和比较。

Method: 将连续肌电控制分解为三个模块：解码器从 sEMG 估计意图相关变量；整形器依据辅助策略将其映射为控制参考；控制器通过执行器控制实现该参考。综述按模块设计、校准与评估对代表性方法进行批判性分析。

Result: 分析表明整形器决定辅助的幅度与形态，却缺乏成熟的设计与调参方法，且常被嵌入解码器或控制器中。主要开放问题包括辅助水平报告不一致、交互感知的稳定性与无源性工具使用不足，以及跨自由度、用户和任务验证不充分。

Conclusion: 综述给出面向模块化系统调参的设计指南，并提出未来研究重点，以弥合准确意图解码与真实工况下安全、有效、可泛化辅助之间的差距。

Abstract: Myoelectric Control Systems (MCSs) enable voluntary control of wearable robots through muscle activity. However, intention decoding, assistance shaping, and low-level actuator control are often not explicitly distinguished, making their contributions difficult to evaluate and compare. This review introduces a functional decoder-shaper-controller framework for continuous myoelectric control of upper-limb exoskeletons. The decoder estimates an intention-related variable from surface electromyography (sEMG), the shaper maps it to a control reference according to an assistance policy, and the controller realizes that reference through actuator control. This decomposition separates intention estimation from assistance-policy design and enables comparison of heterogeneous architectures. Representative approaches are critically examined in terms of module design, calibration, and evaluation. The analysis highlights that the shaper, despite determining the magnitude and shape of assistance, lacks established design and tuning methodologies and is often embedded within the decoder or controller. Key open issues include inconsistent reporting of assistance levels, limited use of interaction-aware stability and passivity tools, and insufficient validation across degrees of freedom, users, and tasks. Building on these findings, this review provides design guidelines for systematic module-wise tuning and identifies future research priorities to bridge the gap between accurate intention decoding and safe, effective, and generalizable assistance under real-world operating conditions.

</details>


### [50] [OpenZilo: An Open Smart-Ring Platform for Human-Centric Sensing and Interaction with Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03410)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Chi Qin,Weijian Lin,Lizhi Ding,Jiankai Tang,Yuntao Wang,Xiangke Liu,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并评估一种面向可穿戴机器人交互的开放式智能戒指平台，将语音、手势与生理事件输入连接到机器人执行，并从识别性能和物理任务结果两方面进行分阶段评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人接口需要把用户输入及时转化为物理动作；现有研究常只关注识别准确率，缺乏跨任务阶段对识别、响应时延和实际任务成功的综合评估。

Method: 平台集成麦克风、IMU、PPG和皮肤温度传感，软件架构分离数据采集、事件解释、交互策略与机器人执行，并定义七种运行模式。研究招募10名参与者，评估语音和手势控制：每种模态5个命令、250次试验；比较高斯隐马尔可夫模型与SVM在三种词表规模下的识别表现，并测量响应时间和整任务成功；还用模拟事件通知考察生理事件触发机器人交互的时延。

Result: 语音和手势任务成功率分别为90.4%和88.8%，两种模态的识别准确率均高于任务成功率。高斯HMM在三种词表规模下准确率高于SVM，但评分时间更长。共享语音任务启动后，手势调整的中位响应时间短于语音。整任务成功率手势为70.0%、语音为64.0%。生理事件触发下，通知接收到机器人到达的平均时间为11.71 s，现场人员为9.20 s。

Conclusion: 描述性结果表明，可穿戴机器人接口应同时评估识别性能与物理任务结果，并针对不同任务阶段采用相应的时延指标；该开放智能戒指平台为跨阶段研究提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Wearable robot interfaces must connect user input to timely physical action. We present the proposed platform, an open smart-ring platform for studying this connection across task stages. The ring integrates a microphone, an inertial measurement unit, photoplethysmography, and skin-temperature sensing. Its software architecture separates acquisition, event interpretation, interaction policy, and robot execution. We characterize seven operating modes and evaluate speech and gesture control with 10 participants. Across five commands and 250 trials per modality, speech and gestures achieved task success rates of 90.4% and 88.8%, respectively. Recognition accuracy exceeded task success for both modalities. Gaussian hidden Markov models achieved higher accuracy than a support vector machine across three vocabulary sizes, with longer scoring times. Following a shared spoken task start, gesture-based adjustments had shorter median response times than speech. Whole-task success was 70.0% with gestures and 64.0% with speech. We further examine physiological-event-triggered robot interaction using simulated event notifications. Mean notification-receipt-to-arrival times were 11.71 s for the robot and 9.20 s for an on-site person. These descriptive findings support evaluating wearable robot interfaces through both recognition and physical outcomes, with timing measures appropriate to each task stage.

</details>


### [51] [Safe-Ballbot: Tilt-Consistent MPC with Control Barrier Functions for Ballbot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03307)
*Duc Cuong Vu,Tung Lam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种三全向轮球平衡机器人双层导航方案：上层基于jerk模型的MPC与离散时间控制障碍函数处理静态、移动、旋转及凹形障碍；下层采用带扩展状态观测器的分层滑模控制与鲁棒指数控制障碍函数约束倾斜；两层通过由最大稳态倾斜闭式推导的加速度上界连接，并用共形预测半径膨胀运动预测障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球平衡机器人需倾斜以加速，倾斜受轮矩和安全限制；全模型规划器计算昂贵，忽略平衡动力学的规划参考不可跟踪。需兼顾实时性、可跟踪性与动态障碍安全。

Method: 上层为球位置jerk模型的模型预测控制，结合离散时间控制障碍函数处理多类障碍；下层为HSMC-ESO-ECBF跟踪并约束倾斜。通过最大稳态倾斜对应的闭式加速度界连接规划与跟踪，避免规划请求跟踪器拒绝的倾斜。对仅预测运动的障碍，用共形预测半径按给定概率界定预测误差并膨胀障碍。

Result: 在MuJoCo含全向轮滚子接触与力矩饱和的仿真中，保持至少10 cm间隙，规划跟踪RMS约2 cm，倾斜低于限制，规划耗时9–12 ms，比进入膨胀障碍的全模型非线性MPC快10–15倍；对预测障碍，共形膨胀在30个随机交叉场景中消除恒速预测器的碰撞，并在封闭环形长跑中减少碰撞。

Conclusion: 该双层方案通过加速度界实现规划与跟踪一致性，在实时性显著优于全模型MPC的同时保证安全间隙与倾斜约束；共形预测膨胀可提升预测障碍场景的安全性。

Abstract: Ball-balancing robots (ballbots) must lean to accelerate, and the lean is limited by the wheel torques and by safety. Planners that use the full robot model are expensive, while planners that ignore the balance dynamics produce references that cannot be followed. This paper presents a two-layer navigation scheme for a three-omni-wheel ballbot. The upper layer is a model predictive controller on a \emph{jerk model} of the ball position, with discrete-time control barrier functions for static, moving, rotating and concave obstacles. The lower layer is a hierarchical sliding mode controller with an extended state observer and a robust exponential control barrier function on the tilt (HSMC-ESO-ECBF). The layers are linked by an acceleration bound derived in closed form from the largest steady tilt that the barrier function admits, so that the plan never asks for a lean the tracker refuses. For obstacles whose motion is only predicted, the obstacles are inflated by conformal prediction radii that bound the prediction error with a prescribed probability. In MuJoCo, with omni-wheel roller contacts and torque saturation, the scheme keeps at least $10$\,cm of clearance, tracks the plan within $2$\,cm RMS, keeps the tilt below the limit and plans in $9$--$12$\,ms, $10$--$15$ times faster than a full-model nonlinear MPC that enters the inflated obstacles; with predicted obstacles, the conformal inflation removes the collisions of a constant-velocity predictor in $30$ random crossing scenarios and reduces them in an endless run on a closed this http URL source code and video illustrations are available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [ForceLDM: A Force-aware Latent Dynamics Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03294)
*Peiyuan Tang,Yao Li,Xiaodong Zhang,Wuyang Zhang,Qin Xia,Yanyong Zhang,Zijiang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForceLDM，一种力感知潜动力学模型，通过蒸馏未来接触与运动动力学特征来指导接触丰富操作中的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅视觉策略难以推断接触状态和交互力，现有引入力/触觉信号的方法多将其作为辅助观测，未充分利用其建模未来交互动力学的作用。

Method: ForceLDM不直接预测未来观测，而是用具备未来力/力矩和光流特权信号的教师网络提取未来动力学特征，蒸馏到仅使用当前观测的学生网络；并引入课程式渐进噪声注入以缓解对未来特征的过度依赖，提升鲁棒性与泛化。

Result: 在五个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上，ForceLDM优于现有最优基线，能有效泛化到新物体，并对环境扰动保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 利用未来力/力矩与运动信息蒸馏学习紧凑的潜动力学表示，可在部署时仅依赖当前观测实现对未来接触和物理动力学的推理，从而提升接触丰富操作策略的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires fine-grained control and complex physical interactions with the environment, posing significant challenges for robot policy learning. Vision-only policies struggle with such tasks, as contact states and interaction forces are difficult to infer from visual observations alone. Existing methods mitigate this issue by incorporating force or tactile sensing. However, they often treat these signals as auxiliary observations without fully exploiting their role in modeling future interaction dynamics. To address this, we propose ForceLDM, a force-aware latent dynamics model that learns future contact and motion dynamics to guide action generation. Rather than directly predicting future observations, which can entangle task-relevant dynamics with irrelevant sensory details, ForceLDM learns a compact representation of future dynamics through knowledge distillation. Specifically, a teacher network with privileged future force/torque and optical flow signals extracts future dynamic features, which are then distilled into a student network trained only on current observations. This enables the student model to reason about upcoming contacts and physical dynamics without relying on future sensory inputs at deployment. To improve robustness and generalization, we further introduce a curriculum-based progressive noise injection strategy to mitigate over-reliance on future features. Experiments on five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate that ForceLDM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, generalizes effectively to novel objects, and remains robust to environmental perturbations. Project page and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [VerifLM: Physically Verified Image-Edit-Guided Goal Pose Generation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03268)
*Xuanyou Chen,Jinyu Chen,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VerifLM 将图像编辑得到的目标构型转化为经物理验证的 6-DoF 物体位姿，用于机器人操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作目标更容易通过视觉示例表达，而难以用显式几何约束进行数值化指定。

Method: VerifLM 从编辑图像恢复主动物体与被动物体的初始相对位姿，提示视觉语言模型生成轴向采样约束和任务特定验证程序；候选位姿在物理模拟器中评估，并由 VLM 选择最符合任务的保留候选。为避免依赖预定义网格和物理参数，方法从 RGB-D 观测重建度量几何，并构建多组变化物理与几何参数的假设仿真，通过并行世界验证按满足验证程序的世界数量对候选排序。

Result: 在九项操作任务中，VerifLM 在八项上取得最高任务成功率，且在需要精确朝向和接触关系的目标上性能提升最大。

Conclusion: 结合图像编辑目标、VLM 生成约束与并行物理验证，VerifLM 能在未知几何与物理参数条件下有效生成并筛选可用于规划的 6-DoF 物体位姿。

Abstract: Many manipulation goals are easier to visualize than to specify numerically through explicit geometric constraints. We present VerifLM, a framework that converts image-edited goal configurations into physically verified 6-DoF object poses for planning. Using an edited image, VerifLM recovers an initial relative pose between an active object and a passive object, then prompts a vision-language model to generate axis-wise sampling constraints and a task-specific validation program. Candidate poses are evaluated in a physics simulator, and the VLM selects the most task-consistent retained candidate. To operate without predefined object meshes or physical parameters, VerifLM reconstructs metric object geometry from RGB-D observations and constructs multiple hypothetical simulations with varying physics and geometry. Parallel-world validation evaluates candidates across geometry--physics variations and ranks them by the number of worlds in which they satisfy the validation program. We evaluate VerifLM on nine manipulation tasks. VerifLM achieves the highest task success on eight of nine tasks, with the largest performance gain on goals requiring precise orientation and contact relationships.

</details>


### [54] [TRAILS: Trajectory Generation for Automated Indoor Locomotion Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03254)
*Juho Kalliokoski,Evan G. Center,Steven M. LaValle,Timo Ojala,Basak Sakcak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRAILS 是一个自动化框架，用于生成完整的虚拟导航场景，包括环境布局、路径与时间参数化运动剖面；它基于约束、种子可控且可复现，适合遥操作与人类被试实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR 导航与模拟机器人运动实验需要大规模、真实且多样化的环境和轨迹，以避免重复并保证受控实验中的可比性；手工设计在满足几何与运动学约束、保持多样性和条件间可比性方面成本高且困难。

Method: 该框架结合受 Wave Function Collapse 启发的约束式程序化环境生成器、基于优化三次 Bézier 曲线的轨迹生成，以及在运动学约束下生成速度剖面；用户只需少量直观参数即可自动生成大量满足预设几何与运动学约束的不同导航场景，且采用显式、种子控制的算法流程而非数据驱动模型。

Result: 生成场景包含走廊、开放空间和障碍物等多样室内环境，并提供时长相同但在路径几何、与墙距离和速度剖面上变化的轨迹，同时尽量减少这些特征间的系统性依赖；生成结果透明、可复现、可配置，代码已公开。

Conclusion: TRAILS 能自动生成大规模、受约束且可复现的虚拟导航场景，适合遥操作与人类被试实验等需要受控多样轨迹的研究场景。

Abstract: Virtual reality experiments involving navigation and simulated robot motion often require large, realistic environments and numerous distinct trajectories to avoid repetitive navigation while providing sufficient variation for controlled experiments. Designing such scenarios manually is labor-intensive, particularly when trajectories must satisfy predefined geometric and kinematic constraints while remaining diverse and comparable across experimental conditions. This paper presents TRAILS, an automated framework for generating complete virtual navigation scenarios consisting of an environment layout, a path, and a time-parameterized motion profile. The framework combines a constraint-based procedural environment generator inspired by Wave Function Collapse, trajectory generation using optimized cubic Bézier curves, and speed-profile generation subject to kinematic constraints. The generated scenarios provide diverse indoor environments with corridors, open spaces, and obstacles, together with trajectories of identical duration that vary in path geometry, wall proximity, and velocity profiles while minimizing systematic dependencies between these characteristics. The framework is designed as an easy-to-use tool requiring only a small number of intuitive user-defined parameters to automatically generate large sets of distinct navigation scenarios satisfying prescribed geometric and kinematic constraints. By relying on explicit, seed-controlled algorithmic procedures rather than data-driven models, the generated scenarios remain transparent, reproducible, and configurable, making the framework well suited for telepresence and human-subject experiments. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [55] [Towards end-to-end co-design for one-shot printable task-specific soft robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03216)
*James Wood,André Farinha,Will Browne,Josh Pinskier,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于仿真的软体机器人协同设计框架，可联合优化材料分布、气动执行器布局与方向及控制，并直接生成可用FDM一体制造、无需后装配的软体臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人协同设计虽能联合优化形态与控制，但设计往往难以制造；而面向制造的方法通常限制执行器布置或需要手工装配。

Method: 构建可微的二维平面应力材料点法（MPM）仿真器，引入经实验验证的材料模型和基于经验数据的三维打印充气执行器代理模型；联合优化材料分布、气动执行器位置与方向以及控制，并用梯度优化设计执行指定末端轨迹的软体臂。

Result: 优化设计可从仿真直接转移到一次性整体FDM制造，执行器集成其中且无需后装配；物理实验表明制造出的软体臂能复现仿真预测的行为。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了从任务级目标到物理可实现软体机器人的路径，同时保留对形态与驱动方式的较大设计自由度。

Abstract: Soft robot co-design methods can jointly optimise morphology and control, but the resulting designs are often difficult to manufacture, while manufacturable approaches typically restrict actuator placement or require manual assembly. We present a simulation-based co-design framework that jointly optimises material distribution, pneumatic actuator placement and orientation, and control, while producing designs that can be fabricated monolithically using fused deposition modelling. The framework uses a differentiable 2D plane-stress Material Point Method simulator, incorporating an experimentally validated material model and an empirically derived surrogate model of a 3D-printed inflatable actuator. We demonstrate gradient-based optimisation soft robotic arms for prescribed tip trajectories. Optimised designs are transferred directly from simulation to one-shot monolithic FDM fabrication, with integrated actuators and no post-assembly. Physical experiments demonstrate that the resulting arms reproduce the behaviour predicted in simulation. This framework provides a path from task-level objectives to physically realisable soft robots while retaining substantial freedom over both morphology and actuation.

</details>


### [56] [Rollout then Recycle: 3,718 Labelled Real-Robot Episodes from Benchmarking 7 Policies on 12 Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03168)
*Praveen Selvaraj,Lorenzo Uttini,Ville Kuosmanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 发布 ArmnetBench v0.1 全部 3,718 个 episode：包含 600 条遥操作演示与 3,118 条七种模仿学习/视觉-语言-动作策略在 12 个桌面任务上的 rollout，采集于低成本 SO-101 机械臂；其中 73% rollout 为失败，并由操作员逐条标注为成功、次优或失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建共享任务、共享训练演示和相同实验单元下的多策略 rollout 语料，便于分析策略失败模式、进行模仿学习与 VLA 评测，并支持奖励建模等研究。

Method: 每个任务的所有策略均使用相同的 50 条演示进行训练或微调，并在同一实验单元上 rollout；每个 episode 包含三路相机视图、关节状态和动作，提供 LeRobot v3.0 与奖励模型格式，发布于 Hugging Face Hub；文档记录采集流程及可能影响使用的局限。

Result: 数据集共 3,718 个 episode，包括 600 条遥操作演示和 3,118 条策略 rollout，覆盖 7 种策略与 12 个桌面任务；73% 的 rollout 为失败；所有 rollout 均有成功/次优/失败的人工标注，并包含多视角观测、关节状态与动作。

Conclusion: 该数据集适合研究多策略在共享任务上的失败分布、策略评估与奖励建模，但使用者需关注作者记录的采集流程和局限性对结论外推的影响。

Abstract: We release all 3,718 episodes from the ArmnetBench v0.1 evaluation ( arXiv:2607.24481 ), which contains 600 teleoperated demonstrations and 3,118 rollouts of seven imitation-learning and vision-language-action policies on 12 tabletop tasks, recorded on low-cost SO-101 arms; 73% of the rollouts are failures. Every policy for a task was trained or fine-tuned on the same 50 demonstrations and rolled out on the same cell, so the corpus contains many policies' failures on shared tasks. An operator labelled every rollout successful, suboptimal or failure. Each episode has three camera views, joint states and actions and is available on the Hugging Face Hub in LeRobot v3.0 and a reward-model format. This note documents how the episodes were collected and the limitations that could affect their use.

</details>


### [57] [FineCraft: Enhancing VLA Models for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03163)
*Shuaike Zhang,Ziheng Quan,Junhao Ran,Muhe Ding,Shaokun Wang,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FineCraft，一个面向MetaFine挑战的细粒度操作框架，通过任务路由的混合策略集成DINOv2-DiT与π0.5，实现指令接地、局部视觉精度与持续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度操作需同时解决指令理解、局部视觉精确感知和长时执行问题；预训练模型还需适配视觉扰动、指令变化与多阶段任务。

Method: 采用统一观测-动作接口，任务路由混合策略在独立适配的DINOv2-DiT与预训练π0.5策略间选择；利用局部视觉特征定位指令对象部件，结合演示与专家续写学习动作，并在执行中依据新观测重规划。

Result: 在MetaFine Challenge中总体得分0.878，排名第四，五个任务均超过公开参考基线；官方结果与本地诊断覆盖视觉扰动、指令变化和多阶段操作。

Conclusion: FineCraft表明任务路由混合策略、局部视觉特征与执行重规划能有效提升细粒度操作性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation couples instruction grounding, local visual precision, and sustained execution. We present FineCraft, a framework that adapts pretrained models for fine-grained manipulation in the MetaFine Challenge. A task-routed mixture of policies selects independently adapted DINOv2-DiT or pretrained $\pi_{0.5}$ policies through a common observation-action interface. Local visual features help identify the instructed object parts, demonstrations and expert continuations teach the required actions, and new observations guide replanning during execution. FineCraft ranks fourth with an overall score of $0.878$, exceeding the published reference on all five tasks. Official results and local diagnostics characterize performance across visual perturbations, instruction changes, and multi-stage manipulation. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [58] [KlaskTron: An Open-Source Platform for Physical Adversarial Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03138)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Jona Schulz,Maurus Derungs,Carlo Angelini,Tobias Meier,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源低成本桌面级对抗式多智能体强化学习测试平台 KlaskTron，基于桌面游戏 KLASK，提供硬件、仿真数字孪生与 sim-to-real 基线，实现零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理多智能体强化学习缺乏可及、标准化且可复现的基准；现有物理平台昂贵、依赖专用设施或缺少开放设计与机制，阻碍仿真成果向真实系统迁移。

Method: 设计双 CoreXY 龙门架与高扭矩无刷电机的桌面机器人平台，成本低于 2500 美元；发布 CAD/BOM 与装配文档，构建 NVIDIA Isaac Lab 的 GPU 原生数字孪生，并采用学习到的神经执行器模型建立经校验的 sim-to-real 基线。

Result: 该基线支持零样本策略迁移，训练得到的智能体在真实平台上表现出涌现的对抗行为；硬件设计、推理软件栈及仿真与 MARL 设置均公开以保障可复现性。

Conclusion: KlaskTron 为物理对抗式 MARL 提供了低成本、标准化、可复现的开放基准与完整生态，有助于连接仿真训练与真实机器人系统。

Abstract: Progress in robot learning, particularly physical multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), is currently challenged by a limited availability of accessible, standardized benchmarks. While simulation-based MARL has produced remarkable emergent behaviors, from coordinated team play to complex tool use, translating these advances to physical systems remains difficult. A key barrier is infrastructural: physical platforms are often prohibitively expensive, require specialized facilities, or lack open mechanisms and designs for reproducibility. We introduce KlaskTron, an open-source, low-cost (<\$2500 USD), desktop-scale robotic testbed for adversarial MARL based on the dynamic tabletop game KLASK. The platform features dual CoreXY gantries with high-torque brushless motors, enabling the high-speed, precise manipulation required for competitive play. Crucially, we provide a complete ecosystem: hardware designs (CAD, BOM), a GPU-native digital twin in NVIDIA Isaac Lab for high-fidelity modeling and simulation, and a validated sim-to-real baseline using a learned neural actuator model. We demonstrate that this baseline enables zero-shot policy transfer, with trained agents exhibiting emergent adversarial behaviors. To ensure full reproducibility of our results, all project assets are released publicly: hardware CAD/BOM and assembly documentation at [ this https URL ], inference software stack at [ this https URL ] and the simulation and MARL setup at [ this https URL ].

</details>


### [59] [A Tightly Coupled Magnetic-Inertial SLAM System for Field Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03106)
*Chuan Huang,Tongxing Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MISLAM：一种紧耦合磁-惯性SLAM系统，利用IMU与九磁强计阵列，在GNSS拒止及视觉退化环境下实现不依赖外部里程计和平台运动假设的定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿山、隧道和灾区等场景中GNSS不可用，且粉尘、烟雾或弱光会导致相机和LiDAR退化；地面与空中机器人任务需要不依赖可见光、预装基础设施或轮式接触的互补传感模态。

Method: MISLAM将惯性导航模型与局部多项式磁场模型结合用于短期运动估计，并采用全局高斯过程磁场模型通过误差状态卡尔曼滤波器进行漂移校正；系统仅使用IMU和九磁强计阵列，不依赖外部里程计或平台特定运动假设。

Result: 在三个室内手持采集数据集上的离线评估显示，总端点闭合误差相较磁-惯性里程计降低45.7%–78.0%；弱光下视觉-惯性里程计失效后MISLAM仍保持跟踪，水平闭合误差为0.332 m，但垂直漂移仍是局限。

Conclusion: 结果表明磁-惯性SLAM可作为机器人传感套件在视觉退化条件下的互补定位模态。

Abstract: Field robots operating in mines, tunnels, and disaster areas require reliable localization despite GNSS denial and degraded camera and LiDAR measurements caused by dust, smoke, or poor illumination. Missions involving both ground and aerial robots further motivate complementary sensing modalities that do not depend on visibility, preinstalled infrastructure, or wheel contact. This letter presents MISLAM, a tightly coupled magnetic-inertial simultaneous localization and mapping system using an IMU and a nine-magnetometer array, without relying on external odometry or platform-specific motion assumptions. The system combines the inertial navigation model with local polynomial magnetic-field models for short-term motion estimation and a global Gaussian process model for drift correction using an error-state Kalman filter. Offline evaluation on three indoor datasets collected with a handheld rig shows reductions in total endpoint closure error of 45.7% - 78.0% relative to magnetic-inertial odometry. Under poor illumination, MISLAM maintains tracking after visual-inertial odometry fails, achieving a horizontal closure error of 0.332 m, although vertical drift remains a limitation. These results support magnetic-inertial SLAM as a complementary localization modality for robotic sensor suites operating under degraded visual conditions.

</details>


### [60] [VLALagBench: Understanding When and Why VLA Inference Latency Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03103)
*Qi Wen,Borui Xu,Hussein Amro,Hongshi Tan,Hanmin Lin,Chaoyi Ruan,Shanghao Liu,Gang Yang,Yuejiang Liu,Jiafei Duan,Bingsheng He,Jason Clemons,Christos Kozyrakis,Wenqi Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出VLALagBench基准，系统研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理延迟对机器人操作任务成功率的影响，并基于实验发现构建可预测任务成功率的经验模型，用于指导VLA算法与系统配置选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型有望实现通用机器人操作，但推理延迟在动态物理世界中对任务成功率的影响尚缺乏系统理解；需要量化不同任务、目标速度、执行模式和策略下的延迟敏感性。

Method: 构建VLALagBench综合基准，覆盖任务类型、物体运动速度、同步/异步执行模式及多种VLA策略；通过实验分析推理延迟与任务成功率关系，并利用有限校准数据拟合经验预测模型。

Result: 即使仅100 ms推理延迟，也可使运动目标上的任务成功率近乎减半，且敏感性随任务和策略显著变化，反映任务对精度与连续运动的不同需求；异步执行通过重叠推理与动作执行可恢复部分成功率，但会增加推理请求频率；经验模型在不同VLA策略下预测任务成功率的均方根误差仅为5.7%–7.8%。

Conclusion: VLALagBench及所提经验模型可根据推理系统延迟和单机器人吞吐量指导系统特定的VLA算法配置选择，以最大化任务成功率，为未来机器人算法-系统协同设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a promising path toward general-purpose robot manipulation. While inference latency can affect task success in a constantly evolving physical world, the relationship between latency and task success rate remains poorly understood. In this paper, we introduce VLALagBench, a comprehensive benchmark for studying VLA inference latency sensitivity across task types, object speeds, execution modes (synchronous and asynchronous), and VLA policies. Using VLALagBench, we find that even a modest inference latency of 100 ms can nearly halve task success on moving targets, with sensitivity varying substantially across tasks and policies. These differences reflect task-specific requirements for precision and continuous motion. By overlapping inference with action execution, asynchronous execution can recover some of the lost success at the cost of more frequent inference requests. Building on these findings, we develop an empirical model that predicts task success from limited calibration data, achieving root-mean-square prediction errors of only 5.7-7.8% across various VLA policies. Given an inference system's latency and per-robot throughput, these predictions guide the selection of system-specific VLA algorithm configurations to maximize task success. We believe VLALagBench and these findings provide a solid foundation for future robotics algorithm-system co-design.

</details>


### [61] [KlaskTron-IL: A Contact-Rich Adversarial Imitation Learning Benchmark with a Validated Sim-to-Real Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03091)
*Nicklas Sohm,Aswin Ramachandran,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源硬件双人对抗强化学习平台 KlaskTron，并基于学习到的执行器模型实现零样本 sim-to-real；同时发布多层级模仿学习数据集 KlaskTron-IL 及诊断基线，揭示接触丰富对抗动力学下标准 IL 的三类典型失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为双人对抗强化学习提供可复现的物理平台，并解决接触丰富、对抗性强场景中模仿学习方法的评测与迁移问题，使训练策略无需改动即可部署到真实机器人。

Method: 构建开源硬件平台 KlaskTron 与学习执行器模型；采集 80k 人类演示时间步，扩增 320k 对称性增强数据，并用经零样本验证的 PPO 策略生成最多 5M 合成专家轨迹；提供 BC、GAIL 等诊断基线并分析失效模式。

Result: 发现 BC 存在数据规模扩展平台期；GAIL 判别器可解释但梯度弱（peg-ball 接近恢复的 Spearman ρ=-1.0，但 logit 跨度小于 0.1）；存在非对称的 BC 先验与奖励密度交互。最强 BC 基线在遵守执行器模型 ±0.2 m/s 辨识范围时可零样本运行于真实机器人。

Conclusion: KlaskTron-IL 为接触丰富对抗场景下的模仿学习提供了物理可部署的基准与诊断工具；策略接口与执行器模型对齐是实现零样本 sim-to-real 的关键，但标准 IL 方法仍存在需解决的系统性失效模式。

Abstract: KlaskTron is an open-hardware physical platform for two-player adversarial reinforcement learning, with a learned actuator model that supports zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of trained policies. Building on this platform, we contribute KlaskTron-IL: a multi-tier imitation learning dataset (80k human demonstration timesteps collected directly on the physical robot, 320k symmetry-augmented timesteps, and synthetic expert trajectories up to 5M steps generated by the zero-shot-validated PPO policy), and diagnostic baselines that expose three characteristic failure modes of standard IL methods under contact-rich adversarial dynamics: BC's data scaling plateau, GAIL's interpretable but weak-gradient discriminator (Spearman $\rho = -1.0$ peg-ball proximity recovery, but a logit span under 0.1), and an asymmetric BC-prior x reward-density interaction. Because the policy interface used throughout this paper is the same one the actuator model bridges to hardware, a policy trained on this benchmark can run unchanged on the physical robot, as we demonstrate zero-shot for the strongest BC baseline, so long as its commands respect the actuator model's $\pm 0.2$ m/s identification range.

</details>


### [62] [PlanContext: Benchmarking In-Context Driving Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03067)
*Ruikai Li,Naiyu Gao,Hao Shan,Yizhe Fan,Yanhao Wu,Han Jiang,Yilong Ren,Zhenning Li,Haiyang Yu,Yanhu Shan,Rui Wu,Hao Zhao,Hongyang Li,Zhiyong Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PlanContext 基准：在固定同一观测场景的前提下改变高层驾驶意图，构建配对评测，用以刻画端到端驾驶模型的上下文相关决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶基准主要以安全、合规、舒适和行驶进度来衡量轨迹质量，对依赖上下文的决策能力刻画不足，缺少可直接归因于“当前情境应如何决策”的评估手段。

Method: PlanContext 通过在同一场景中改变高层驾驶意图，构造配对评测实例，从而以自然且可控的方式评估上下文决策；定义六类代表性驾驶上下文，并采用类别均衡指标，联合评估上下文决策正确性、道路合规性和动态安全性；在 NAVSIM 的 9,561 个场景条件实例上评测 23 个公开端到端驾驶模型。

Result: 实验表明，较高的常规驾驶质量分数并不能稳定对应较强的路线级上下文决策能力；进一步分析显示，模型行为会随决策时机、道路结构和交通交互，以及当前上下文所要求的决策类型而变化。

Conclusion: 上下文驾驶决策应作为评估端到端驾驶系统的重要互补维度，与常规轨迹质量指标共同使用。

Abstract: Real-world driving requires context-aware decision-making. Existing end-to-end driving benchmarks primarily assess trajectory quality through safety, compliance, comfort, and driving progress, leaving context-dependent decision-making insufficiently characterized. To fill this gap, we present PlanContext, a benchmark for evaluating in-context driving decision-making. PlanContext constructs paired evaluations by varying high-level driving intentions while keeping the observed scene fixed, enabling a natural and controllable assessment of context-dependent decision-making. We further define six representative driving contexts and jointly evaluate contextual decision correctness, road compliance, and dynamic safety with class-balanced metrics. Experiments on 9,561 scene-condition instances from NAVSIM and 23 public end-to-end driving models show that high conventional driving-quality scores do not consistently correspond to strong route-level in-context driving decision-making. Further analysis shows that model behavior varies with decision timing, road structure, and traffic interaction depending on the decision required by the current context. Our results highlight in-context driving decision-making as an important complementary dimension for evaluating end-to-end driving systems.

</details>


### [63] [BeyondContact: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Airflow-Based Non-Contact Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03059)
*Peiyu Yang,Mohamad S. Moudallal,Antonio Langella,Giulio Turrisi,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作为Isaac Lab开发了与机器人和任务无关的气流扩展，支持大规模并行强化学习训练，并在真实机器人上零样本部署混合推-吹策略和纯气流长方体朝向控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式机器人操作受末端执行器可达性和物体机械属性限制；气流可在不直接接触物体的情况下施力，扩展操作范围并避免不希望的接触，但缺乏合适仿真阻碍了强化学习在气流操作中的应用。

Method: 作者提出支持大规模并行训练的Isaac Lab气流仿真扩展，利用实验辨识的气流模型计算作用在物体上的力和力矩；基于该扩展训练混合推-吹（HPB）策略和仅用气流控制长方体朝向的矩形重定向（RR）策略；并设计可与夹爪共存的小型高速鼓风机以实现物理执行。

Result: 零样本真实世界部署中，HPB在20次试验中达到80%成功率；RR在四个目标朝向下达到2.6度的平均绝对朝向误差。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了气流操作强化学习仿真的空白，验证了接触式推挤与气流结合的混合操作以及纯气流非接触控制的可行性，并提供了项目材料以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Contact-based robotic manipulation is constrained by end-effector reachability and the mechanical properties of objects. Airflow can exert forces without direct robot-object contact, enabling manipulation of objects beyond the reach or when direct contact is undesirable. However, the lack of suitable simulation has hindered the application of reinforcement learning to airflow-based manipulation, limiting the broader adoption of this mechanism compared with contact-based methods. To address this gap, we developed a robot- and task-agnostic airflow extension for Isaac Lab that supports massively parallel training. The extension uses experimentally identified airflow models to compute the forces and torques exerted on objects. Based on this extension, we train a hybrid push-blow (HPB) policy that combines contact-based pushing with airflow manipulation and a rectangular reorientation (RR) policy that controls a cuboid's heading using airflow alone. To enable physical execution, we design a lightweight, high-speed blower that coexists with a gripper. In zero-shot real-world deployment, HPB achieves an 80% success rate over 20 trials, while RR achieves a mean absolute heading error of 2.6 degrees across four target headings. The project materials are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [64] [Swarm Choreography Made Simple: Superposed Guiding Vector Fields for Rigid Formation Path Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03016)
*Jo{ã}o Machado,Matthieu Verdoucq,Christophe Jouffrais,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于简单规则向量场叠加的分布式引导向量场框架，用于多无人机刚性编队路径跟随；各控制目标对应独立向量场分量，协调仅需交换单标量，并用五架四旋翼完成两种3D编队实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机刚性编队路径跟随需同时实现沿路径推进、向路径收敛、保持编队形状和避免碰撞，同时希望降低通信带宽、提高可扩展性和实际可实现性。

Method: 将控制目标分解并叠加为归一化切向项、收敛项、基于伪路径参数的局部协调项和局部分离避碰项；协调机制仅要求每个智能体交换一个标量。理论分析表明，在无分离项时集群收敛到期望路径与编队，避碰则得到形式化保证。

Result: 使用五架四旋翼执行两种3D编队实验：一是沿螺旋路径保持刚性V形编队，二是通过旋转圆形编队跟踪垂直线产生螺旋状运动。实验结果支持该框架的有效性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架能以低通信开销实现多无人机刚性编队路径跟随，并具有较好的实验可行性；但收敛性理论结果主要在无分离项条件下成立，避碰与编队收敛的完整耦合保证仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized guiding vector field framework for rigid formation path following in multi- UAV systems based on the superposition of simple rule-driven vector fields. Each control objective is encoded as a dedicated vector-field component: a normalized tangential term to ensure progression along a prescribed geometric path at a desired speed, a convergence term driving the formation toward the path, a local coordination term that preserves the desired formation shape through a pseudo-path parameter, and a local separation term for inter-agent collision avoidance. The coordination mechanism requires the exchange of only a single scalar per agent, resulting in low communication bandwidth. Partial theoretical results establish convergence of the swarm to the desired path and formation in the absence of the sepa- ration term, while collision avoidance is formally guaranteed. The approach is experimentally validated using a swarm of five quadrotors executing two 3D choreographies. The first experiment involves tracking a helical path while maintaining a rigid V-shaped formation. The second demonstrates a spiral- like motion obtained by a rotating circular formation tracking a vertical line. The experimental results support the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [65] [SyncHeC: Markerless Spatio-Temporal Hand-Eye Calibration via Differentiable Rendering and Continuous-Time Joint Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02999)
*Zhengdong Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SyncHeC 是一个无需标记、基于稠密可微渲染的连续运动手眼标定框架，可从单条机器人轨迹中联合估计相机几何与图像-关节状态时间偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续运动下的无标记手眼标定需同时解决相机几何与时间同步问题；现有方法通常依赖标记或分离估计，难以在短轨迹内获得高精度且可部署的标定结果。

Method: 提出 SyncHeC：利用连续时间正运动学使延迟可微，结合度量深度与掩膜轮廓提供几何约束，并通过离线规划器兼顾空间敏感性与时间激励；采用交替优化支持 Eye-to-Hand、Eye-in-Hand 和多相机标定。

Result: 在 7 自由度机械臂上，6.5 秒运动实现 0.21 cm 平移误差、0.34 cm 触碰测试误差，以及相对 87 ms 参考的 2.7 ms 延迟误差（95% 不确定度 ±1.5 ms）；与 CM-Calib 在匹配记录上相比，平移和标称延迟误差分别改善 3.7 倍和 2.3 倍；实验覆盖七种机械臂和四种深度传感器。补偿恢复的时间偏移后，在六类机器人和六项任务、1800 对匹配样本上策略成功率提升 8.1 个百分点（配对 95% 置信区间 [6.3, 9.9]）。

Conclusion: SyncHeC 能在短连续轨迹中实现高精度无标记手眼与时间偏移联合标定，并可实际提升机器人策略训练与部署性能。

Abstract: Markerless hand-eye calibration under continuous motion requires jointly resolving camera geometry and the time offset between images and joint states. We present SyncHeC (Synchronized Hand-Eye Calibration), a dense differentiable-rendering framework that estimates both from a single robot trajectory. Continuous-time forward kinematics makes latency differentiable, metric depth complements mask contours, and an offline planner combines spatial sensitivity with temporal excitation. Alternating updates support Eye-to-Hand, Eye-in-Hand, and multi-camera calibration. On a 7-DoF arm, one $6.5$\,s motion yields $0.21$\,cm translation error against a marker-board reference, $0.34$\,cm touch-test error, and $2.7$\,ms latency error relative to an $87$\,ms reference with $95\%$ uncertainty of $\pm1.5$\,ms. Against CM-Calib on matched recordings, translation and nominal latency errors improve by $3.7\times$ and $2.3\times$. Experiments span seven arms and four depth sensors. Compensating the recovered offset during policy training and deployment increases success by $8.1$ percentage points across six families and six tasks ($1{,}800$ matched pairs; paired $95\%$ confidence interval $[6.3,9.9]$ points).

</details>


### [66] [MS-DOTT: A Multi-Modal Dataset for Off-Road Traversability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02992)
*Shivani Subramanyan,Saksham Sharma,Samahith SA,Ahaan Kotian,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MS-DOTT多模态越野数据集，融合长距离3D sensing与短距离精细感知，并包含多种光照条件下的2D/3D传感、4D RADAR及本体感知信号，用于越野地形可通行性估计与多传感器融合研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境地形复杂、光照变化大且不可预测，现有传感配置难以同时获取远场环境上下文与近场地形细节，也难以覆盖多空间尺度与多成像模态，亟需高质量多模态数据集支撑鲁棒越野感知和可通行性估计。

Method: 构建MS-DOTT数据集，采集正常光照、低光照和无光照条件下的轨迹；结合本体感知信号与外部感知的图像、点云数据，覆盖长距离大尺度3D sensing和短距离高分辨率感知，并加入可提供点云速度估计的4D RADAR；通过适配当前最优的多传感器融合方法进行可通行性估计以验证数据集效用。

Result: 数据集包含多光照条件、多尺度2D/3D传感、4D RADAR和车辆动力学相关本体感知信号，并成功用于适配SOTA多传感器融合方法开展可通行性估计，展示了其实际可用性。

Conclusion: MS-DOTT将促进越野可通行性估计、非结构化环境感知和多传感器融合研究，为机器人社区提供推进鲁棒越野自主导航算法开发与评测的基础资源。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are gaining traction in off-road environments, driven by their rising demand in agriculture, defense, and search-and-rescue operations. However, these environments are inherently unpredictable and navigation remains a formidable challenge given the complex environmental conditions including challenging terrain and varying illumination. Off-road autonomy requires robust perception across multiple spatial scales and imaging modalities, yet most sensing setups struggle to capture both far-field environmental context and near-field terrain detail simultaneously across various illumination conditions. To facilitate development of robust off-road perception and traversability estimation systems, we propose MS-DOTT (Multi-Sensor Dataset for Off-road Terrain Traversability) a comprehensive multi-modal off-road dataset with long-range 3D sensing and short-range, fine-grained perception to facilitate high-fidelity terrain understanding by combining proprioceptive signals for tracking vehicle dynamics with exteroceptive imaging and pointcloud data. The dataset includes trajectories recorded across nominal lighting, low-light, and no-light conditions with diverse 2D and 3D sensing for both long-range large-scale and short-range high-resolution sensing, as well as 4D RADAR measurements that provide velocity estimates for detected pointclouds. We demonstrate the utility of the dataset by adapting a SOTA multi-sensor fusion method for the purpose of traversability estimation. This dataset will aid the robotics community in advancing research and development of methods for traversability estimation, off-road perception and multi-sensor fusion in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [67] [ReContact: Reconstruct and Retarget Human Contacts from Video for Contact-Rich Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02968)
*Zhongkai Gu,Yang Liu,Wei Zhu,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReContact 通过以物体为中心的接触计划重建并重定向人-物交互，解决遮挡下的物体位姿跟踪与接触力稳定问题，实现人形机器人全身接触转换操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要通过全身接触重新分配物体支撑，但人类视频中身体遮挡会破坏物体位姿跟踪并污染重定向运动；同时，仅复现物体轨迹无法保证机器人在预期接触点施加稳定力，易导致后续滑移或掉落。

Method: 提出物体中心接触计划，显式指定接触阶段、接触对、锚点、按压方向及粘附/滑动模式；利用接触先验在遮挡下稳定物体位姿重建；在冻结的全身跟踪器上训练残差策略，奖励在计划锚点按指定方向施力。

Result: 仿真大腿支撑重抓取任务中，三个训练种子下83.5%的回合实现双掌底部保持，最佳适配基线为0.16%；单一策略基于247条参考重解新放置，将工作空间内管传递成功率从20.4%提升至97.8%；在真实Unitree G1上执行7个接触丰富操作和2个移动操作任务。

Conclusion: ReContact 能利用人类视频学习可泛化的全身接触转换操作，并在仿真与真实人形机器人上验证有效性。

Abstract: Contact transitions allow humanoid robots to redistribute object support across the whole body, enabling manipulation beyond hand-only grasps. Human videos provide a scalable source of such transitions for robots to learn from. However, body occlusion disrupts object pose tracking and corrupts retargeted motion. Moreover, accurately reproducing the object trajectory does not ensure that the robot applies stabilizing forces at the intended contacts, risking slips or drops in later phases. To address these challenges, we propose ReContact, which reconstructs and retargets human-object interactions through an object-centric contact plan. The plan specifies contact phases, contact pairs, anchors, pressing directions, and sticking or sliding modes. Contact priors stabilize object pose reconstruction through occlusion. A residual policy over a frozen whole-body tracker is rewarded for making contact at planned anchors with forces aligned to the specified directions. In simulation, on a thigh-supported regrasp task, ReContact achieves the planned two-palm bottom hold in 83.5 of episodes over three training seeds, versus 0.16% for the best adapted baseline. One policy trained on 247 references re-solved for new placements raises in-workspace tube-transfer success from 20.4% (source reference only) to 97.8%. On a real Unitree G1, policies execute seven contact-rich manipulation and two loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Positive Safety Margins from Artificial Potential Field Reward Augmentation for Size-Robust Obstacle Avoidance under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02947)
*Ziyi Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在障碍物尺寸与精确位置不可观测的机械臂避障到达任务中，仅通过奖励函数中的水平人工势场项引入安全裕度，使强化学习策略在障碍半径分布外仍保持高安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中障碍物几何常不可观测，若策略输入包含障碍几何则难以应用；仅使用碰撞惩罚的奖励不约束障碍外间隙，导致策略贴障滑行，并在测试障碍半径超过训练值时失效。

Method: 在仿真 Franka Emika Panda 到达任务中，对每个双柱障碍（固定标称布局、随机漂移）周围安全裕度内的接近行为施加水平人工势场惩罚，并仅以隐藏观测训练策略；与无该奖励项的策略对比，评估半径扫描、最小间隙、碰撞消除速度、跨求解器与全观测泛化。

Result: 隐藏观测下，加入势场项的策略在 0.03–0.055 m 半径扫描中安全成功率高于 88%（30 个种子），无该项策略在最大测试半径下低于 10%；最小间隙为 47.9 mm 对 9.6 mm，训练碰撞消除所需中位步数至少提前 8 倍；优势在所有分布外半径上统计显著，并在三种强化学习求解器和全观测下成立；安全成功率与给定柱位置的经典势场规划器相当。

Conclusion: 单一解析奖励项可在不向策略暴露障碍几何的情况下诱导安全裕度，但该裕度是固定缓冲而非随半径自适应，且会使路径长度增加约 19%。

Abstract: Collision-free reaching is central to manipulation, yet real cells often leave the size and exact position of an obstacle unobserved, which excludes reinforcement learning controllers that take obstacle geometry as input. A reward limited to a collision penalty does not depend on the clearance outside the obstacle, so under a step cost its learned policy skims the obstacle surface and fails once the obstacle radius exceeds its training value. We investigate whether a single analytic reward term can induce a safety margin without exposing the obstacle to the policy. We augment the reward of a simulated Franka Emika Panda reaching task with a horizontal artificial potential field that penalizes proximity within a safety margin around each of two pillars (fixed nominal layout, random drift). Under hidden observation, policies trained with this term maintain a safe success rate above 88% across a radius sweep from 0.03 m to 0.055 m (30 seeds), while policies without it fall below 10% at the maximum tested radius; the term induces a minimum clearance of 47.9 mm against 9.6 mm without it and eliminates training collisions at least eight times earlier (median steps). The advantage is statistically significant at all out-of-distribution radii and holds across three reinforcement learning solvers and under full observation. Its safe success rate is comparable to that of a classical potential-field planner given the pillar positions. The margin is a fixed buffer rather than an adaptation to the radius, and it lengthens the path by about 19%.

</details>


### [69] [RESONATE: a Real Social Navigation Testbed for Autonomous Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02937)
*Stefano Trepella,Andrea Ostuni,Mauro Martini,Irene Borgini,Marcello Chiaberge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RESONATE真实世界社交导航基准，基于统一机器人平台、共享ROS 2软件栈、精确轨迹真值和结构化社交互动场景，完成320次物理实验并联合评估效率、安全、舒适度与社交接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主服务机器人在人类环境中进行社交导航至关重要，但缺乏大规模真实世界基准限制了研究进展与可比评估。

Method: 构建可复现的RESONATE测试床；评估8种控制器配置（经典与预测型局部规划器结合不同社交建模策略），覆盖8类代表性场景、每类5次重复，共320次物理实验；结合定量导航与社交指标以及参与者主观评估，联合衡量效率、安全、舒适度和感知社交行为。

Result: 结果显示不同方法存在场景相关权衡；DWA结合高斯社交代价地图在可靠性方面表现突出，SFM-MPPI在鲁棒性、导航效率与社交接受度之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: RESONATE为机器人社区提供真实世界基准、可复用基线、数据、测试床和一致评估协议，推动社交感知导航研究发展。

Abstract: Social navigation is essential for autonomous service robots operating in human environments, but progress remains constrained by the lack of extensive real-world benchmarks. This paper presents the Real Social Navigation Testbed (RESONATE), a reproducible benchmark for human-aware navigation based on a unified robotic platform, a shared ROS 2 software stack, precise trajectory ground truth, and structured social interaction scenarios. We evaluate eight controller configurations, combining classical and predictive local planners with different social modeling strategies, over eight representative scenarios with five repetitions each, yielding 320 physical experiments. The benchmark combines quantitative navigation and social metrics with participant-based qualitative assessments to jointly measure efficiency, safety, comfort, and perceived social behavior. Results reveal scenario-dependent trade-offs among the tested methods. The algorithms that stands out for their performance are the DWA with Gaussian social costmaps for its reliability, and the SFM-MPPI to provide the best balance between robustness, navigation efficiency, and social acceptance. Overall, RESONATE supports the robotics community with a real-world benchmark, reusable baselines, data, and testbed, and a consistent evaluation protocol for advancing socially aware navigation research.

</details>


### [70] [PointPilot: Unified Robotic Manipulation via Object-Centric 3D Point Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02930)
*Yihan Fang,Yuxin Tian,Ruixiang Wang,Zhanqi Zhang,Xing Hu,Zhen Yang,Huayi Zhou,Yongyi Su,Wei Gao,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointPilot 是一个以任务为中心、3D 对齐的机器人操作框架，基于统一 3D 空间中的点流预测，联合学习子任务规划、任务相关物体定位和 3D 点流预测，并实现跨不同实施例的零样本泛化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人泛化仍面临多维度域变化挑战，包括不同实施例、相机位姿和场景布局。现有方法常需建模全局场景而引入冗余，依赖额外动作头或动作专家，且多为半自动或单臂模式专用，难以统一支持跨实施例操作。

Method: 给定传感器观测和语言指令，PointPilot 端到端联合学习子任务规划、任务相关物体 grounding 与 3D 点流预测；仅表示任务相关物体（将机器人夹爪视为物体），避免预测无关场景部分；在统一坐标系中对齐 3D 空间表示，并直接由物体点流支持闭环、全自动、多模式动作执行，无需单独训练动作专家；通过共享 3D 点流空间处理异构数据，吸收不同实施例的演示数据。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验验证了方法有效性；尤其展示了零样本实施例泛化能力：单一训练策略可直接部署到未见过的实施例上，无需额外微调即可成功完成操作任务。

Conclusion: PointPilot 表明，以任务为中心的统一 3D 点流预测框架能够有效避免冗余场景建模和额外动作专家，并支持跨实施例、多模式、闭环自动操作，为机器人泛化提供了一种可扩展的统一范式。

Abstract: Generalization remains a central challenge in robotics, calling for policies that address domain variations from multiple dimensions such as diverse embodiments, camera poses, and/or scene layouts. In order to approach this problem, we introduce PointPilot, a task-focused and 3D aligned manipulation framework built on a 3D space point-flow prediction. Rather than modeling the global scene, PointPilot only represents task-focused objects (robot grippers treated as objects), avoiding the prediction of potentially redundant scene parts unrelated to the manipulation tasks. Moreover, PointPilot aligns 3D spatial representation in one unified coordinate and supports closed-loop and fully automatic action execution across different manipulation modes from object point flow without a separately trained action expert, while existing works typically need an extra action head or are (semi-)automatic and single-arm mode specific. Given sensor observations and language instructions, our model jointly learns subtask planning, task-relevant object grounding, and 3D point flow prediction in an end-to-end way. By processing heterogeneous data in a shared 3D point flow space, PointPilot is able to absorb demonstrations from different embodiments. Extensive simulative and real world experiments validate our method. Notably, we demonstrate zero-shot embodiment generalization with PointPilot. A single trained policy can be deployed on previously unseen embodiments and successfully complete manipulation tasks without any additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [71] [An intelligent satellite must do nothing well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02922)
*Fabrizio Pilone,Giordana Bucchioni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文主张，在自主抵近观测空间目标时，经典下一最佳视角规划与强化学习之争并非最关键设计轴；更关键的是是否利用轨道自然漂移、状态是否记录漂移轨迹、规划时域是否足够长，以及是否配置安全过滤层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者质疑将自主巡检简化为下一最佳视角规划与强化学习二选一。巡检航天器没有静止状态，停止推力后会沿轨道自然漂移，而该漂移可零燃料用于观测目标，因此需要识别真正影响燃料效率与安全性的设计轴。

Method: 采用控制变量式阶梯实验，每次只改变一个设计轴，考察是否允许漂移、状态表示是否记录漂移历史、规划时域是否足够长；同时比较算法通过世界模型所能表达的信息，尤其是模型是否考虑燃料；并在决策层下加入控制屏障函数滤波器进行安全约束。

Result: 燃料效率主要由三点决定：允许漂移、状态记录漂移所到之处、规划时域足够长；移除任一项会使燃料开销翻倍或更多。算法仍重要，但体现在表示能力上：基于模型且学习世界模型忽略燃料的智能体会多消耗数倍燃料。学习策略本身不具构造性安全保证，而控制屏障函数滤波器在两千次评估回合中消除了所有禁入区违规。

Conclusion: 算法选择的信息量低于漂移利用、状态表示、规划时域和安全层等设计轴；模型表示对燃料效率有实质影响；学习型策略需外接安全过滤才能可靠满足禁入区约束。

Abstract: Autonomous inspection of a resident space object is usually posed as a contest between classical next-best-view planning and reinforcement learning. We argue that the choice of algorithm is the least informative of the design axes. An inspector spacecraft has no rest state: when it stops thrusting, orbital dynamics carry it along a natural trajectory that can be used to observe the target at no fuel cost. On a controlled ladder in which one design axis is varied at a time, fuel efficiency turns out to be set by three things: whether the agent is allowed to drift, whether its state records where drifting has taken it, and whether its planning horizon is long enough for the benefit to appear. Removing any one of them doubles the fuel bill or more. The algorithm still matters, but through what it can represent: a model-based agent whose learned world model ignores fuel spends several times more of it. Finally, no learned policy is safe by construction, and a control barrier function filter beneath the decision layer removed every keep-out-zone violation in two thousand evaluation episodes.

</details>


### [72] [AnchorHEC: Targetless Hand-Eye Calibration of Wrist and Fixed Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02884)
*Guebin Hwang,Taeyeop Lee,Yeonseo Lee,Hyosup Shin,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorHEC 是一种无需标定物的手眼标定框架，可在单一流程中同时标定腕部相机与固定相机，仅需 RGB 图像、正运动学（FK）和已知相机内参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统常同时使用腕部相机和固定相机，因此需要分别求解两类相机到机器人的变换。多数手眼标定方法依赖标定靶标或机器人本体观测；基于场景三维重建的方法虽无需靶标，但通常只处理腕部相机，无法统一标定固定相机。

Method: 该方法利用 FK 和共享的 3D 基础模型重建来初始化两类相机。针对腕部相机，引入 FK 约束的图像束调整，以降低重建位姿与尺度误差的影响；若优化失败或内点过少，则放宽对可能不准确 FK 位姿和镜头参数的约束。随后利用细化后的腕部变换与 FK 三角化度量三维点，并估计固定相机位姿，从而在单一流程中完成两类相机标定。

Result: 在仿真中，相对于基于重建的基线方法，AnchorHEC 在三种骨干网络上对两类相机平均将平移标定误差降低 86.4%、旋转误差降低 71.6%；并在真实机器人上进行了验证。

Conclusion: AnchorHEC 证明了无需靶标即可统一标定腕部与固定相机的可行性，并通过 FK 约束优化与松约束机制提升了场景重建式手眼标定的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent robot systems combine wrist and fixed cameras, requiring camera-to-robot transforms for both. Most hand-eye calibration methods rely on targets or robot-body observations. Scene-based methods using 3D reconstruction require neither but address only wrist cameras. We introduce AnchorHEC, a targetless hand-eye calibration framework for wrist and fixed cameras in a single procedure using RGB images, forward kinematics (FK), and known camera intrinsics. We initialize both camera types using FK and a shared 3D foundation model reconstruction. FK-constrained wrist-image bundle adjustment reduces the impact of reconstructed pose and scale errors. If this refinement fails or retains too few inliers, we relax the constraints on potentially inaccurate FK poses and lens parameters. We use the refined wrist transform and FK to triangulate metric points and estimate fixed-camera poses. Across three backbones, calibration errors in simulation decrease by 86.4% in translation and 71.6% in rotation, averaged over both camera types, relative to reconstruction-based baselines. We also validate AnchorHEC on a physical robot. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [73] [Design and Experimental Validation of the Separation and Deployment System for the CHERI Lunar Micro-Rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02872)
*Ahmet Mustafa Kangal,Oğuzhan Şahan,Teoman Efe Aycan,Görkem Egemen Güloğlu,Halil Ersin Söken,Ali Emre Turgut,Uluç Saranlı,Mustafa Mert Ankaralı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对5 kg级CHERI月球微型巡视器，设计、优化并测试了其分离系统，解决了弹射速度损失、振动失效与着陆恢复等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星微型巡视器必须在承受发射振动后可靠地与着陆器分离并安全弹射出去，因此分离机构的可靠性、结构动力学性能与着陆恢复能力至关重要。

Method: 分离系统由铝制底板、中心非爆炸式压紧释放机构和非对称四弹簧弹射阵列组成；通过多体动力学模型以等效底盘阻尼表征柔性轮腿和底盘吸能，并重新优化弹簧；针对振动测试中弹簧导向座卡滞和底板34 Hz一阶模态问题，采用密度基拓扑优化提升基频；最后开展正弦、随机、冲击振动试验及基于光学运动捕捉的1/6质量模型分离试验。

Result: 拓扑优化将底板基频由34 Hz提升至84 Hz，高于着陆器70 Hz要求且满足1.6 kg质量预算；最终构型通过验收级正弦、随机和冲击振动测试且无结构退化；分离试验确认巡视器避开着陆器，自由飞行与仿真结果在弹射速度逐次波动范围内一致。

Conclusion: 着陆结果主要由触地和稳定动力学决定，残余俯仰角速度是主要可控设计参数，自扶正恢复应作为部署架构的组成部分。

Abstract: Reliable separation mechanisms are critical for planetary micro-rovers, which must withstand launch vibrations and then eject the vehicle clear of the lander. This paper presents the design, optimization, and testing of the separation system of the CHERI (Challenging Environment Exploration Rovers for Intelligence) lunar micro-rovers. Sized for a 5 kg vehicle, the assembly comprises an aluminum ground plate, a central non-explosive hold-down and release mechanism, and an asymmetric four-spring ejection array. Initial separation trials revealed a velocity deficit caused by energy absorption in the flexible wheel-legs and chassis during the ejection stroke. This loss was represented as equivalent chassis damping in a multi-body dynamic model, and the springs were re-optimized accordingly. Early hardware iterations failed vibration testing, first through spring jamming in the guide housings and then through a 34 Hz fundamental mode of the flat ground plate. A density-based topology optimization raised the fundamental frequency to 84 Hz, above the 70 Hz requirement of the lander, within a 1.6 kg mass budget. The final configuration passed acceptance-level sinusoidal vibration, random vibration, and shock testing without structural degradation. Separation tests with a weight-matched one-sixth mass model, tracked by optical motion capture, confirmed clearance of the lander and reproduced the simulated free flight to within the run-to-run variation of the measured ejection velocity. These tests further showed that landing outcome is dominated by touchdown and settling dynamics, indicating that the residual pitch rate is the main controllable design parameter and confirming self-righting recovery as an integral element of the deployment architecture.

</details>


### [74] [ReGATE: Recurrent Gated Visual Fusion for Reactive Humanoid-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02871)
*Hao Zhu,Jorge Peña-Queralta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReGATE 是一个面向人形机器人 loco-manipulation 的全身控制器，融合第一视角深度、本体感知与在线运动/接触指令，在视觉中断和无需外部物体位姿估计的条件下实现抓取—运输—释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人执行 loco-manipulation 时需同时协调物体获取、运输和释放，并依赖机载感知响应用户指令；但视觉观测常不完整甚至中断，且传统方法依赖外部物体位姿估计。

Method: 提出 ReGATE，基于教师—学生框架的全身控制器：融合第一视角深度、本体感知和在线运动与接触命令；通过循环适配器进行交互状态估计，预测物体几何与物理接触而无需外部物体位姿估计器；采用 head-wise 与全局门控调节视觉更新对本体感知通路的注入；使用视觉 grounding 与 corruption 课程训练适配器，并用阶段感知初始化提升短暂接触过渡的覆盖。

Result: 仿真中，330 次静态物体获取试验成功率为 85.2%，相同协议下 VisualMimic 为 48.5%；440 次运动物体获取试验成功率为 85.9%。在 Unitree G1 上部署展示了指令化抓取—运输—释放序列及横向偏移箱体获取；定性硬件实验显示，在初始观测后、接触指令前相机被遮挡时仍能成功获取箱体。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个将机载深度与本体感知的循环门控融合从感知运动扩展到人形机器人 loco-manipulation、并面向视觉感知中断场景的工作。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinating object acquisition, transport, and release from onboard sensing while responding to user commands and incomplete visual observations. We present ReGATE, a whole-body controller that combines egocentric depth, proprioception, and online locomotion and contact commands. Built on a teacher-student framework, ReGATE integrates interaction-state estimation through a recurrent adapter that predicts object geometry and physical contact without an external object-pose estimator. Head-wise and global gates regulate visual updates to a proprioceptive pathway. A visual-grounding and corruption curriculum trains the adapter, while phase-aware initialization increases coverage of brief contact transitions. In simulation, ReGATE achieves 85.2% success across 330 static-object acquisition trials, compared with 48.5% for VisualMimic under the same protocol, and 85.9% across 440 moving-object acquisition trials.
