Deployment on a Unitree G1 demonstrates commanded grasp-transport-release sequences and acquisition of laterally displaced boxes. A qualitative hardware experiment further demonstrates successful box acquisition when the camera is occluded after an initial observation and before the contact command. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to extend recurrent gated fusion of onboard depth and proprioception from perceptive locomotion to humanoid loco-manipulation under interrupted visual sensing.

</details>


### [75] [SomaAlign: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Representation Learning for Soft Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02794)
*Haoyuan Wang,Shuai Peng,Yongkai Liao,Yuxiao Sun,Wei Luo,Xinxing Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出软体机器人抓取多模态感知数据集Soma、抓取结果数据集Soma-Success及表示学习方法SomaAlign，用于提升多模态感知的鲁棒性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人抓取的多模态感知受模态相关噪声、语义歧义和缺乏协调多传感器数据限制，亟需统一的数据与表示学习基础。

Method: 构建Soma数据集，包含100个物体、1000条连续抓取轨迹和74914个按采集周期分组的多模态样本，覆盖远程RGB、手部RGB、深度、触觉、IMU和结构化文本；另构建Soma-Success独立抓取结果数据集。SomaAlign分三阶段学习表示：掩码重建估计模态相对可靠性；可靠性感知对比对齐使用成对自适应温度调整对齐锐度；冲突感知语义约束提升样本内跨模态一致性并保持全局特征几何。

Result: SomaAlign在30个有向跨模态检索任务上平均Recall@10达90.66%；表示可支持物理属性识别、多模态问答、跨模态生成及跨数据集迁移到抓取结果预测。在Soma-Success上，冻结SomaAlign编码器加轻量探针实现94.59%的事后分类准确率和84.07%的早期预测准确率。

Conclusion: Soma、Soma-Success和SomaAlign共同为软体机器人操作中鲁棒且可迁移的多模态感知提供了数据与表示学习基础。

Abstract: Robust multimodal perception for soft robotic grasping is challenged by modality-dependent noise, semantic ambiguity, and limited coordinated multisensory data. We introduce Soma, a dataset containing 100 objects, 1,000 continuous grasping trajectories, and 74,914 multimodal samples grouped by software acquisition cycle, spanning remote RGB, hand RGB, depth, tactile, IMU, and structured text observations. We also collect Soma-Success, an independent grasp-outcome dataset with 1,000 trials (998 retained after quality control), to evaluate frozen-encoder transfer for post-hoc grasp outcome classification and early grasp success prediction. Built on Soma, SomaAlign learns multimodal representations in three stages: masked reconstruction estimates relative modality reliability; reliability-aware contrastive alignment uses pair-specific adaptive temperatures to adjust alignment sharpness; and conflict-aware semantic constraints improve intra-sample cross-modal consistency while preserving global feature geometry. SomaAlign achieves an average Recall@10 of 90.66% across 30 directed cross-modal retrieval tasks. The learned representations also support physical attribute recognition, multimodal question answering, cross-modal generation, and cross-dataset transfer to grasp outcome prediction. On Soma-Success, frozen SomaAlign encoders with lightweight probes achieve 94.59% post-hoc classification accuracy and 84.07% early prediction accuracy. Together, Soma, Soma-Success, and SomaAlign provide a data and representation-learning foundation for robust and transferable multimodal perception in soft robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Policy Patches: Distilling RL Specialists into Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02777)
*Zhennan Jiang,Tingying Xie,Shilong Ji,Shu'ang Yu,Tianxing Zhou,Yuhui Chen,Jiahuan Gao,Kaizhi Xie,Yuanfan Xu,Feng Gao,Yu Wang,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REPAIR 提出一种值引导蒸馏框架，将轻量级 RL 专家的能力迁移到单一流匹配 VLA 中，在部署时移除专家并提升精度关键阶段的操作成功率与动作平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略能处理长时程操作中的非关键阶段，但在短时、精度关键阶段容易失败。轻量级 RL 专家可修复这些问题，但部署时需保留辅助控制器和切换机制；直接模仿专家 rollout 又会复制动作抖动，并使 VLA 对自身执行中遇到的状态准备不足。

Method: REPAIR 结合离线初始化与在线策略精炼：在每个关键阶段观测处，查询对应专家生成多个动作候选，并用其 critic 加权构造监督分布；通过共享的加权流匹配目标，先利用专家辅助 rollout 的观测训练 VLA，再利用 VLA 自身执行采集的观测进行在线精炼。在线采集时专家仅提供监督而不接管控制，部署时专家被移除。

Result: 在 6 个真实机器人任务、每任务 30 次评估试验中，REPAIR 在全部 6 个任务上达到 100% 成功率，且在每个任务上的平均末端执行器抖动均低于专家辅助执行。

Conclusion: REPAIR 能将 RL 专家的精度关键阶段能力蒸馏进单一流匹配 VLA，无需部署时保留专家或切换机制，并在真实机器人任务中实现全成功率与更平滑的动作执行。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can handle noncritical stages of long-horizon manipulation yet fail during short precision-critical phases. Lightweight reinforcement learning (RL) specialists can address these failures without directly applying RL to the full VLA, but retain auxiliary controllers and switching mechanisms at deployment. We study how to transfer their capabilities into a single flow-matching VLA. Direct imitation of specialist rollouts can reproduce action jitter and leave the VLA unprepared for states encountered during its own execution. We introduce REPAIR, a value-guided distillation framework that combines offline initialization with on-policy refinement. At each critical-phase observation, REPAIR queries the corresponding specialist for multiple action candidates and weights them using its critic to construct a supervision distribution. A shared weighted flow-matching objective trains the VLA first on observations from specialist-assisted rollouts, then on observations collected through its own execution. Specialists provide supervision without taking control during on-policy collection and are removed at deployment. Across six real-robot tasks with 30 evaluation trials per task, REPAIR achieves 100% success on all six tasks, with lower mean end-effector jerk than specialist-assisted execution on every task.

</details>


### [77] [Right Ranking, Wrong Scale: Reproducing the Robotic World Model's Dynamics Model and Testing the Uncertainty of Its Follow-up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02699)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 复现 RWM 动力学模型并检验 RWM-U 的集成不确定性：其误差排序能力较好，但不确定性校准严重不足、方差塌缩，按预测步长重缩放只能部分修复，且发布评估流水线存在两处缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证 Robotic World Model（RWM）动力学模型的可复现性，并评估其后续 RWM-U 在离线策略优化中用于奖励惩罚的不确定性估计是否可靠。

Method: 重新实现 RWM 动力学模型并校验梯度一致性；在留出数据上比较基于模型自身 rollout 的训练与单步训练；评估 RWM-U 集成分歧对 rollout 误差的排序能力和校准覆盖；测试滚动集成成员、方差塌缩、按预测步长重缩放及留一 episode 评估；审查发布评估流水线。

Result: 实现与发布模型梯度一致；在预注册长预测步长上，模型自身 rollout 训练在全部 10 个 episode 上优于单步训练，但在短单步窗口上更差。RWM-U 集成分歧对 100 步 rollout 误差的排序优于预测步长（Spearman rho=0.90），但一 sigma 覆盖率仅 4.6%，远低于校准高斯的 68%；滚动集成成员后覆盖率升至 33%。采样的平方误差损失使各成员方差趋近于零。按预测步长重缩放可在留一 episode 评估下恢复一 sigma 覆盖，但不能恢复二 sigma 覆盖。另发现发布评估流水线两处缺陷。

Conclusion: RWM-U 的集成分歧可用于相对排序，但当前不确定性估计严重欠校准，且训练目标导致方差塌缩；步长重缩放仅部分缓解。结论受限于单一机器人、单一步态、单一数据集，且未训练策略。

Abstract: We reimplement the dynamics model of the Robotic World Model (RWM) and test the uncertainty estimate that its follow-up, RWM-U, uses to penalize reward in offline policy optimization. Our implementation matches the released model's gradients. On held-out data, training on the model's own rollouts beats one-step training at the preregistered long horizon for all ten episodes, but loses on short one-step windows. RWM-U's ensemble disagreement ranks rollout errors better than forecast step (Spearman rho = 0.90 at 100 steps), yet its spread substantially understates error: one-sigma coverage on the released checkpoint is 4.6% at 100 steps, versus 68% for a calibrated Gaussian. Rolling ensemble members on their own predictions raises coverage to 33%. The implemented squared-error loss on sampled predictions drives each member's variance toward zero. A horizon-specific rescaling restores one-sigma coverage under leave-one-episode-out evaluation, but not two-sigma coverage. We also identify two defects in the released evaluation pipeline. The study covers one robot, one gait and one dataset; it trains no policy.

</details>


### [78] [Source-Resolved Collective Cognition in Robotics: Mathematical Foundations for Multimodal and Collective Estimation, and Their Reach across the Cognition Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02693)
*Juergen Graf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该著作从基础出发系统构建广义奇异值分解（GSVD）及其估计理论，用于在固定输出度量下、以基无关方式比较作用于同一未知量的两个线性算子，并回答第一算子沿哪些状态方向支配第二算子及其支配程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两个作用于同一未知量的线性算子时，需要一种不依赖坐标基、能定量刻画方向性支配关系的工具；同时，GSVD相关估计理论分散且缺少自包含、可复现的基础阐述。

Method: 第一部分自包含重推所需线性代数与统计估计工具，包括内积、四子空间、正交矩阵、谱定理、二次型、普通SVD、李群切空间扰动、Jacobian、协方差、白化、Fisher信息、CCA、互信息、判别准则与协方差交集；第二部分给出GSVD的透明形式与一般维度形式，并用两条互补路线证明；第三部分用五种视角手工计算并数值验证同一小矩阵对；第四部分区分集体系统中的信息归因、子空间几何、统计依赖与决策论判别；第五部分评估GSVD在认知环路中的作用与局限；第六部分讨论数值实践并给出经测试的参考实现。

Result: 形成一部从线性代数与估计理论基础到GSVD证明、实例、概念分离、应用评估和数值实现的系统性著作；所有数值均以双精度验证，假设与定理明确区分。

Conclusion: GSVD为比较两个线性算子提供了原理清晰、基无关的框架，可支撑估计、统计依赖分析、判别决策及认知环路中的相关应用，并以明确假设和可复现实现保证可靠性。

Abstract: The Generalized Singular Value Decomposition (GSVD) is a central tool for comparing two linear operators that act on the same unknown. Once the output metrics are fixed, it answers the basis-independent question "along which state directions does the first operator dominate the second, and by how much?". This foundational volume builds the GSVD and its surrounding estimation theory from the ground up. Part I re-derives every piece of linear algebra and statistical estimation machinery needed later -- inner products, the four subspaces, orthogonal matrices, the spectral theorem, quadratic forms, the ordinary SVD, Lie-group tangent perturbations, Jacobians, covariance, whitening, Fisher information, CCA, mutual information, discrimination criteria, and covariance intersection -- with self-contained derivations and worked numerical examples. Part II states the GSVD in both a transparent and a dimensionally general form, then proves it by two complementary routes. Part III works one small matrix pair through five different lenses, each computed by hand and verified numerically. Part IV separates information attribution, subspace geometry, statistical dependence, and decision-theoretic discrimination for collective systems. Part V evaluates the role and limits of the GSVD across the cognition loop. Part VI covers numerical practice, including a tested reference implementation. Every number printed in this document was verified in double precision. Where a statement is an assumption rather than a theorem, it is labelled as such.

</details>


### [79] [Closing the Dynamics Gap through Self-Directed Robotic Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02691)
*Xilun Zhang,Liam Achenbach,Luis Pabon,Matthew Foutter,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CUE 是一个自主智能体系统，通过主动与环境交互来弥补 Real2Sim2Real 中摩擦、恢复系数等接触动力学参数不可直接观测的“动力学鸿沟”，并在台球任务上显著提升仿真与真实部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖相机观测的 Real2Sim2Real 可重建场景，但接触丰富场景中的摩擦和恢复系数等动力学参数必须通过交互才能观测，形成动力学鸿沟。

Method: 提出 CUE：仅给定高层指令且无环境重置条件下，智能体自行构建感知、辨识物理参数，并优先选择仿真最不准确的交互进行探索与校准。

Result: 在仿真台球的 25 种隐藏物理条件下，CUE 将留出球位置误差相对最强基线降低 72%，并进一步部署到真实台球桌上。

Conclusion: 主动交互式参数辨识与针对性探索能够有效缩小接触丰富任务的仿真到现实动力学差距，并具备真实场景部署潜力。

Abstract: Agentic Real2Sim2Real systems can reconstruct a scene in simulation from camera observations, but contact-rich dynamics such as friction and restitution cannot be observed without interaction. We call this the dynamics gap and present CUE, an agentic system that closes it by interacting with the environment itself. Given only a high-level instruction and no resets, the agent builds its own perception, identifies physical parameters, and targets interactions where its simulation is least accurate. On billiards, CUE reduces held-out ball-position error by 72% relative to the strongest baseline across 25 hidden-physics conditions in simulation, and we further deploy it on a real pool table. More details can be found on the project website: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [Visuo-tactile Diffusion Policy for Robotic Manipulation with Auxiliary Tactile Dynamics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02677)
*Kaihong Ouyang,Zihao Su,Chengxuan Zheng,Weiyi Lai,Dong Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种视觉-触觉扩散策略，融合双视角视觉与双指尖触觉历史，并通过训练期辅助任务预测未来触觉变化，在长程机器人装配中显著优于纯视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人装配涉及抓取、对齐和插入，需要跨阶段可靠的感知与动作。视觉提供全局物体位姿和场景上下文，而指尖触觉提供局部接触线索，尤其在接触状态变化时至关重要。

Method: 方法以两个相机视角和两个指尖触觉图像的4步历史为条件生成动作序列，并用循环时间表示整合两种模态的近期观测。训练时增加辅助目标：从时间表示和示范动作预测未来2步的指尖图像变化，以促使表示捕捉动作下接触演化；该目标仅用于训练，不增加推理计算。

Result: 在MuJoCo装配实验中，3个独立训练种子下完整模型平均成功率为59.0%±2.6%，相比纯视觉基线42.7%±1.5%提升16.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 融合视觉-触觉历史信息并利用训练期接触动态预测辅助目标，可有效提升长程装配策略成功率，且不引入额外推理开销。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic assembly requires reliable perception and action across grasping, alignment, and insertion. Vision captures global object pose and scene context, where as fingertip touch provides local contact cues that are especially useful as contact states change. We present a visuotactile diffusion policy that combines four-step histories from two camera views and two fingertip tactile images to condition action-sequence generation. A recurrent temporal representation integrates recent observations from both modalities. To encourage it to capture how contact evolves under action, an auxiliary objective predicts fingertip-image changes two steps ahead from the temporal representation and demonstrated actions. This objective is used only during training and adds no inference-time computation. Across three independent training seeds in MuJoCo assembly experiments, the full model achieves an average success rate of $59.0\% \pm 2.6\%$, improving on the visual-only baseline ($42.7\% \pm 1.5\%$) by 16.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [81] [HeLM: Hierarchical Explicit Language Memory for Non-Markovian Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02660)
*Gyeonghun Kim,Donghoon Kim,Minsoo Choi,Suyun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HeLM分层VLA框架，通过显式语言记忆与事件触发更新解决非马尔可夫任务中的工作记忆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA多遵循马尔可夫假设，仅将当前观测映射为动作，难以处理需要历史信息或跨任务持久上下文的非马尔可夫环境；受人类“内心独白”启发。

Method: HeLM将任务相关历史以显式语言维护，采用分解式事件检测和事件触发记忆更新，形成分层VLA框架；在10个真实非马尔可夫任务、56个变体上评估，包括5个需跨episode持久上下文的介入任务。

Result: 相比最强基线，episode内成功率提升12.8个百分点；在组合泛化任务上达到78%子任务成功率；长时程下内存使用最多减少4.5倍，运行速度比关键帧基线快2.5倍。

Conclusion: 显式语言记忆结合事件触发分层更新可有效赋予VLA工作记忆，提升非马尔可夫任务与组合泛化性能，并兼顾长时程效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have enabled fine manipulation capabilities, yet performing tasks that require working memory remains a key challenge. Most existing VLA architectures operate under a Markovian assumption, mapping only the current observation to an action, which leads to failure in non-Markovian environments. Inspired by the human 'inner monologue', we propose Hierarchical Explicit Language Memory (HeLM), a hierarchical VLA framework that maintains task-relevant history in explicit language through factorized event detection and event-triggered memory updates. We evaluate HeLM across 10 non-Markovian real-world tasks with 56 variants, including five newly formulated inter-episode tasks that require persistent context across task boundaries. HeLM improves intra-episode success by 12.8 percentage points over the strongest baseline and achieves 78% subtask success on compositional generalization tasks, while using up to 4.5x less memory and running 2.5x faster than the keyframe baseline at long horizons.

</details>


### [82] [DexArena: A Unified Benchmark for Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02629)
*Haonan Ping,Jianshu Hu,Yimin Wang,Yi Zhu,Pai Liang,Qiyan Jin,Qirui Bao,Ying Qian,Xinyang Ding,Hongfei Qiao,Tianle Xue,Yucheng Bai,Soo Wei Jie,Shuguang Li,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DexArena，一个面向长时程双臂灵巧操作的机器人学习基准，包含 10 个长时程任务和 30 个阶段级任务，用于系统评估技能组合、双手协调、视觉泛化与技能过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准多聚焦孤立技能或仅支持有限末端执行器，难以系统评估长时程双臂灵巧操作中的多技能组合、精确协调、泛化视觉理解和稳健技能过渡。

Method: DexArena 覆盖家庭与工业场景，每个长时程任务由显式定义且具物理依据成功条件的阶段组成；提供统一基准接口、经校验的人类遥操作示范、标准化仿真配置、场景随机化、轨迹回放和任务级评估，以支持不同策略学习算法的测试。

Result: 大量基线实验表明，现有方法在精确操作、场景变化泛化和技能组合方面仍面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: DexArena 为发展面向可泛化长时程灵巧行为的机器人学习方法提供了具有挑战性且可复现的基准。

Abstract: Long-horizon dexterous manipulation remains a fundamental challenge for robot learning, requiring the composition of multiple skills, precise bimanual coordination, generalizable visual understanding, and robust skill transitions. Yet existing manipulation benchmarks largely emphasize isolated skills or support only a limited range of end-effectors, making these capabilities difficult to evaluate systematically. We introduce DexArena, a benchmark designed to evaluate learning and generalization in long-horizon bimanual dexterous manipulation. DexArena features 10 long-horizon tasks and 30 stage-level tasks spanning diverse household and industrial scenarios. Each long-horizon task is composed of explicitly defined stages with physically grounded success conditions, enabling reliable evaluation of both intermediate progress and end-to-end task completion. A unified benchmark interface and verified human-teleoperated demonstrations enable testing different policy learning algorithms, with standardized simulation configurations, scene randomization, trajectory replay, and task-level evaluation. Through extensive baseline experiments, we identify substantial challenges in precise manipulation, generalization to scene variation, and skill composition. DexArena establishes a challenging and reproducible benchmark for developing robot learning methods that move toward generalizable long-horizon dexterous behavior.

</details>


### [83] [Neural Integral Control Barrier Functions for Agile Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02626)
*Dasari Raj Vamsi,Vrushabh Zinage,Efstathios Bakolas,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从数据中学习动力学、名义控制器和障碍函数的积分控制屏障函数（ICBF）安全滤波方法，并以可行性而非几何定义安全；在6维动态自行车敏捷竞速中实时消除不安全事件并严格满足执行器约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF/ICBF依赖已知控制仿射动力学、闭式障碍函数和已知名义控制器；几何安全标签还忽略执行器限制与应急机动可行性，高速时问题更严重。

Method: 用神经常微分方程学习动力学，用模仿学习得到名义控制器，用监督回归学习障碍函数，并据此解析构造ICBF滤波约束；障碍函数按可行性训练，即仅当存在应急机动仍能在执行器限制下避免约束时状态才安全；在线程序中执行器限制作为硬边界，学习到的屏障行可松弛以保证处处可解，并给出返回输入相对真实模型输入的偏差界。

Result: 在200个留出赛道布局的6维动态自行车竞速中，滤波器消除全部12次不安全事件且成功率不变，单CPU核心上超过100Hz；在输入约束基准中每个回合均满足约束，而联合训练基线仅53.38%；在被控对象摩擦存在大误差时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可完全从数据学习ICBF所需三要素，并将可行性安全、精确执行器限制与实时性结合，在敏捷竞速中显著优于联合训练基线且对模型误差鲁棒。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) are a standard way to keep a planner inside a safe set, but they assume control affine dynamics, a barrier function given in closed form, and a known nominal controller. Integral control barrier functions (ICBFs) remove the first assumption and absorb input constraints, yet they still assume a given nominal controller and a barrier function. In this work, we present an approach to learn all three from data, using neural ordinary differential equations (ODEs) for the dynamics, imitation for the nominal controller, and supervised regression for the barrier function. The learned dynamics, nominal controller, and the barrier function are used to build the ICBF filter constraint analytically. Furthermore, the barrier is trained on viability rather than geometry, meaning that a state counts as safe only if some emergency maneuver can still avoid the constraint under the actuator limits, a correction that geometric labels miss entirely and that grows with speed. Actuator limits then enter the online program as hard bounds while the learned barrier rows may be relaxed, which keeps the program solvable at every state and the actuator limits exact, and we bound how far the input it returns can drift from the one the true models would give. On agile racing with a 6-D dynamic bicycle, the filter removes all 12 unsafe episodes from 200 held out layouts at an unchanged success rate and above 100 Hz on one CPU core, holds the input constraint on every episode of a benchmark where a jointly trained baseline holds it on 53.38%, and keeps that advantage under large errors in the plant friction.

</details>


### [84] [MAPO: Mixture of Experts with Alternating Policy Optimization for Universal Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02613)
*Seonghyeon Lim,Hyeonwoo Lee,Mincheol Kim,Kimin Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MAPO，通过交替优化MoE策略中的路由器和专家网络，缓解策略梯度更新时因动态路由导致的新旧策略散度误估计，从而提升人形机器人通用运动跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要在人类环境中执行多样任务，通用运动跟踪至关重要；MoE策略通过动态路由增强策略表达能力，但在MoE策略梯度更新中，动态路由会使新旧策略散度被误估计，进而导致更新过于保守或过大，得到次优策略。

Method: 提出Mixture of Experts with Alternating Policy Optimization（MAPO），交替优化路由器与专家网络，以避免MoE策略更新中的散度误估计。

Result: MAPO可持续跟踪182个PPO+MoE无法跟踪的运动，而PPO+MoE仅能跟踪1个MAPO无法跟踪的运动；在对比方法中取得最高跟踪成功率，并在大多数误差指标上取得最低跟踪误差。

Conclusion: MAPO通过交替优化路由器和专家，有效缓解MoE策略梯度更新中的散度误估计问题，显著提升人形机器人通用运动跟踪的成功率与精度。

Abstract: Universal humanoid motion tracking is essential for enabling humanoid robots to perform diverse tasks in human environments. To capture the wide diversity of human motions, mixture-of-experts (MoE) policies have been widely adopted, leveraging dynamic routing to enhance policy expressiveness. However, we identify that divergence between old and updated policies can be misestimated during policy-gradient updates of MoE policies, due to their dynamic routing. Because most policy-gradient algorithms regulate policy updates based on the divergence, this misestimation can lead to either overly conservative or excessively large updates, which can yield a suboptimal policy. In this paper, we propose Mixture of Experts with Alternating Policy Optimization (MAPO), which alternately optimizes the router and the experts to avoid the divergence misestimation. MAPO consistently tracks 182 motions that PPO with MoE fails to track, whereas PPO with MoE tracks only one motion that MAPO fails to track. MAPO also attains the highest tracking success rate among the compared methods and the lowest tracking error across most error metrics, demonstrating its superior performance. Videos can be found on the project website: this https URL

</details>


### [85] [W2A: Distilling World Priors into Action Models for Real-Time Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02600)
*Xiaopeng Zhang,Yongjin Mu,Yueyang Weng,Chengzhi Zhao,Yanjie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出W2A两阶段蒸馏框架，将视频扩散模型的中间去噪表征蒸馏到动作模型中，测试时丢弃视频模型，实现实时机器人操作并显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models在测试时需显式想象未来状态，带来大量计算开销和延迟，难以满足实时机器人控制需求。

Method: W2A在训练阶段从视频扩散模型的中间去噪表示中提取监督信息并蒸馏到动作模型，测试阶段完全丢弃视频模型；其核心假设是高维视频潜表征中存在足够有效的低维压缩子空间，可用于监督动作生成。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中取得有竞争力的成功率；在LIBERO上平均成功率达95.9%，推理速度比代表性WAM基线约快21倍。

Conclusion: W2A能够在保留视频模型未来想象监督优势的同时，避免测试时视频生成开销，实现高效实时机器人操作。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently shown great promise in robotic control, benefiting from the future imagination capability of large-scale pretrained video models. However, most existing WAMs require explicitly imagining future states at test time, incurring substantial computational overhead and latency that make them unsuitable for real-time robotic control. In this paper, we propose W2A, a two-stage distillation framework for real-time robotic manipulation that distills intermediate denoising representations from a video diffusion model into an action model during training, while entirely discarding the video model at test time. We hypothesize that although video latent representations are high-dimensional, a compressed low-dimensional subspace is sufficient to provide effective supervision for action generation. This makes it possible to transfer such information effectively into an action model. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate competitive success rates while significantly improving inference efficiency. On LIBERO, W2A achieves a 95.9% average success rate. More importantly, W2A achieves about 21x faster inference than the representative WAM baseline.

</details>


### [86] [Central pivot point control of a body-attached spring-mass runner: periodic gaits, stabilization, template embedding and approximate analytic return maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02564)
*İzel Sever,O. Kaan Karagöz,M. Mert Ankaralı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出中央枢轴点（CPP）髋力矩律，将带躯干跑步模型精确解耦为平面中心力问题，从而恢复SLIP式解析可处理性，并给出首个躯干模型的解析返回映射与步态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLIP描述跑步者质心运动但忽略躯干；在躯干模型中，髋力矩耦合姿态与质心运动，破坏SLIP分析所依赖的中心力结构，导致此类模型几乎只能数值研究。

Method: 定义CPP使地面反作用力通过质心，从而精确解耦俯仰并将支撑相化为平面中心力问题；直立姿态下利用时间反演对称得到单参数周期步态族；用支撑相俯仰律和步幅级调节器稳定步态；在线性径向模板下获得闭合形式、重力修正的解析返回映射及死拍步幅控制器。

Result: 解析返回映射预测离地速度和顶点位置的平均误差分别为1.6%和0.9%；嵌入跑步者的自然步态对称且触地无需力阶跃，近可逆性论证解释其单位Floquet乘子，标量方程预测误差在0.8%以内；基于解析映射的死拍控制器可跟踪速度、高度和姿态变化。

Conclusion: CPP使带躯干模型能够使用为SLIP发展的解析工具，在保留姿态耦合的同时实现可解析、可稳定和可控制的步态分析。

Abstract: The spring-loaded inverted pendulum (SLIP) describes the center-of-mass (COM) motion of runners but has no trunk. In trunk models, the hip torque couples posture and COM motion and destroys the central-force structure on which SLIP analysis relies. As a result, trunk models have been studied almost only numerically. We show that this structure can be restored. The central pivot point (CPP), a hip-torque law that directs the ground reaction force through the COM, decouples pitch exactly and reduces stance to a planar central-force problem. At upright posture, time-reversal symmetry produces one-parameter families of periodic gaits. A stance-phase pitch law and a stride-level regulator stabilize all computed gaits, also with bounded hip torque and friction. Under CPP, any radial force law in virtual-leg coordinates can be embedded independently of posture. For a linear radial template, the stance radius and the constant-momentum sweep have exact closed forms. With a new gravity correction, the resulting analytic return map, to our knowledge the first for a trunk model, predicts liftoff velocity and apex position with mean errors of 1.6% and 0.9%. The natural gaits of the embedded runner are symmetric and need no force step at touchdown. A near-reversibility argument explains their unit multiplier, and a scalar equation predicts them within 0.8%. A deadbeat stride controller based on the analytic map tracks changes of speed, height and posture. CPP thus makes trunk models accessible to the analytic tools developed for SLIP.

</details>


### [87] [Sequential Recovery Updates for Learned Controllers under Successful-Trajectory Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02555)
*Zongtan Li,Tianming Liu,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过约束“进入规则”而非冻结技能参数来安全地组合学习控制器的方法，在保证不破坏已有成功轨迹的前提下新增扰动恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有学习控制器已能处理许多扰动，进一步改进需要学习修正策略并判断何时启用；仅冻结技能参数不能防止新进入规则把旧技能原本成功的状态重定向，从而破坏既有能力。

Method: 在测得失败处搜索反馈技能，并拟合一个读取观测的进入规则，使其拒绝累计成功轨迹上的所有状态；仅当组合控制器能自主复现新恢复且精确重放所有受保护轨迹时才接纳该技能，并给出有限轨迹条件下的保持性判据。

Result: 在两个类人系统的四条运动流上，每条流两次更新在192个留出扰动中新增62次恢复，且不损失基控制器的成功；去除约束可多恢复33个条件，但会使8条受保护轨迹完全无法完成，而仅排除接纳所证明必要的状态并不能挽回该代价。

Conclusion: 约束进入规则可在组合新技能时提供可验证的旧能力保持保证；安全性来自对受保护轨迹的精确重放约束，但会牺牲部分潜在恢复性能。

Abstract: A competent learned controller already recovers from many disturbances. Improving it means learning a correction, and learning when not to apply it. Freezing a skill's parameters does not settle the second half. In a controller that routes among frozen skills, a new entry rule can redirect the states an old skill succeeded on. We therefore constrain entry instead of parameters. Each update searches for a feedback skill at a measured failure. It fits an entry rule that reads observations and rejects every state on an accumulated successful trajectory. The skill is admitted only if the composed controller reproduces the new recovery on its own and replays every protected trajectory exactly. A finite-trajectory condition states when this preserves the previous controller. One mechanism runs on four streams: two motions on each of two humanoid systems. Two updates per stream add 62 recoveries over 192 withheld perturbations, and lose none of the base controller's successes. We then price that guarantee. Removing the constraint recovers 33 more conditions but stops eight protected ones from completing at all, and excluding only what admission proves necessary recovers none of that cost.

</details>


### [88] [Energy-Based Policy Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02546)
*Weirui Ye,Yen-Jen Wang,Elvis Hsieh,Zhiyang Dou,Shibo Zhao,Pieter Abbeel,Yilun Du,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EPO：一种基于能量的策略编排器，用于在动态高维机器人任务中运行时选择并细化“低层策略-目标”对，以提升跟踪可行性、精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层控制将任务规划与低层控制分离，但在接触、平衡或扰动变化快于高层策略更新时，固定“规划器到策略”的接口会失效；执行时不仅需选择低层策略，还需选择该策略应接收的特定目标。

Method: 将问题形式化为策略编排：运行时选择可能保持可行且能跟踪规划目标的策略-目标对。针对模块化目标条件策略族，EPO 为每个策略学习关于低层目标的复合能量模型，低能量表示更高可行性与更准确目标实现；运行时细化各策略目标并执行能量最低的策略-目标对。由于能量模型是策略特定的，新策略只需学习自身能量模型即可加入，无需重训现有策略或集中式调度器。

Result: 仿真中，EPO 通过在执行前细化策略特定目标并基于能量分数选择性切换策略，改善了经验上的精度-鲁棒性权衡；并在真实人形 loco-manipulation 任务上，使用相同冻结策略与能量模型完成 sim-to-real 部署。

Conclusion: EPO 表明基于能量的策略编排可有效解决动态高维任务中固定分层接口的失配问题，并支持模块化策略的可扩展集成与真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Hierarchical control scales robot behavior by separating task-level planning from low-level control. In dynamic, high-dimensional tasks, however, a fixed planner-to-policy interface can fail when contacts, balance, or disturbances evolve faster than high-level policy updates. Execution therefore requires choosing not only which low-level policy to run, but also which policy-specific goal should be given to it. We formulate this problem as policy orchestration: runtime selection of a policy-goal pair that is likely to remain feasible while tracking the planner target. We develop EPO, an energy-based policy orchestrator for modular goal-conditioned policy families. For each policy, EPO learns a composite energy over low-level goals, where lower energy corresponds to higher feasibility and more accurate target realization. At runtime, EPO refines each policy's goal and executes the lowest-energy policy-goal pair. Because energies are policy-specific, new policies can be incorporated by learning their own energy models, without retraining the existing policies or a centralized scheduler. In simulation, EPO improves the empirical precision-robustness trade-off by refining policy-specific goals before selectively switching among policies based on energy scores. We further demonstrate sim-to-real deployment of the same frozen policies and energy models on real humanoid loco-manipulation tasks. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [89] [Interactive 3D Cutaway Visualization for Subsurface Target Localization in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02537)
*Yuxin Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Wanwen Chen,Qi Zeng,Septimiu E. Salcudean*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出交互式3D剖切可视化界面Virtual Box，用于改善机器人辅助手术中混合AR深度线索模糊导致的目标定位问题；在组织体模实验中显著降低3D定位误差和深度误差，但增加完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合增强现实（AR）叠加显示中，皮下解剖结构可能“漂浮”于组织表面之上，产生模糊的深度线索，影响机器人辅助目标定位的准确性。

Method: 提出Virtual Box，一种可独立操纵观察视角以检查皮下目标的交互式3D剖切界面。采用受试者内、平衡设计的组织体模对照研究，15名工程学生分别使用Virtual Box和深度增强混合AR叠加界面，操控da Vinci器械定位水槽中的虚拟目标；以配准3D经直肠超声测量3D定位误差和深度误差，并记录完成时间与主观评分。

Result: Virtual Box将平均3D误差由12.92 mm降至4.96 mm（降低61.6%），深度误差由9.48 mm降至2.07 mm（降低78.2%），均p<0.001；空间理解、深度感知和信心评分均更高（未校正p<0.001）；完成时间由21.64 s增至32.58 s（p=0.008）；总体工作负荷无显著差异。

Conclusion: 在该受控任务中，Virtual Box提高了定位准确性和感知空间理解，但增加了完成时间，支持其作为手术导航方案进一步评估。

Abstract: Purpose: Blended augmented reality (AR) overlays can provide ambiguous depth cues in which subsurface anatomy floats above the tissue surface. We investigate whether an interactive 3D cutaway visualization can improve robot-assisted target localization. Methods: We propose Virtual Box, an interactive 3D cutaway interface that allows users to independently manipulate the viewing perspective to inspect subsurface targets. In a controlled, counterbalanced within-subject tissue phantom study, 15 engineering students used Virtual Box and a depth-enhanced blended AR overlay to position a da Vinci instrument at virtual targets in a water tank. We used registered 3D transrectal ultrasound to measure 3D localization error and depth error. We also collected completion time and subjective ratings. Results: Virtual Box reduced mean 3D error from 12.92 to 4.96 mm (61.6% reduction) and depth error from 9.48 to 2.07 mm (78.2% reduction; both p < 0.001). Spatial understanding, depth perception, and confidence ratings were higher (all unadjusted p < 0.001), but completion time increased from 21.64 to 32.58 s (p = 0.008). Overall workload did not differ significantly. Conclusion: Virtual Box improved localization accuracy and perceived spatial understanding in this controlled task, with additional completion time, supporting further evaluation for surgical navigation.

</details>


### [90] [A Study on the Effects of Finger Movements on a Humanoid Hugging Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02517)
*Daigo Suhara,Tomoko Yonezawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验考察拥抱机器人手指运动的有无及模式对用户心理感受的影响，发现手指运动模式会调节用户从机器人处寻求的感觉与关怀感，但不影响其对机器人主动身体接触的抵抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拥抱机器人中手指运动是否存在及其模式如何影响用户心理，从而为拥抱机器人的触觉交互设计提供依据。

Method: 采用实验研究，操纵拥抱机器人手指运动的有无与运动模式，并测量用户的心理反应，包括用户从机器人处寻求的感觉、关怀感以及对机器人主动身体接触的抵抗。

Result: 手指运动的存在与模式会影响用户从机器人处寻求的感觉；重复运动和多指异步运动倾向于增强这些感觉；所有手指同时运动与轻柔拥抱倾向于引发更强的关怀感；对机器人主动身体接触的抵抗未观察到差异。

Conclusion: 手指运动设计可调节拥抱机器人的用户体验：重复与异步多指运动可增强用户寻求的感觉，同步全指运动结合轻柔拥抱可提升关怀感，但对身体接触抵抗无显著影响。

Abstract: This study aimed to examine the impact of finger movements in hugging robots on users by experimentally investigating how the presence and patterns of such movements affect users psychologically. The results suggest that the presence and patterns of finger movements influence the sensations users seek from the robot; specifically, repetitive movements and asynchronous movements across multiple fingers tended to enhance these sensations. Additionally, simultaneous movement of all fingers and gentle hugging tended to evoke a greater sense of care. Conversely, no differences were observed regarding resistance to physical contact initiated by the robot.

</details>


### [91] [Neuro Inspired Robotics: What Robot Learning Can Borrow from the Sensorimotor Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02510)
*Ruihan Zhang,Davy Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述认为机器人策略在日常操作中的失败主要源于设计缺陷，而非单纯数据不足，并提出借鉴神经系统机制的新一代机器人模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略即使基于百万级轨迹和数十台机器人预训练，仍难以泛化到新任务、新物体、动态场景和持续改进技能；作者将此归因于模型与系统设计问题。

Method: 提出若干可检验的设计假设：从身体借鉴柔顺控制，策略同时输出位置与刚度；从视觉系统借鉴快速动作通路，构建相机帧率驱动的专用快速视觉与反应式策略；从小脑借鉴与策略并行的预测器，预测指令后果并自我校准；从运动皮层借鉴单一模型组合动作基元，在感知边界验证、针对最弱基元带假设练习并推理逼近目标；将已验证基元从代码固化为权重以提升流畅性与响应速度。

Result: 摘要未给出实验结果；其贡献是系统性提出受神经科学启发的机器人模型设计框架，并将各项提案定位为待检验假设。

Conclusion: 作者主张下一代机器人应具备自主行动能力，这是部署与递归自我改进的基础；所提设计需通过后续实验验证。

Abstract: Robot policies still fail at everyday manipulation: an unfamiliar task, an object never held before, a scene that changes mid-motion, a skill that should improve with use. They fail despite pretraining on over a million trajectories, pooled from dozens of robots at great expense, while a child acquires all of these abilities within a few years of learning from a single body. This survey argues that the gap is one of design, and proposes designs for the next generation of robot models, drawn from how the nervous system solves the same problems. From the body we take compliance: the policy commands stiffness alongside position, so the controller yields where the task allows and presses where it must, which greatly lowers the demand for precise position output. From the visual system, where a fast pathway for action runs in parallel with the slow pathway for recognition, we take a dedicated fast vision system for motor behavior: it precomputes the quantities the policy needs and drives a reactive policy at camera rate. From the cerebellum we take a predictor beside the policy that forecasts the consequence of each command and recalibrates from its own error. From motor cortex we take a single model that composes action units from primitives, verifies each at a sensory boundary, practices the weakest unit with a hypothesis in mind, and reasons each attempt closer to the goal. Proven primitives are then consolidated from code into weights, so they run fluently and respond quickly the next time. Together these describe a robot that acts on its own initiative, the basis of deployment and recursive self-improvement, and we offer each proposal as a hypothesis to be tested.

</details>


### [92] [RoboColosseum: Evaluating Physical Intelligence at Scale In-The-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02497)
*Tan Sang Nguyen,Yi Ru Wang,Shailesh Shailesh,Gang Yang,Jiamo Hu,Jianuo Jiang,Haoquan Fang,Zhongzheng Ren,Zhongyi Luo,Ian Chong,Aaron Tung,Santiago Pravisani,Minghui Lin,Yao Mo,Ishneet Singh,Benjamin Tee,Harold Soh,Siddhartha Srinivasa,Dieter Fox,David Hsu,Haoming Song,Maxime Ellerbach,Thomas Wolf,Pepijn Kooijmans,Ruijie He,Michael Cho,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboColosseum 分布式真实机器人评测框架，通过统一策略服务与标准化协议，在多样本地环境中系统评估 VLA 策略与前沿 AI 智能体的泛化、适配与部分进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略需跨机器人、任务和环境工作，但真实物理试验中的环境与具身差异虽提供信息，也导致实验难以复现和公平比较。

Method: 构建跨 124 个站点、本地运行机器人的分布式评测网络，统一策略服务与协议；初始研究覆盖 5 个 VLA 策略和 3 个前沿 AI 智能体、4 种具身（SO-101、Franka、Bimanual-YAM、G1 Humanoid）。Open Track 在匹配本地条件下进行盲测成对比较且不做任务特定适配；Fine-tune Track 对 20 个任务在四种具身上以每任务相同 100 条示范微调；并分析部分进展、排序稳定性与主动匹配采样。

Result: 共完成 5,699 次 Open Track 成对比较。前沿智能体可与专用 VLA 策略匹敌，但所有系统在开放世界任务上可靠完成仍困难。微调后 GR00T N1.7 在 SO-101 领先，pi0.5 在 Franka 与 Bimanual-YAM 领先。跨具身任务套件的策略排序一致性弱（Kendall W=0.24）；Open Track 排序对微调后相对表现预测较差，三个共有具身上均出现显著排名反转。部分进展指标揭示成功率遗漏的差异：G1 Humanoid 上无策略完成五个任务，但平均进展为 13.3%–23.2%。Open Track 排序随试验累积趋于稳定，主动匹配能以显著更少物理试验达到与均匀采样相当的排序一致性。

Conclusion: 分布式真实物理评测能够揭示机器人策略在何处泛化、如何适配新任务，以及即使任务失败时仍取得多少进展；统一协议与高效采样对可复现、可扩展的真实机器人评测至关重要。

Abstract: General-purpose robot policies must work across robots, tasks, and environments, yet the variation that makes physical trials informative also makes them difficult to reproduce. We introduce RoboColosseum, a distributed evaluation framework that combines unified policy serving and standardized protocols across a network of locally operated robots in diverse environments. Our initial study evaluates five vision-language-action (VLA) policies and three frontier AI agents across four embodiments (SO-101, Franka, Bimanual-YAM, and G1 Humanoid). The Open Track compares models without task-specific adaptation in blinded, pairwise trials under matched local conditions. The Fine-tune Track measures how well VLA policies adapt to 20 tasks across all four embodiments when each policy receives the same 100 demonstrations per task. Across 5,699 pairwise Open Track comparisons at 124 sites, frontier agents rival specialized VLA policies, while all systems struggle to complete open-world tasks reliably. After fine-tuning, GR00T N1.7 leads on SO-101 and pi0.5 leads on Franka and Bimanual-YAM. Policy rankings show weak agreement across embodiment-specific task suites (Kendall W = 0.24). Open Track rankings poorly predict relative performance after fine-tuning, with substantial rank reversals on all three embodiments shared between tracks. Measuring partial progress reveals differences that task success alone misses. On the G1 Humanoid, no policy completes any of the five tasks, yet average progress ranges from 13.3% to 23.2% across policies. Open Track rankings stabilize as trials accumulate, and active matchmaking achieves comparable ranking agreement with substantially fewer physical trials than uniform sampling. These results show how distributed physical evaluation can reveal where robot policies generalize, how well they adapt to new tasks, and how far they progress on tasks they fail to complete.

</details>


### [93] [To Coordinate or Not to Coordinate? Learning to Swim with Eight Soft Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02468)
*Junkai Chen,Dehan Yuan,Yiyuan Zhang,Yu Duan,Fan Shi,Dixia Fan,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究检验八臂欠驱动软体游泳器是否需要显式臂间相位协调，发现仅通过共享节律驱动下学习各臂独立振幅即可实现有效速度与方向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多欠驱动软臂游泳的有效控制通常可能依赖臂间相位耦合，但作者假设八臂游泳器无需预设臂间相位关系，只需在共享节律驱动下独立学习各臂驱动振幅，即可控制游泳速度和方向。

Method: 采用基于近端策略优化（PPO）的三种强化学习控制策略进行对比：(1) 无臂间相位耦合、学习各臂特定驱动振幅；(2) 预设臂间相位耦合、学习驱动振幅；(3) 无显式节律结构的端到端PPO直接学习执行器命令。在200个随机三维速度指令回合中评估，并进行直线游泳和指令方向变化试验。

Result: 无相位耦合控制器在200个回合中有169个满足跟踪 criterion，相位耦合控制器为117个，端到端PPO为28个；直线游泳和指令方向变化试验也显示无相位耦合策略具有更准确的速度跟踪。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有效的八臂游泳并不需要预设臂间相位耦合，而可通过节律控制结构内的学习型臂特异性振幅调制实现。

Abstract: Does effective swimming with multiple underactuated soft arms require explicit coordination between arms? We hypothesize that an eight-arm swimmer can control its swimming speed and direction by independently learning each arm's actuation amplitude under a shared rhythmic drive, without prescribed inter-arm phase coupling. To test this hypothesis, we compare three reinforcement learning-based control strategies using proximal policy optimization (PPO): (1) learning arm-specific actuation amplitudes without inter-arm phase coupling, (2) learning actuation amplitudes with prescribed inter-arm phase coupling, and (3) directly learning actuator commands through end-to-end PPO without an explicit rhythmic structure. Across 200 episodes with randomized three-dimensional velocity commands, the controller without phase coupling satisfies the tracking criterion in 169 episodes, compared with 117 for the phase-coupled controller and 28 for end-to-end PPO. Straight-line swimming and commanded direction-change trials further demonstrate more accurate velocity tracking without phase coupling. These findings suggest that effective eight-arm swimming does not require prescribed inter-arm phase coupling, but can instead be achieved through learned arm-specific amplitude modulation within a rhythmic control structure.

</details>


### [94] [Learning Purposeful Co-designs of Bio-inspired Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02462)
*Fabio Boekel,Jiatao Ding,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dogger 模块化仿生四足机器人及两阶段形态-控制协同设计框架，在真实硬件上实现任务驱动的形态优化与零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人常将形态与控制分开设计，效率与适应性受限；已有协同设计方法受限于狭窄设计空间，难以充分发挥任务驱动形态-控制协同优化的潜力。

Method: 设计 Dogger：三段式模块化腿、四杆机构和可调被动膝关节柔顺性。采用两阶段框架：先用强化学习训练跨设计条件的形态条件控制策略，再在任务特定设计选择中固定策略，并用贝叶斯优化搜索形态与柔顺性参数。

Result: 零样本部署所得设计与策略在巡检和负载搬运任务中分别降低运输成本 18.6% 和 35.7%；完整几何-柔顺性协同设计优于固定或受限设计范围；跨任务测试显示不同任务具有特定形态，但共享机械权衡。

Conclusion: 硬件级证据表明，扩展物理设计空间并与学习控制协同优化，是实现腿部机器人任务驱动形态-控制协同设计潜力的关键。

Abstract: Treating morphology and control as separate design problems has constrained the efficiency and adaptability of quadrupedal robots, while current co-design approaches are often constrained by restricted design spaces. We overcome this limit by introducing Dogger, a bio-inspired quadruped with modular three-segment legs, four-bar linkages and adjustable passive knee compliance. A two-stage framework combining reinforcement learning and Bayesian optimization is developed: the morphology-conditioned control policy is trained across designs and then held fixed during task-specific design selection. The resulting designs and policies deployed zero-shot reduce the cost of transport by 18.6% and 35.7% for inspection and payload-carrying tasks. Comparisons demonstrate that full geometry-compliance co-design outperforms fixed and restricted design scopes. Cross-task tests further reveal distinct task-specific morphologies with shared mechanical trade-offs. These results provide hardware-level evidence that expanding the physical design space alongside learned control is essential to realize the full potential of task-driven morphology-control co-design in legged robotics.

</details>


### [95] [Late Autumn: LLM Agents Specialize a General Multi-Robot Planner for One Warehouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02391)
*Yimin Tang,Han Zhang,Shao-Hung Chan,Erdem Bıyık,Sven Koenig,Jingkai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“Late Autumn”，让LLM编码智能体通过自主研究，将通用多机器人规划器针对单一仓库场景进行特化，以吞吐量为目标换取通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实仓库布局与流程相对固定，但运营方通常仍在多个站点共用同一个通用多机器人规划器，专家难以为每个站点单独调参；作者希望低成本地让规划器适配具体站点偏差。

Method: 在结合任务分配与多智能体路径规划（MAPF）的终身规划器上，针对模拟现有自动化仓库的地面场景开展六轮LLM编码智能体自主研究 campaign，让其生成、评分并迭代补丁，类似对预训练LLM做后训练特化。

Result: 吞吐量提升22%–60%，且在未见随机种子上保持；智能体重新发现走廊出口优先级、局部引导、rollout等已知MAPF机制，不同campaign定位同一瓶颈；增益可迁移到另一布局、更大车队和其他服务时间，在更小车队下缩小，在出库偏重需求组合下基本消失；最终补丁为0.7k–2.4k行、由带评分提交链构成、中位步长27行，可专家审查，且审查发现评分未覆盖的问题，部分补丁使规划器计算量增加超过100倍。

Conclusion: LLM智能体能以较低工程成本将站点偏差转化为吞吐增益，并产出可审查补丁；未来专家角色可能转向设计有洞察力的指标和贴近真实问题的快速仿真器。

Abstract: No algorithm is best on all problems, but a real warehouse is a narrow problem: its layout and processes rarely change. Operators still run one general multi-robot planner at many sites, because experts cannot tune it for each one. In this work, which we call Late Autumn, we explore whether LLM coding agents running autonomous research can specialize a general planner for one site, trading generality for throughput, much as post-training adapts a pretrained LLM to one task. We study six campaigns on a lifelong planner that combines task assignment with multi-agent path finding, on a floor modeled on an existing automated warehouse. We find that (1) the agents raise throughput by 22--60%, and the gains hold on unseen seeds; (2) they rediscover known MAPF mechanisms, such as corridor-exit priorities, local guidance, and rollouts, and independent campaigns find the same bottleneck; (3) the gains largely carry over to another layout, a larger fleet, and other service times, shrink with a smaller fleet, and mostly vanish under an outbound-heavy demand mix; and (4) each final patch (0.7k--2.4k lines) is a chain of scored commits with a median step of 27 lines, so experts can review it, and review finds problems that the score misses; some patches multiply planner compute by over 100x. LLM agents can therefore turn each site's bias into extra throughput at little engineering cost, with reviewable patches; we expect experts to shift toward designing insightful metrics and fast simulators that mirror the real problem.

</details>


### [96] [When to Probe and When to Act: Clarity-Aware Risk-Adaptive Control for Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02240)
*Youran Wang,Devodita Chakravarty,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一信念空间MPC框架，将多步信息规划与清晰度自适应CVaR屏障耦合，使自动驾驶在交互交通中根据不确定性动态调节安全保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互交通中需推理不可观测的人类特征与多智能体互惠交互；传统预测与规划解耦易导致“冻结机器人问题”或高模糊度下不安全行为，因为风险评价与信息获取被视为独立目标。作者主张安全不应静态，而应与系统内部置信度动态协同演化。

Method: 将“清晰度”这一标量信息状态作为显式规划变量，构建统一信念空间模型预测控制，联合设计主动探测策略与安全规范；采用清晰度自适应条件风险价值（CVaR）屏障机制，在高模糊度下收紧保守性、置信度提升后放松约束，并给出信息寻求成本及相对于知晓真实人类特征控制器的期望规划成本差距受残余模糊度与求解器误差界定的性能分析。

Result: 在汇入车道与无信号交叉口场景中与代表性交互规划基线对比，5,000次仿真中方法取得最高成功率（94.6%与93.2%）和最低碰撞率（1.2%与1.4%）。

Conclusion: 该框架为以人为中心的交互环境提供了原则性的鲁棒决策基础，表明安全约束可与信息获取和置信度演化统一协同。

Abstract: Safe navigation in interactive traffic requires autonomous vehicles to reason about unobservable human characteristics and the reciprocal nature of multi-agent interactions. Traditional architectures often decouple prediction from planning, leading to the "Frozen Robot Problem" or unsafe maneuvers under high ambiguity because they treat risk assessment and information acquisition as independent objectives. We argue that the notion of safety should not remain static but must instead dynamically co-evolve with the system's internal confidence: the robot should actively clarify ambiguity when necessary while also recognizing the diminishing returns of information, and naturally transition toward task execution. We introduce a unified belief-space Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that couples multistep information planning with a clarity-adaptive Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) barrier mechanism. By treating clarity, a scalar informational state, as an explicit planning variable, the controller co-designs its probing strategy with its safety specification, tightening conservatism under high ambiguity and relaxing constraints as confidence improves. We further provide a performance analysis showing that the cost of information seeking and the expected planning-cost gap to a controller that knows the true human characteristic are bounded by residual ambiguity and solver error. We evaluate the framework in lane-merging and unsignalized-intersection scenarios against representative interactive-planning baselines. Across 5,000 simulations, the proposed method achieves the highest success rates (94.6% and 93.2%) and the lowest collision rates (1.2% and 1.4%) among the compared methods, providing a principled foundation for robust decision-making in human-centric environments.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Ensemble Disagreement: Predictive STL Monitoring of RL Locomotion under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02218)
*Qurban Ali,Badaruddin Chachar,Maqsood Ali,Sajjad Ali Rajper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估闭环世界模型预测式STL监控在RL运动策略分布偏移下的安全性排序能力，发现预测鲁棒性有STL特定排序价值，但相较当前状态鲁棒性增益有限，且集成分歧不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL运动策略在名义仿真训练后可能遭遇分布偏移并危及安全；预测式监控可提前预警，但世界模型预测在偏移下可能失效，且集成模型分歧未校准，需验证其可靠性。

Method: 对冻结的Ant-v5与HalfCheetah-v5策略，采用闭环世界模型rollout和三模型集成，评估预测式STL监控；在外部力、观测噪声和质量偏移下，比较预测鲁棒性、当前状态鲁棒性、分歧与观测预测误差/违规的关系，并测试用分歧惩罚预测鲁棒性。

Result: HalfCheetah中预测鲁棒性对未来STL违规排序较强，但分歧不能可靠跟踪观测预测误差或违规；严重噪声下违规常见（K=20、3个PPO种子为64.2±24.4%），当前状态鲁棒性仅比预测鲁棒性低0.03 AUROC；分歧惩罚未稳定提升排序。Ant提供逐规范互补证据，但复合目标无正违规，any-violation目标在多数种子-条件切片中为单类。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性可提供有用的STL特定排序，但相较当前状态未证明有显著额外排序价值；不应将集成分歧视为安全不确定性的可靠指标。

Abstract: RL locomotion policies trained under nominal simulation conditions may encounter distribution shifts that compromise safety. Predictive monitors could anticipate violations, but their world-model forecasts may become unreliable under shift, and ensemble disagreement is not calibrated. We evaluate predictive Signal Temporal Logic (STL) monitoring for frozen Ant-v5 and HalfCheetah-v5 policies using closed-loop world-model rollouts and a three-model ensemble. Across external-force, observation-noise, and mass shifts, predicted robustness ranks HalfCheetah's future STL violations strongly, whereas disagreement does not reliably track observation prediction error or violations. Under severe noise, violations remain common ($64.2\pm24.4\%$ at $K{=}20$ across three PPO seeds), yet current-state robustness without prediction comes within $0.03$ AUROC of predictive robustness. Penalizing predicted robustness by disagreement does not consistently improve ranking. Ant provides complementary per-specification evidence, but its composite target has no positive violations and its any-violation target is single-class in most evaluated seed-condition slices. Overall, predictive robustness provides useful STL-specific ranking but little demonstrated additional ranking value over the current state; these findings caution against treating ensemble disagreement as a reliable indicator of safety uncertainty.

</details>


### [98] [Directional Bounded-Confidence Matrix Alignment in Vicsek Flocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02189)
*Hossein B. Jond*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种方向性有界置信度的离散时间Vicsek多智能体模型：用状态依赖半正定矩阵在瞬时视线坐标系中分别门控径向与切向航向对齐，并给出适定性、局部收缩、接触保持等理论及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Vicsek平均或标量置信规则难以按视线方向选择性地过滤不一致邻居，矩阵加权更新也可能面临退化与非适定问题；需要在保持度量接触和避免重合的同时实现分量选择性对齐，并刻画结构性限制。

Method: 构建归一化矩阵加权动力学；证明候选更新向量具有一致非消失下界，从而保证任意网络状态下的全局适定性；在完全度量交互和小扩散条件下证明航向直径线性几何收缩，并给出可离线验证的空间自举条件以确保度量接触保持和重合避免；构造连通矩阵图上的极化不变态反例。

Result: 理论上获得全局适定性、局部线性收缩、接触保持与重合避免的充分条件，并揭示连通矩阵图上仍可能存在不变极化态的结构极限；数值上相对无过滤Vicsek、标量绑定置信、迹匹配标量基线和角度限制规则，展示无被拒通道泄漏的分量选择性过滤，并在多种群密度、多随机种子基准上改善固定界对齐。

Conclusion: 该方向性有界置信度矩阵加权Vicsek框架在理论上可保证良好动力学与局部收敛/接触性质，在实验中实现分量选择性对齐并优于多种基线，但连通网络上的极化不变态表明方法存在系统性结构限制。

Abstract: This paper proposes a directional bounded-confidence variant of the discrete-time Vicsek multiagent model, in which pairwise heading alignment is modulated by a state-dependent, positive semidefinite matrix that independently gates orthogonal radial and tangential components in the instantaneous line-of-sight frame. We analyze the normalized, matrix-weighted system dynamics, proving a uniform non-vanishing bound on proposal vectors that guarantees global well-posedness across arbitrary network states. For local small-spread states under complete metric interaction, we prove linear geometric contraction of the heading diameter and establish an offline-verifiable spatial bootstrap condition that guarantees metric contact preservation and coincidence avoidance a priori. A counterexample demonstrates invariant polarized states on connected matrix graphs, delineating systemic structural limits. Numerical experiments against unfiltered Vicsek averaging, scalar tied confidence, trace-matched scalar baselines, and restricted-angle rules demonstrate component-selective filtering without rejected-channel leakage, along with improved fixed-bound alignment on multi-seed benchmarks across varying population densities.

</details>


### [99] [Human Sensorimotor Model Adaptation to Account for Robot Accuracy and Speed Augmentation in Human-Robot Co-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02181)
*Frits van Hall,Kevin Haninger,Luka Peternel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种面向人机协同操作的机器人轨迹规划方法：将任务约束作为最终状态约束、Fitts定律作为时间约束构建随机最优控制问题，并用递归最小二乘在线估计Fitts参数，使机器人运动时长自适应人类行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协同操作中，机器人轨迹应适应人类的感觉运动行为，而人类行为受任务时空约束和个人因素影响。现有嵌入人类感觉运动模型的规划方法未考虑适应人类交互行为的时间约束，难以更好对齐人机协同运动。

Method: 将任务约束转化为随机最优控制问题的最终状态约束，并依据Fitts定律引入时间约束；采用递归最小二乘法估计Fitts定律参数，使机器人运动持续时间适应人类。方法在定位任务中验证：机器人控制具有可重复运动的自由度，人类在互补自由度上调整运动。

Result: 该方法计算上适合在线使用；随着人与机器人相互适应，方法能够收敛；并可提高运动速度。但摘要指出仍需开展充分的人类受试者研究。

Conclusion: 所提方法在定位任务中验证了可行性，能够在线适配人类运动时长并提升协同运动速度，但其实际人机协同效果仍需未来系统的人因实验验证。

Abstract: In human-robot co-manipulation, the robot's trajectory should accommodate the human's, where human sensorimotor behavior is affected by the task's spatial and temporal constraints, as well as personal factors. Several robot trajectory planning methods have been proposed to embed human sensorimotor models in the robot trajectory planning, but these methods do not consider temporal constraints that adapt to how the human behaves when interacting with the robot. To better align human and robot collaborative motion, this work adapts a stochastic optimal control problem with a final state constraint from the task constraints and a temporal constraint from Fitts' law. A recursive least squares method is used to estimate the Fitts' law parameters, adapting the robot's motion duration to that of the human, thus allowing the robot to adapt to how the human behaves when interacting with the robot. It is hypothesized that a speed increase can result from the robot augmenting the accuracy. The feasibility of the approach is validated on a positioning task where the robot controls degrees of freedom which have repeatable motion, and the human adapts motion in the complementary degrees of freedom. The approach is shown to be computationally suited to online use, it converges as the human and robot mutually adapt, and can increase the movement speed, although thorough human studies are needed in future work.

</details>


### [100] [Directional Bounded-Confidence Cucker-Smale Flocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02173)
*Hossein B. Jond*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Cucker--Smale群集模型的方向性有界置信扩展：在瞬时视距坐标系中将相对速度分解为径向与切向通道，并用紧支撑核分别门控，得到严格推广经典标量CS对齐的时变、状态依赖矩阵加权交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CS模型采用各向同性标量对齐，传统tied-confidence有界置信律在部分场景下仍会导致极化；需要一种能刻画方向性交互并保持可分析性的群集模型。

Method: 在瞬时视线框架下将成对相对速度分解为独立径向和切向分量，通过紧支撑核门控构造矩阵加权交互；在 disagreement 子空间上建立滑窗持续激励（PE）条件，并推导精确能量耗散恒等式与均值速度守恒。

Result: 证明了指数速度共识；给出分离度量衰减与聚合方向强制性的CS有界直径判据及聚类收敛保证；仿真验证了精确耗散平衡，并显示方向门控能在传统各向同性tied-confidence仍极化的场景中恢复全局对齐。

Conclusion: 方向性有界置信为CS群集提供了严格泛化且可分析的框架，在各向异性门控交互下可实现速度共识、有界直径与聚类行为，并优于传统各向同性有界置信机制。

Abstract: This paper introduces a directional bounded-confidence extension of the Cucker--Smale (CS) flocking model that decomposes pairwise relative velocities into independent radial and tangential channels within the instantaneous line-of-sight frame. Gating these components via compactly supported kernels creates a time-varying, state-dependent, matrix-weighted interaction that strictly generalizes classical scalar CS alignment. We establish an exact energy-dissipation identity with conserved mean velocity and prove exponential velocity consensus under a sliding-window persistent excitation (PE) condition formulated directly on the disagreement subspace. Furthermore, we derive a CS bounded-diameter criterion separating metric decay from aggregate directional coercivity, along with a cluster convergence guarantee. Simulations validate the exact dissipation balance and demonstrate that directional gating successfully restores global alignment in scenarios where traditional isotropic tied-confidence laws remain polarized.

</details>


### [101] [GoalDex: Learning Image-Goal-Conditioned Policies for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02171)
*Arjun Bhardwaj,Mayank Mittal,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Vaishakh Patil,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种不依赖显式物体位姿估计的端到端视觉运动框架，用于灵巧手内物体重定向，并支持以图像目标指定期望物体构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手内操作多依赖显式物体位姿估计，但在严重手-物遮挡下位姿跟踪不可靠，且常需物体特定监督，形成感知与控制之间的瓶颈，限制鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Method: 直接以RGB观测和本体感知为输入，联合学习视觉表征与控制策略；通过从特权状态教师进行行为蒸馏并引入辅助重建目标来训练；进一步提出图像目标条件化的重定向，用图像而非规范位姿表示指定目标构型。

Result: 在仿真中覆盖多种物体几何与实例，方法 consistently 优于基于位姿的方法和基于预训练视觉编码器的策略；并将单一图像目标条件化策略直接迁移到真实世界五个物体，实现灵巧重定向。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无需显式位姿估计也可实现准确的灵巧手内重定向，并具备跨物体与sim-to-real迁移潜力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation is commonly formulated around explicit object pose estimation, yet reliable pose tracking is challenging under severe hand-object occlusions and often requires object-specific supervision. This dependence creates a bottleneck between perception and control that can limit robustness and scalability. We present an end-to-end visuomotor framework for dexterous in-hand reorientation that operates on RGB observations and proprioception without intermediate pose estimation. Our approach jointly learns visual representations and control policies through behavior distillation from privileged state teachers and auxiliary reconstruction objectives. Furthermore, we introduce image-goal-conditioned reorientation, allowing desired object configurations to be specified directly through images rather than canonical pose representations. We evaluate our approach across a diverse set of object geometries and instances in simulation, where it consistently outperforms pose-based methods and policies built on pretrained visual encoders. Finally, we demonstrate direct sim-to-real transfer of a single image-goal-conditioned policy across five real-world objects, showing that accurate dexterous reorientation can be achieved without explicit pose estimation. For videos and code release, please refer to the project website: this https URL

</details>


### [102] [History-Aware Force Sensing and Tunable Stiffness in a Pneumatic Kresling Origami Cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02140)
*Hengyi Zhou,Jiaqi Wang,Yilin Ma,Dezhong Tong,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成磁性角度传感的3D打印PEBA Kresling单元，结合多尺度GRU与能量补偿，实现软体折纸系统在零/正/负表压下的接触力估计、可调刚度和自感知驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人中机械滞回和气动加载使基于形变测量的接触力估计变得困难，需要一种紧凑方案同时实现力反馈、可调轴向刚度和自感知驱动。

Method: 设计3D打印PEBA Kresling单元并集成磁性角度传感，支持零表压力传感、正表压可调刚度力传感和负表压自感知驱动三种模式；利用多尺度门控循环单元（GRU）从角度历史估计接触力，以兼顾几何变形与材料响应；负表压下采用基于能量的补偿模型。

Result: 零表压测试RMSE为0.76 N；+20 kPa正表压使前3 mm压缩的平均轴向加载刚度约翻倍，压力专用模型RMSE低于0.93 N；负表压下利用零表压训练模型并结合能量补偿，在准静态条件下RMSE为1.66--2.10 N。

Conclusion: 结果表明该紧凑方法可在软体折纸系统中统一实现力反馈、可调轴向刚度和自感知驱动。

Abstract: Mechanical hysteresis and pneumatic loading complicate contact force estimation from deformation measurements in soft robots. This work presents a 3D-printed polyether block amide (PEBA) Kresling cell with magnetic angular sensing that supports three operating modes: force sensing at zero gauge pressure, force sensing with tunable stiffness under gauge positive pressure, and self-sensing actuation under negative gauge pressure. A multiscale gated recurrent unit (GRU) network estimates contact force from angular histories to account for the the combined effects of geometric deformation and material response. At zero gauge pressure, the model achieves a test RMSE of 0.76~N. Positive gauge pressure approximately doubles the average axial loading stiffness at +20~kPa over the first 3~mm of compression, while pressure-specific models maintain RMSEs below 0.93~N. Under negative gauge pressure, an energy-based compensation model enables contact force estimation using the the model trained at zero gauge pressure, achieving RMSEs of 1.66--2.10~N under quasi-static conditions. These results demonstrate a compact approach to combining force feedback, tunable axial stiffness, and self-sensing actuation in soft origami systems.

</details>


### [103] [When Delay Meets Drift: Physical Safety of Robot Foundation Models Is a Cross-Layer Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02101)
*Liangkai Liu,Bimal Thapa Magar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出跨层变异传播框架，主张机器人基础模型的物理安全来自环境、模型、计算平台与执行在闭环中的交互，并用pi0.5/LIBERO实验量化推理延迟与视角偏移的超加性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估通常一次只改变一个因素：鲁棒性基准扰动场景但假设推理瞬时完成，实时执行方法研究推理延迟但局限于名义场景，因而无法刻画闭环系统中多因素联合变化对物理安全的影响。

Method: 将物理环境、模型、计算平台与执行视为系统层级，形式化跨层变异传播，并采用交互对比度量联合变异是否产生超过各因素独立效应之和的风险；在LIBERO上对pi0.5视觉-语言-动作模型进行4000对闭环回合实验，考察400 ms推理延迟与60度相机视角偏移的交互。

Result: 400 ms推理延迟在训练视角下使安全违规率提高3.5个百分点，但在60度相机偏移下提高37个百分点；联合效应比两个独立效应相加多33.5个百分点（95% CI：25–43），显示出显著的超加性风险。

Conclusion: 物理安全不能仅归因于模型本身，而需预测变异何时转化为风险，并据此自适应调整计算、功耗与控制，迈向可预测的物理智能。

Abstract: Robot foundation models now act in a closed loop, yet their safety is evaluated one variation at a time: robustness benchmarks perturb the scene while assuming instantaneous inference, and real-time execution methods study inference delay in nominal scenes. We argue that physical safety is not a property of the model alone, but emerges from how variations in the physical environment, the model, the computing platform, and execution interact in closed loop. We formalize this as cross-layer variation propagation across these system layers and use an interaction contrast to measure when joint variation creates more risk than the sum of its parts. In 4,000 paired closed-loop episodes of the pi0.5 vision-language-action model on LIBERO, a 400 ms inference delay raises the safety-violation rate by 3.5 percentage points under the training viewpoint but by 37 percentage points under a 60-degree camera shift: 33.5 percentage points (95% CI: 25-43) more than the two effects combined. We close with a research agenda toward predictable physical intelligence: predicting when variation becomes risk, and adapting compute, power, and control accordingly.

</details>


### [104] [Local Solution Repair for Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02090)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出局部解修复方法 LSR，用于多机器人仓储拣取-配送任务规划，在运行时检测并局部优化次优行为，从而提升长期大规模部署中的任务吞吐并减少冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MAPD 方法虽能生成全局可行计划，但局部效率低，机器人回退、拥堵空闲等次优行为在长时大规模部署中累积，导致性能下降。

Method: LSR 是一种目标重规划方法：运行时检测次优行为，对现有计划进行局部修复以减少智能体间冲突；并将其应用于最先进 MAPD 基线 M2M 与 LNS-PBS 生成的解。

Result: 在三类环境的长时仓储仿真中，LSR 修复 M2M 和 LNS-PBS 的解，任务吞吐相较当前最先进方法提升最高 5.9%；通过减少约 3.6 万个次优实例（或降低 14.6%），且仅增加少量计算时间。

Conclusion: LSR 能有效改善现有 MAPD 方案在长时大规模仓储场景中的局部效率与整体吞吐，是一种轻量且有效的计划修复策略。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must efficiently attend to a constant stream of pickup-and-delivery tasks while avoiding collisions. Existing Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) solutions generate plans that are globally feasible but often locally inefficient. In large-scale, long-horizon deployments, these inefficiencies, such as robots backtracking from target goal locations and idling in congested areas, accumulate over time and degrade performance. This paper contributes Local Solution Repair (LSR), a goal replanning approach that detects suboptimal behaviors at run-time and locally improves plans to reduce conflict between agents. We demonstrate the utility of our approach by repairing solutions generated by state-of-the-art MAPD baselines, M2M and LNS-PBS. Our results in long-horizon warehouse simulations over three environments show upwards of 5.9% improvement in task throughput over current state-of-the-art methods by reducing the number of suboptimal instances by ~36k or by 14.6% with minor additional computation time.

</details>


### [105] [Back to the Future: Predicting Long-Horizon Dynamics with Retrospective Correction for Disturbance-Aware Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02073)
*Jiwoong Choi,Haoyu Li,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DARE 是一个几何感知的关系动力学框架，通过回溯式校正提升长时程扰动预测，并用于密集堆叠环境中的扰动感知物体取回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在狭窄空间中取回物体时，接触会扰动周围物体，且扰动可能沿接触链传播；同时学习到的动力学在长时程规划中会累积误差，导致预测不可靠。

Method: DARE 使用紧凑物体描述符，通过物体级消息传递预测动作条件驱动的扰动传播；当获得稀疏观测时，学习到的校正器利用预测偏差修正早期预测状态，并重跑其间动力学，将校正沿预测接触链传播；预测结果用于滚动时域规划器，在取回进度与周围物体位移和旋转之间权衡，允许必要接触但抑制有害扰动。

Result: 在平面环境和 RoboCasa 环境中评估了长时程预测、泛化性和密集杂物中扰动感知取回的鲁棒性；摘要未给出具体定量结果。

Conclusion: DARE 通过几何感知的关系动力学、回溯式校正和扰动约束规划，有望实现密集堆叠场景中兼顾取回效率与周围物体扰动控制的长时程物体取回。

Abstract: Retrieving objects from densely packed storage requires anticipating how contact disturbs surrounding objects when limited clearance prevents lifting the target over clutter. However, contact can propagate beyond the target's immediate neighbors, while accumulated prediction errors make learned dynamics unreliable over long planning horizons. We introduce DARE (Disturbance-Aware REtrieval), a geometry-aware relational dynamics framework that uses retrospective correction to improve long-horizon prediction for disturbance-aware retrieval. DARE encodes compact object descriptors and predicts action-conditioned disturbance propagation through object-level message passing. When a sparse observation becomes available, a learned corrector uses the prediction discrepancy to amend an earlier predicted state and reruns the intervening dynamics, propagating the correction through the predicted contact chain. These predictions guide a receding-horizon planner that balances retrieval progress against displacement and rotation of surrounding objects, permitting necessary contact while discouraging harmful disturbance. Experiments in planar and RoboCasa environments evaluate long-horizon prediction, generalization, and robustness for disturbance-aware retrieval in dense clutter. See our project website: this https URL

</details>


### [106] [Do Embodied VLMs Actually Use Their Scene-Graph Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02056)
*Shubh Garg,Divyansh Pandey,Vedant Khandelwal,Niyati Rawal,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种“图交换”诊断测试，用于判断具身问答中的VLM是否真正依赖场景图记忆，并比较文本与图结构两种记忆输入方式的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体回答路线问题时需要记忆所见内容，场景图是自然表示；但仅看准确率无法确认场景图是否包含情节事实、是否传入模型以及模型是否真正依赖它。

Method: 构建图交换测试：将每个情节的场景图替换为另一情节的图，统计答案变化数量；在包含2至7个房间路线的具身问答基准上，对微调VLM比较文本输入、图编码器输入及逐节点token输入等场景图记忆传递方式。

Result: 文本形式记忆被强烈使用，路线问题提升30分；图形式未被有效使用，原因是编码器虽保留信息，但标准池化与后期融合丢弃了图信息；逐节点独立输入token可恢复部分信息，路线问题提升8.3分，且交换测试确认该提升来自图。测试还揭示准确率掩盖的捷径：当相同事实出现在文本中时，模型会忽略其图。

Conclusion: 图交换测试可低成本检验VLM是否使用场景图记忆；分析表明场景图记忆应以更细粒度、逐节点方式传递给模型，以避免融合机制丢弃信息并暴露文本捷径问题。

Abstract: Embodied agents that answer questions about their route need a memory of what they saw, and scene graphs are a natural form for it. A vision-language model (VLM) can receive such a graph as text or through a graph encoder, but accuracy alone cannot show whether the graph holds the episode's facts, reaches the model, or is actually relied on. In this diagnostic study, we introduce a graph-swap test for the last of these: each episode is given another episode's graph, and we count how many answers change. On an embodied question-answering benchmark with routes through two to seven rooms, we use it to compare ways of delivering scene-graph memory to a fine-tuned VLM. As text, the memory is used strongly, adding 30 points on route questions. As a graph, it is not, and we trace why: the encoder preserves the information, but standard pooled and late fusion discard it. Giving each node its own input token recovers part of it, a gain of 8.3 points on route questions that the swap test confirms comes from the graph. The test also exposes a shortcut that accuracy hides: when the same facts appear in the text, the model ignores its graph. The test thus offers a cheap check of whether a VLM uses its scene-graph memory, and our analysis points to how that memory should be delivered.

</details>


### [107] [Answering Without Arriving: An Evidence Audit for Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01958)
*Shubh Garg,Divyansh Pandey,Niyati Rawal,Vedant Khandelwal,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多房间具身问答基准与固定问题、仅改变证据来源的评测阶梯，揭示闭环视觉语言智能体可在未到达目标时靠文本/选项答对，且证据可达不等于被使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅看答案准确率无法判断智能体是否真的导航、观察并依据所见证据回答；答案可能来自问题、房间标签或选项，正确并不证明支持性观测发生过。因此需要可追溯到观测证据，并能区分证据获取与证据使用的评测。

Method: 构建含200个测试回合、800个问题的多房间基准，按路线范围分层；问题由房间、可见物体和帧的符号记录生成，使每个答案可追溯到支持观测。评测阶梯保持问题不变，仅改变提供给回答者的证据：纯文本探针、闭环智能体、参考轨迹回放，并进行问题家族级审计与oracle条件分析。

Result: 闭环视觉语言智能体在200个回合中零目标到达却答对45.5%，与同一模型无图像输入相差0.1个百分点；回放参考轨迹使同一模型升至57.4%，说明证据沿路线存在但未被采集。八类问题家族中四类主要可由文本回答，一类引用无法支持自身答案的帧；oracle条件在Rooms-visited上通过计数供给图像达到59.5%。

Conclusion: 在将准确率提升归因于记忆之前，必须先验证证据获取、答案覆盖与捷径敏感性；访问证据并不等同于使用证据。

Abstract: Embodied question answering asks an agent to navigate, observe, and then answer questions about what it saw, yet answer accuracy alone cannot tell whether any of this happened. A plausible answer can come from the question, the room label, or the answer choices, and a correct answer does not show that the supporting observation was ever made. We present a multi-room benchmark whose 200 test episodes and 800 questions are stratified by route extent, with an evaluation ladder that holds the questions fixed while varying only the evidence supplied to the answerer, from text-only probes through closed-loop agents to replay of the reference trajectory. Questions are generated from symbolic records of rooms, visible objects, and frames, so every answer can be traced to the observations that support it. A closed-loop vision-language agent reaches no target in 200 episodes yet answers 45.5% of questions, within 0.1 points of the same model given no images; replaying the reference trajectory raises the same model to 57.4%, so the evidence exists along the route but is not gathered. A family-level audit then shows that access to evidence is not the same as use of it. Four of eight families are largely answerable from text, one cites frames that cannot establish its own answer, and the oracle conditions reach 59.5% on Rooms-visited by counting the supplied images. Evidence acquisition, answer coverage, and shortcut sensitivity must therefore be established before an accuracy gain can be credited to memory.

</details>


### [108] [OpenNWM: A Generalist Navigation World Model with Latent Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01844)
*Dujun Nie,Ruiqi Song,Zixuan Wang,Hanqing Wang,Yifan Han,Baiyong Ding,Wenzhao Zheng,Hangbin Wu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OpenNWM：通过潜在动作预训练利用无动作导航视频学习通用导航世界模型，并用动作条件后训练对齐真实运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导航世界模型依赖带动作标签的数据，真实环境覆盖不足；无动作导航视频多样但缺少动作标注，难以直接用于动作条件训练。

Method: 从大规模无动作视频中通过视觉预测预训练学习通用潜在动作，再通过动作条件后训练将潜在动作与物理运动对齐；构建NavAnywhere数据集，含15个异构来源、1750万视觉观测，覆盖地面机器人、人类、无人机及五类场景。

Result: 在域内和域外基准上视觉预测均有提升，导航性能也得到改善。

Conclusion: 潜在动作预训练可将多样无动作视频转化为动力学学习与泛化能力，提升开放世界导航表现。

Abstract: Navigation world models predict future observations conditioned on navigation actions to support planning, but their generalization across real-world environments remains constrained by the limited coverage of action-labeled data. Action-free navigation videos provide diverse visual experience, yet lack the recorded actions required for action-conditioned training. We introduce OpenNWM, a generalist navigation world model with latent action pretraining. It learns universal latent actions from large-scale action-free videos through visual prediction pretraining, and further aligns them with physical motions via action-conditioned post-training. To support video pretraining, we curate NavAnywhere, a large-scale action-free navigation video dataset comprising 17.5 million visual observations from 15 heterogeneous sources. It captures navigation by ground robots, humans, and drones, with diverse viewpoints and motion patterns across five broad scene types: residential areas, public and commercial spaces, urban settings, parks, and natural landscapes. Experiments demonstrate improved visual prediction on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, together with improved navigation performance. These results show that latent-action pretraining enables diverse, action-free videos to improve dynamics learning and generalization for open-world navigation.

</details>


### [109] [Control Barrier Functions for Safety-Aware Latent-Space Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01814)
*Chandrakiran Ravula,Siddhanth Ramesh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文将潜空间路径规划中的二值碰撞分类器替换为在潜空间直接学习的神经控制屏障函数（CBF），并用闭式一阶校正将目标导向的潜空间步投影回CBF约束，从而提升机械臂避障规划的成功率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LSPP框架依赖二值碰撞分类器进行避障，但其在障碍边界附近梯度饱和、缺乏时间结构，导致优化器停滞且无法提供前向不变性保证。

Method: 在VAE学习到的紧凑可微潜流形上学习神经CBF；训练目标由安全/不安全符号约束和基于障碍盲标称规划器转移的离散时间衰减条件三项组成；规划时对每个目标导向潜步施加闭式一阶校正，使其满足CBF约束。

Result: 在7-DoF Franka Panda上，固定障碍任务成功率为75.6%，比35组调优分类器配置的最佳结果高12.0个百分点；多位置任务成功率为83.8%；衰减率α扫描显示安全—性能权衡的最佳区间为α∈[0.10,0.20]；完全通用的随机障碍设置下成功率降至33.0%，归因于障碍盲训练转移与校正规划器访问状态之间的协变量偏移。

Conclusion: 神经CBF与闭式投影校正能有效替代二值分类器并改善LSPP避障性能，但随机障碍场景仍受训练—部署分布偏移限制，需进一步处理协变量偏移以提升泛化性。

Abstract: Latent-space path planning (LSPP) compresses the configuration space of a robotic manipulator into a compact, differentiable manifold learned by a variational autoencoder, enabling fast gradient-based planning without explicit inverse kinematics. Existing LSPP frameworks enforce obstacle avoidance with a binary collision classifier, whose saturated gradients near obstacle boundaries and lack of temporal structure cause the optimizer to stall and provide no forward-invariance guarantee. We replace the classifier with a neural control barrier function (CBF) learned directly in the latent space. The barrier is trained with a three-term objective that combines safe and unsafe sign constraints with a discrete-time decrease condition evaluated on transitions of an obstacle-blind nominal planner, and it is enforced at planning time by a closed-form, first-order correction that projects each goal-directed latent step back onto the CBF constraint. On a 7-DoF Franka Panda manipulator, the CBF planner achieves a 75.6\% success rate on a fixed-obstacle task, 12.0 percentage points above the best of 35 tuned classifier configurations, and 83.8\% on a multi-position task. A sweep of the decay rate $\alpha$ characterizes the safety--performance trade-off, with the best operating points in $\alpha \in [0.10, 0.20]$. In a fully general random-obstacle setting, success drops to 33.0\%, which we trace to covariate shift between the obstacle-blind training transitions and the states visited by the corrected planner.

</details>


### [110] [CompliantBot: Learning Whole-Body Compliance across Task Interfaces for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01748)
*Haoyang Li,Keqi Zhu,Zhanxiang Cao,Yang Zhang,Yizhi Chen,Xuekang Yang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CompliantBot：一种无需预构建柔顺数据或目标轨迹、保留原始任务输入的人形机器人柔顺训练框架，可同时用于运动跟踪与速度指令行走，并在受力时让位、卸力后恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形控制器需在受外力时柔顺让位，同时保持恢复原任务的能力；但跟踪目标会惩罚让位所需偏差，长期放松跟踪又增加恢复难度。现有方法常依赖任务特定交互指令接口或构造柔顺运动数据/参考轨迹，难以跨任务接口复用柔顺训练机制。

Method: CompliantBot 保留原始任务输入，不依赖预构建柔顺数据或目标轨迹。其核心包括：状态依赖的交互力场将外加载荷与机器人运动耦合，使局部关节运动和全身移动改变所受外力；互补的力幅值奖励鼓励降低交互载荷；加载期间降低任务奖励权重、卸载后恢复，以协调让位与任务恢复。训练出的策略仅使用任务输入和本体感知历史，不显式测量外力。

Result: 在两类任务接口下，CompliantBot 在恒定拉力下让位更远、弹簧交互载荷低于匹配基线，并在撤力后恢复任务跟踪；更强拉力下任务完成率最高；功率评估显示其平均关节机械功率最低；真机演示通过手臂运动、迈步和身体重定向体现柔顺响应。

Conclusion: CompliantBot 提供了一种通用、无需显式外力测量和任务特定柔顺数据的双任务接口柔顺训练机制，在保持任务恢复能力的同时实现更低交互载荷与更低机械功率，并具备真机柔顺行为表现。

Abstract: Humanoid controllers need the ability to yield to external forces while retaining the capacity to resume their original tasks. However, tracking objectives penalize the deviations needed for yielding, while relaxing tracking goals persistently can make task recovery harder. Existing approaches often rely on task-specific interaction command interfaces or constructed compliant motion data and reference trajectories, limiting the direct reuse of their compliance-training mechanisms across different task interfaces. We introduce CompliantBot, a compliance-training framework for motion tracking and velocity-command locomotion. It preserves the original task inputs and requires no preconstructed compliant data or target trajectories. A state-dependent interaction force field couples applied loads to the robot's motion, enabling local articulation and whole-body movement to change the forces experienced. A complementary force-magnitude reward encourages lower interaction loads, while task rewards are downweighted during loading and restored after release to coordinate yielding and task resumption. The trained policies use task inputs and proprioceptive history without explicit external-force measurements. Across both task interfaces, CompliantBot yields farther under constant pulls and experiences lower spring interaction loads than matched baselines, while resuming task tracking after force removal. CompliantBot achieves the highest task completion among the compared methods under stronger pulls. Power evaluations further show that CompliantBot has the lowest mean joint mechanical power among the compared methods. Real-robot demonstrations further illustrate compliant responses through arm motion, stepping, and body reorientation.

</details>


### [111] [ODE Policy: Robot Learning via Action Dynamics, Not Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01729)
*Jindou Jia,Jingliang Li,Kuangji Zuo,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Mengfei Zhao,Bofan Lyu,Jiaqi Bai,Daoyuan Zhu,Hengyu Dai,Junpeng Zhang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ODE policy：用神经 ODE 学习动作的向量场与动力学，而非直接回归离散动作值；部署时由前一动作积分生成下一动作，使轨迹天然连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略在离散控制步上将观测映射为动作值，物理运动的连续性只能被隐式学习，导致平滑性、泛化与多模态行为受限。

Method: 神经 ODE 在 IL 中通过短动作块、在 RL 中通过单控制步匹配积分动作与目标，学习动作向量场；轻量多样性头通过随机化向量场方向捕获多模态行为；执行时逐步积分生成连续轨迹。

Result: 作为视觉运动策略、VLA/WAM 动作头和基于模型 RL 的 actor，在单臂、双臂、灵巧、运动与移动操作任务上与 14 个基线比较。IL 中一 epoch 后相对 flow matching 达 5.9 倍成功率、5.4 倍泛化、3.9 倍模式平衡，动作生成快 10.8 倍，且多数情况比人类示范更平滑；14 个真实任务成功率最高。RL 中加速 SuperMimic 的人形运动跟踪并平滑动作。

Conclusion: ODE policy 在学习、数据、参数和执行效率上具有优势，为机器人学习规模化提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Most robot policies, whether trained by imitation learning (IL) or reinforcement learning (RL), map observations to action values at discrete control steps and leave the continuity of physical motion to be learned implicitly. We propose ODE policy, which learns the dynamics of the action rather than the action itself. A neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) learns the vector field of the action by matching the integrated actions to the targets over short action chunks in IL and single control steps in RL. A lightweight diversity head captures multi-modal behavior by randomizing the direction of the field. At deployment, each action is integrated from the previous one, so the executed trajectory is continuous by construction. We evaluate it as a visuomotor policy, as the action head of vision-language-action (VLA) and world action models (WAMs), and as the actor of model-based RL against 14 baselines on unimanual, bimanual, dexterous, locomotion, and loco-manipulation tasks. In IL, against flow matching, it reaches 5.9$\times$ the success, 5.4$\times$ the generalization, and 3.9$\times$ the mode balance after one epoch, and generates actions 10.8$\times$ faster. It mostly moves more smoothly than human demonstrations. On 14 real-world tasks across single-arm, dual-arm, dexterous, and humanoid robots, it achieves the highest success rate. In RL, it further speeds up SuperMimic for humanoid motion tracking and smooths its actions.
