Efficient in learning, data, parameters, and execution, ODE policy offers a promising path to scaling robot learning.

</details>


### [112] [HuG: Learning Humanoid Whole-Body Affordance-Aware Grasping with Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01659)
*Xuanbin Peng,Lai Wei,Yuze Zhai,Qingyuan Yu,Keyi Wang,Jianglong Ye,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HuG 提出一种面向人形机器人的全身可供性感知抓取框架，通过主动感知与“感知-对齐-抓取”流水线，在仿真中仅用控制器在环的动态筛选数据训练，实现零真实示教的真实世界抓取与空间外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类环境中抓取物体困难：自视角下目标常被部分遮挡，且到达目标需要协调全身运动，在保持目标可见、可达到的同时维持基座稳定。

Method: 提出 HuG 的 Perceive-Align-Grasp 流水线：以信念驱动的主动感知将视角作为显式决策变量以解除遮挡；用点云条件化的可供性策略生成全身抓取；再通过可供性感知对齐将身体重定位到最可执行构型。关键是用控制器在环的动态仿真筛选训练数据，剔除 73% 虽运动学可行但会使部署控制器失稳的抓取，使策略学习运动动力学可行的可供性；全程仿真训练且无真实示教。

Result: 在多层级货架任务上取得 87.5% 真实世界成功率，可泛化到新物体，并在所有测试高度上保持至少 73% 成功率；在大规模仿真中还外推到远低于训练中见过的桌面高度并成功。

Conclusion: 经运动动力学筛选的合成数据使可供性建立在物体几何与全身可行性上，而非记忆化构型，从而支持真实抓取、物体泛化与空间外推。

Abstract: Acquiring objects in human environments is hard for humanoids: targets are partially occluded under egocentric viewpoints, and reaching them demands coordinated whole-body motion that keeps the target visible and reachable while the base stays stable. We present HuG, a framework for whole-body affordance-aware grasping with active perception that couples perception and action through a Perceive-Align-Grasp pipeline: belief-driven active perception resolves occlusion by treating viewpoint as an explicit decision variable; a point-cloud-conditioned affordance policy proposes whole-body grasps; and an affordance-aware alignment stage repositions the body to the most executable configuration. A key insight is that, on a floating base, kinematic validity is not enough: we curate training data with controller-in-the-loop dynamics simulation, rejecting 73% of kinematically valid grasps that would destabilize the deployment controller, so the policy learns kinodynamically feasible affordances. Trained entirely in simulation with zero real demonstrations, HuG attains 87.5% real-world success on a multi-level shelf task, generalizes to novel objects, and holds at least 73% success at every tested height. Beyond generalizing within its training distribution, the policy extrapolates spatially: at scale in simulation it succeeds at table heights well below any seen during training, indicating that kinodynamically filtered synthetic data grounds affordances in object geometry and whole-body feasibility rather than in memorized configurations.

</details>


### [113] [MagPillar: A Soft Magnetopillar Array Tactile Sensor for Robotic Surface Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01609)
*Haotian Guo,Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Jiazhi Tang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受蜘蛛触觉毛启发的磁柱阵列三轴霍尔触觉传感器MagPillar，用于机器人表面材料识别与轮廓感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人表面探索需要能适应接触并捕捉表面特征变化的触觉接口，以实现材料识别和几何轮廓感知。

Method: 设计磁柱阵列与三轴霍尔传感结合的柔性触觉传感器，并建立全阵列机械-磁场模型，解释柱体变形如何影响叠加磁场及接触响应；通过机器人表面感知实验提取频谱特征并用逻辑回归分类材料，同时用连续扫描周期凸起分析几何变化。

Result: 在会话内时间分块交叉验证下，对六种材料表面的离线识别准确率达99.34%；连续扫描周期凸起时，响应幅值和峰间距变化与表面几何变化一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将柔性接触力学与分布式磁传感联系起来，为材料识别和定性表面轮廓感知提供了新路径。

Abstract: Robotic surface exploration benefits from tactile interfaces that accommodate contact and capture variations in surface characteristics. Herein, we report MagPillar, a tactile sensor inspired by spider tactile hairs that combines a magnetic pillar array with three-axis Hall sensing. A full-array mechanical and magnetic-field model characterizes how pillar deformation affects the superposed fields measured across the array, providing a physical basis for interpreting its contact responses. The compliant pillar architecture translates contact with surface textures and geometric features into distinct magnetic response patterns. Then, in robotic surface perception experiments, spectral features and logistic regression distinguish six material surfaces with an offline accuracy of 99.34% under within-session temporally blocked cross-validation. Continuous scans over periodic protrusions produce amplitude and peak-spacing variations consistent with changes in surface geometry. These findings connect compliant contact mechanics with distributed magnetic sensing, paving a new path for material recognition and qualitative surface-profile perception.

</details>


### [114] [SCOUT: Planning under Occlusion via Object-Centric World Model Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01600)
*Amir Hossain Raj,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT 是一个面向杂乱环境中遮挡场景的规划框架，通过物体中心表示重建隐藏几何，并结合 LLM 候选动作生成与 MCTS 世界模型评估，实现从部分视图出发的长期机器人操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱空间中常只能获得部分视图，前景物体会遮挡目标、周围几何和可用自由空间；反应式策略难以处理隐藏约束，而穷举搜索在密集杂乱中计算代价过高。

Method: SCOUT 使用物体中心场景表示重建隐藏几何，构建包含遮挡层级与可通行走廊的关系型规划状态；利用 LLM 提出结构化候选动作，再通过基于学习到的动作条件世界模型的蒙特卡洛树搜索进行严格评估，从而连接高层推理与物理 rollout。

Result: 在 Reveal 和 Placement 任务上，SCOUT 优于视觉-语言-动作策略、具身智能体和 3D 世界模型，并在需要推理遮挡几何的长时程任务中取得显著提升；同时通过杂乱货架环境中的真实机器人操作验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCOUT 表明，将遮挡感知场景表示、LLM 结构化动作提议与 MCTS 世界模型评估结合，可有效应对杂乱遮挡环境中的长时程机器人操作规划。

Abstract: Robots manipulating in cluttered spaces must often plan from partial views where foreground objects occlude the target, surrounding geometry, and usable free space. Reactive policies often miss these hidden constraints, while exhaustive search becomes computationally prohibitive in dense clutter. We present SCOUT, an occlusion-aware planning framework that bridges high-level deliberation with grounded physical rollouts. SCOUT leverages an object-centric scene representation to reconstruct hidden geometry and derive a relational planning state capturing blocker hierarchies and accessibility corridors. To navigate the vast action space in clutter, an LLM proposes structured action candidates which are then rigorously evaluated via Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) using a learned action-conditioned world model. Evaluations on 'Reveal' and 'Placement' tasks show that SCOUT outperforms Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, embodied agents, and 3D world models, with significant gains in long-horizon tasks requiring reasoning over occluded geometry. We further validate the framework's robustness through real-world robotic manipulation in a cluttered shelf environment.

</details>


### [115] [Hi-CoLA: High Confidence Lower Bound Approximation Based Reinforcement Learning for Flex-Route Transit Operation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01071)
*Qi Kang,Haris N. Koutsopoulos,Hongliang Guo,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Hi-CoLA 框架，用于柔性线路公交（FRT）实时运营控制中的安全策略改进，通过高置信下界估计与策略梯度优化，在保证性能不下降的前提下提升平均与尾部风险表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在智能交通等安全与成本关键场景中应用受限，因为学习策略在部署前缺乏统计意义上的性能保证；FRT 实时控制对鲁棒性要求高且不允许性能下降。

Method: 提出 High Confidence Lower Bound Approximation（Hi-CoLA）强化学习框架，将重要性采样与集中不等式结合，并引入可微分代理网络，高效估计高置信性能下界，再通过策略梯度优化该下界以实现鲁棒性能改进。

Result: 在 FRT 运营控制问题上，Hi-CoLA 的平均性能和尾部风险表现均优于当前先进的离线强化学习与安全强化学习方法；基于芝加哥实际线路并在 SUMO 仿真器中的案例研究验证了其概念可行性。

Conclusion: Hi-CoLA 能够为实时 FRT 控制提供带性能保证的改进，并可推广到其他安全关键决策场景。

Abstract: This paper studies safe policy improvement for real-time operation control of flex-route transit (FRT) systems, where robustness is critical and any performance decrease is undesirable. While reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong empirical success, its adoption remains limited in safety and cost critical applications, such as intelligent transportation, since learned policies lack statistically certified performance guarantees before deployment. In this paper, we propose the High Confidence Lower Bound Approximation (Hi-CoLA) RL framework, which combines importance sampling and concentration inequalities with a differentiable surrogate network to efficiently estimate the high-confidence performance lower bound and optimize it via policy gradient for robust performance improvement. On the FRT operation control problem, Hi-CoLA outperforms state-of-the-art offline and safe RL methods in both average and tail-risk performance. A case study on an actual route in Chicago, implemented in the SUMO simulator, serves as a proof of concept. Hi-CoLA enhances real-time FRT control with guaranteed performance and is broadly applicable to other safety critical decision-making scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [pico-WAM: Evaluating Counterfactual Prediction and Planning Error in a Compact World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01039)
*Keivalya Pandya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: pico-WAM 通过受控干预诊断紧凑世界动作模型的控制瓶颈，发现轨迹评分比候选生成更值得优先改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当世界动作模型在控制任务中失败时，需要判断瓶颈来自候选提议生成还是轨迹评分，并建立可复现的诊断协议。

Method: 提出 pico-WAM，向模型提供物理状态以追踪策略与动力学的交互；使用 206 条 Push-T 演示和 15 个训练种子，比较不同数据条件与搜索配置，并进行反事实分支训练、用模拟器代价替换学习评分、仅在候选生成中替换模拟器状态等干预，另开展开门任务研究。

Result: 反事实分支训练改善了短时域代价排序，并将策略重启搜索相对热启动搜索的覆盖优势扩大 4.8 个百分点；策略重启在两种数据条件下均胜出。用模拟器代价替换学习轨迹评分在保留学习候选提议时提升覆盖率 12.2 个百分点；仅在候选生成中替换模拟器状态未检测到收益。诊断还表明更好的排序不一定带来更好的控制或超越模仿学习。

Conclusion: 在测试设定中，轨迹评分是有效的改进目标；研究提供了诊断紧凑世界模型的可复现协议，并强调排序提升未必转化为控制性能或优于模仿学习。

Abstract: When a World Action Model fails at control, is the bottleneck proposal generation or trajectory scoring? We introduce pico-WAM, a compact model with supplied physical state for tracing policy-dynamics interactions. Using 206 Push-T demonstrations and 15 training seeds, we compare data regimes and search configurations. Counterfactual branch training improves short-horizon cost ranking and enlarges the coverage advantage of policy-restarted over warm-started search by 4.8 percentage points; restart wins under both data regimes. Replacing learned trajectory scores with simulator costs improves coverage by 12.2 points while retaining learned proposals. Substituting simulator states in proposal generation alone shows no detected gain. Component diagnostics and a separate door-opening study show why better ranking need not yield better control or outperform imitation. These controlled interventions identify scoring as an effective improvement target in the tested setting and provide a reproducible protocol for diagnosing compact world models.

</details>


### [117] [HALO-LP: A Homogeneous All-Shifted Non-Interior-Point Method for Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01038)
*Jose Rojas,Ana Aguilar,Eusebio Bugarin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HALO-LP：一种结合齐次自对偶嵌入、全偏移非线性互补函数与近端原始-对偶正则化的齐次全偏移非内点线性规划方法，支持任意原始-对偶热启动并具备不可行性检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内点法通常要求严格内点迭代，难以直接利用任意原始-对偶热启动；需要一种无需严格内点迭代、可高效求解并检测原始/对偶不可行性的线性规划方法。

Method: 将齐次自对偶（HSD）嵌入与全偏移非线性互补（NCP）函数及近端原始-对偶正则化相结合；所得牛顿方程可基于正定矩阵凝聚，并用 Cholesky 分解高效求解；HSD 嵌入通过 Farkas 证书实现原始和对偶不可行性检测。

Result: 在由 Netlib 测试集构造的扰动 LP 实例上，HALO-LP 展现出最先进的热启动性能，并在评估设置中优于竞争求解器；在解析刻画的 LP 问题上验证了最优性与不可行性检测；通过序列线性规划（SLP）用于四足机器人质心步行和小跑的机器人最优控制，验证了适用性。

Conclusion: HALO-LP 是一种有效的齐次全偏移非内点 LP 方法，兼具任意热启动、高效牛顿求解、不可行性检测和机器人最优控制应用潜力。

Abstract: This letter introduces HALO-LP, a novel homogeneous all-shifted non-interior-point method for linear programming (LP). The proposed method combines a homogeneous self-dual (HSD) embedding with all-shifted nonlinear complementarity (NCP) functions and proximal primal-dual regularization, enabling arbitrary primal-dual warm starts without requiring strictly interior iterates. The resulting Newton equations admit a condensation based on a positive-definite matrix that can be efficiently factorized using Cholesky decomposition, while the HSD embedding enables primal and dual infeasibility detection through Farkas certificates. Numerical evaluations on perturbed LP instances derived from the Netlib test set demonstrate state-of-the-art warm-starting performance, outperforming the competing solvers in the evaluated setting. We further validate optimality and infeasibility detection on analytically characterized LP problems. Finally, we demonstrate HALO-LP's applicability to robotic optimal control through sequential linear programming (SLP) for centroidal walking and trotting of quadrupedal robots.

</details>


### [118] [Generalizable robotic manipulation through compositional skill reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00978)
*Hao Ren,Shuohan Chen,Senyuan Mu,Ruihan Huang,Wanfeng Shang,Yajing Shen*

Main category: cs.RO
