### [119] [Evaluating Tactile Foundation Models for End-to-End Imitation Learning in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00957)
*Masaki Murooka,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Hanbit Oh,Keisuke Shirai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将多种触觉基础模型集成到Diffusion Policy中，系统评估其在接触丰富操作模仿学习中的有效性，并研究编码器微调与任务特定中期训练等适配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉基础模型已通过大规模触觉数据预训练获得通用触觉表征，但如何将这些预训练表征用于端到端模仿学习仍缺乏充分探索。

Method: 将多个触觉基础模型接入Diffusion Policy，在常见实验设置下系统评估其用于接触丰富操作的表现；进一步考察编码器微调与任务特定中期训练等适配策略；在真实世界和仿真环境的多类操作任务中开展大量评估，包括超过5000次真实机器人策略 rollout。

Result: 通过跨任务、跨环境的广泛实验，获得了关于不同触觉基础模型及其适配策略在端到端模仿学习中实际效果的实证洞见。

Conclusion: 预训练触觉表征可被集成并适配到下游接触丰富模仿学习中；系统评估与适配策略研究为触觉基础模型在端到端机器人学习中的应用提供了经验依据。

Abstract: Tactile sensing plays an important role in imitation learning for contact-rich manipulation by providing direct observations of physical contact with objects. Recently, several tactile foundation models (TFMs) have been proposed to learn general-purpose tactile representations through pretraining on large-scale tactile datasets. However, how these pretrained tactile representations should be leveraged for end-to-end imitation learning remains insufficiently explored. In this work, we integrate multiple TFMs into Diffusion Policy and systematically evaluate their effectiveness for contact-rich manipulation under common experimental settings. We further investigate adaptation strategies, including encoder fine-tuning and task-specific mid-training, to understand how pretrained tactile representations can be effectively used for downstream imitation learning. Through extensive evaluation across diverse manipulation tasks in both real-world and simulation environments, including more than 5,000 real-world policy rollouts, we provide empirical insights into the use of TFMs for end-to-end imitation learning. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [120] [Task-Specialized Language-Conditioned Models for Fine-Grained Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00955)
*Chanho Kim,Soyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文介绍 MetaFine Open Competition（CoRL 2026）参赛方案：基于冻结 DINOv2/CLIP 编码器、语言条件特征调制和任务专用动作头的五模型系统，通过数据改进与推理策略实现细粒度机器人操作，官方总分 0.914，15 支队伍中排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度机器人操作需要指令 grounding、鲁棒感知和精确接触控制，作者旨在构建面向该竞赛的高性能多任务操作方案。

Method: 采用统一架构训练五个独立模型，使用冻结 DINOv2 与 CLIP 编码器、可训练的语言条件特征调制及任务专用动作头；通过接触感知抓取过滤、指令匹配数据和插入失败专家修正改进示范；推理时采用光度测试时增强、随机动作平均和不确定性触发重规划等任务相关策略。

Result: 官方总体得分为 0.914，在 15 支队伍中排名第三。

Conclusion: 该方案表明，结合冻结视觉语言表征、任务专用动作头、数据质量改进和任务自适应推理策略，可有效提升细粒度机器人操作竞赛表现，并为模型及任务特定行为分析提供基础。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic manipulation requires instruction grounding, robust perception, and precise contact control. We describe our submission to the MetaFine Open Competition at CoRL 2026. Five independently trained models use a common architecture with frozen DINOv2 and CLIP encoders, trainable language-conditioned feature modulation, and task-specific action heads. We improve demonstrations through contact-aware grasp filtering, instruction-matched data, and expert correction of insertion failures. At inference, task-dependent strategies include photometric test-time augmentation, stochastic action averaging, and uncertainty-triggered replanning. Our submission achieves an official overall score of 0.914 and ranks third among 15 teams. We analyze the models and their task-specific behavior.

</details>


### [121] [Why Koopman Structure Does Not Help Differentiable World Models for Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00951)
*Haizhou Ge,Mengshuo Wang,Yufei Jia,Lei Han,Lu Shi,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在通过可微世界模型反传训练 Unitree G1 全身跟踪策略的设定下，Koopman 提升线性动力学并不优于 MLP 世界模型；每步重提升效果最好，但仍退化为动作仿射模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 Koopman 结构（提升状态空间中的线性动力学）能否帮助人形全身跟踪策略训练：在其余管线不变的情况下，用 Koopman 世界模型替换原 MLP 世界模型。

Method: 在模拟 Unitree G1 跟踪任务中比较 Koopman 与 MLP 世界模型，评估回报、单步预测误差和训练速度，并测试不同提升与滚动方案；分析动作仿射类模型对策略数据的拟合问题，并在 G1、Ant、Humanoid 上做动作输入消融。

Result: 未带来改善：回报最多持平（0.823 对 0.830），同数据单步误差为 MLP 的 1.3–1.6 倍，训练慢约 15%；提升滚动会发散或无法训练策略。每步重提升最佳，但不使用提升线性动力学，等价于带状态相关偏移和斜率的动作仿射模型；动作仿射 MLP 同样更差。固定状态下真实动力学对动作的曲率仅解释部分误差；跨状态时策略动作形成曲面，仿射模型仅在策略动作切点准确（G1 上该切点与 MLP 相当，均值动作处差 31 倍）。偏移需仅从状态编码策略作用位置，而 MLP 可读取动作；输入策略动作可消除大部分差距。唯一接近 MLP 的在线修复是把动作输入非线性提升，但放弃 Koopman 结构。

Conclusion: 在该任务与管线中，MLP 世界模型仍是更好选择；Koopman 线性提升结构收益不足，保持该结构会限制对策略动作流形的拟合。

Abstract: We test whether Koopman structure, linear dynamics in a lifted state space, helps when a humanoid whole-body tracking policy is trained by backpropagating through a differentiable world model, replacing the MLP world model of an otherwise unchanged pipeline for simulated Unitree G1 tracking. It does not: return is at best equal (0.823 versus 0.830), one-step error on identical data is 1.3 to 1.6 times the MLP's, and training is about 15% slower. Lifted rollouts diverge or fail to train a policy; of the schemes tested, re-lifting at every imagined step trains best. Re-lifting never uses the lifted linear dynamics and leaves a model affine in the action, with state-dependent offset and slope; an action-affine MLP also predicts worse, so the cost belongs to this class. At a fixed state, the curvature of the true dynamics in the action explains only part of it. Across states, the policy's actions span a curved region, so an affine model fits policy data only as the tangent at the policy's action, the operating point: on G1, the MLP's tangent there is as accurate as the MLP; at the mean action, 31 times worse. The offset must encode, from the state alone, where the policy acts, whereas an MLP reads the action. Supplying the policy's action removes most of the remaining gap on G1, Ant and Humanoid. The only online repair approaching the MLP feeds the action into the nonlinear lift, giving up the structure. The MLP world model thus remains the better choice here.

</details>


### [122] [Trading Transparency for Stability: Friction-Cone-Constrained Bilateral Teleoperation for Opening Doors of Unsecured Furniture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00898)
*Cheng Zhou,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出鲁棒摩擦锥投影（RFCP）与触觉保护机制，在保证未固定家具在开门时保持静止的前提下，量化并控制稳定性与触觉透明性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 打开未固定家具会加载其支撑处摩擦接触，拉力过大导致滑动或倾覆；直接施加摩擦锥约束可避免失效，但会损失触觉透明性，因此需要量化该权衡并提供可证明稳定的控制方法。

Method: 基于接触摩擦锥与支撑多边形倾覆裕度构造允许扳手集合，并针对有界质量与摩擦误差收缩集合以增强鲁棒性；收缩集合内指令可证明使家具保持静止（命题1）。该映射称为RFCP，其与触觉保护器具有有界力传递误差（命题2），透明性仅在约束边界附近由稳定性因子决定的带宽内退化。工作条件控制器让操作者指令标量摆动幅度，从端姿态跟随铰链运动学，并按阶段调节抓取扳手；预任务证书判断门能否在不移动家具的情况下打开。

Result: 在三类柜体和两种支撑、五名操作者共100次试验中测量稳定性-透明性权衡，并对触觉保护器、RFCP和位置基线进行消融。

Conclusion: 该机制不限于门体，适用于任何受摩擦接触反作用的主体。

Abstract: Opening the door of unsecured furniture loads the frictional contacts at its supports; too large a pull slides or tips the object. A friction-cone constraint on the commanded wrench removes this failure mode but costs haptic transparency. We quantify the trade-off: an admissible wrench set built from contact friction cones and support-polygon tipping margins is shrunk for robustness to bounded mass and friction errors, and any command in the shrunken set provably leaves the furniture static (Prop. 1). We call this map the robust friction cone projection (RFCP). RFCP and its haptic guard admit a bounded force transmission error (Prop. 2): transparency degrades only within a band near the constraint boundary, whose width is set by one scalar, the stability factor . A working condition controller handles the door: the operator commands a scalar swing amplitude, the slave orientation follows hinge kinematics, and the grasp wrench is regulated per phase. A pre task certificate decides whether a door can be opened without moving the furniture. Experiments on three cabinets and two supports with five operators (100 trials) measure the stability transparency trade-off and ablate the guard, RFCP and the position baseline. The mechanism is not door-specific: it applies to any body reacted by frictional contacts.

</details>


### [123] [Verifying Test-State Identity in Paired Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00896)
*Rafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出：在向量化 reset/autoreset 条件下，仅凭 task、seed、episode ID 相同不足以证明两个策略面对同一测试初始状态；终止时机差异会改变后续初始状态分配。作者提出评估完整性框架（I1-I5 不变量、provenance 日志、可选 scene fingerprint、自动检查器）并在 LeRobot 0.6.1 LIBERO harness 上用模拟控制器做受控诊断，建议配对统计前核验声明/解析的初始状态身份与来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真 VLA 配对评估常默认匹配 task、seed、episode 标识即代表相同测试条件，但在特定 vectorized reset 与 autoreset 机制下，不同终止时序会改变后续 initial-state assignment，使相同 episode index 未必对应相同测试状态，从而威胁配对比较的有效性。

Method: 提出 evaluation-integrity 框架：I1-I5 不变量、provenance logging、可选 scene fingerprints 与 automated checker；在 LeRobot 0.6.1 LIBERO harness 中进行受控研究，控制器为 emulated controllers，并区分四类证据层级：(A) mixed-horizon Sync wave-1 ID 对比 [20,5,22,7] vs [4,5,6,7]；(B) homogeneous separate-job Sync 对照（16 cells，0 ID/fingerprint mismatch）；(C) Async+Freeze batch-size map 差异（12 个 I3 mismatch；bs=8 不完整；机制未隔离）；(D) 仅 synthetic McNemar 示例。

Result: A 层显示混合 horizon 下 episode ID 对应关系可发生偏移；B 层在均质 separate-job Sync 对照中未发现 ID/fingerprint 不匹配；C 层发现 12 个 I3 不匹配且 bs=8 数据不完整，但机制未被隔离；D 层仅为合成 McNemar 演示。作者明确未完成真实 VLA shared-vector 实验，控制器为模拟，因此结果主要是机制诊断与评估流程证据，而非真实排行榜复现。

Conclusion: 配对统计前应检查 declared/resolved init-state identity 与 provenance，不能仅凭 task/seed/episode ID 判定测试条件一致；该文不主张已发表的 VLA leaderboards 普遍无效，而是提供完整性检查框架并提示特定 vectorized reset/autoreset 条件下的评估风险。

Abstract: Simulation-based VLA evaluation often treats matching task, seed, and episode identifiers as sufficient evidence that two policies faced identical test conditions. Under specific vectorized reset and autoreset conditions, different termination timing can change subsequent initial-state assignment, so the same episode index need not mean the same test state. We present an evaluation-integrity framework for paired comparison - invariants I1-I5, provenance logging, optional scene fingerprints, and an automated checker - and a controlled LeRobot 0.6.1 LIBERO harness study with emulated controllers. Four evidence levels are kept separate: (A) mixed-horizon Sync wave-1 IDs [20, 5, 22, 7] vs [4, 5, 6, 7]; (B) homogeneous separate-job Sync control (16 cells, 0 ID/fingerprint mismatches); (C) Async+Freeze batch-size map differences (12 I3 mismatches; bs=8 incomplete; mechanism unisolated); (D) a synthetic McNemar illustration only. Controllers were emulated and no real-VLA shared-vector experiment was completed. We recommend checking declared/resolved init-state identity and provenance before paired statistics. We do not claim that published VLA leaderboards are generally invalid.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-quadrotor systems interconnected via structurally unknown forces and moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00887)
*Kaiyue Xing,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Sarthak Mishra,Di Liu,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi,Leonid Fridman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多四旋翼系统在紧密不确定耦合下的协调控制，提出嵌入自适应律的协调策略，并给出计及位置-姿态耦合的稳定性分析；通过绳连接负载协同空中运输实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多四旋翼协同任务中，飞行器间互联力/力矩与协调误差相关且结构未知，传统解耦位置-姿态设计难以保证耦合稳定性，因此需要在缺乏功能/参数结构知识（甚至无线性参数化假设）的条件下实现协调控制。

Method: 将互连力与力矩建模为协调误差的状态相关函数，仅依赖适用于多类Euler-Lagrange机械系统的一般状态相关二次界；提出嵌入自适应律的协调策略以补偿结构知识缺失，并进行计及互联力/力矩诱导的位置-姿态耦合的耦合稳定性分析。

Result: 真实实验采用绳连接负载的多四旋翼协同空中运输；对比实验表明，当负载掉落产生经绳索在全编队传播的扰动时，所提策略显著减小扰动后的瞬态响应。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在未知互连耦合结构下有效补偿复杂互连影响，并通过耦合稳定性分析与真实实验验证了多四旋翼紧密耦合协调控制的有效性。

Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of controlling multi-quadrotor systems subject to tight uncertain couplings. We consider interconnection forces and moments among the quadrotors to be state-dependent functions of the coordination errors, without assuming knowledge of their functional/parametric structures, e.g., no linear-in-parameters assumptions are made. We only rely on general state-dependent quadratic bounds valid for several Euler-Lagrange mechanical systems. We propose a coordination strategy embedding adaptive laws dedicated to compensating for the lack of structural knowledge. Unlike conventional decoupled position-attitude designs, we present a coupled stability analysis accounting for the position-attitude couplings induced by the interconnection forces and moments. The analysis, along with real experiments involving cooperative aerial transportation of rope-interconnected payloads, demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed solution in compensating for these complex interconnections. Comparative experiments show that, when a payload drop induces a disturbance propagating through the ropes across the whole formation, the proposed strategy reduces significantly post-disturbance transients.

</details>


### [125] [Flap like a bat: Soft pneumatic wings for biomimetic ornithopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00879)
*Besetty Satyanarayana,Sirivelu Akhilesh,Bammidi Abhilash,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仿蝙蝠飞行的扑翼机构，用PneuNet软体气动执行器替代传统转动铰链，并通过气阀与气泵控制实现平滑扑翼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扑翼机构依赖刚性转动关节，机械复杂度和声学特征较高；蝙蝠飞行的复杂运动学启发了更柔顺、可变形的仿生翼设计。

Method: 在柔性翼骨架中集成气动气腔，由3/2通电磁阀和气泵调节气流与执行器变形；利用ANSYS有限元分析评估PneuNet执行器在不同压力下的弯曲行为，以指导翼结构设计。

Result: 仿真展示了PneuNet执行器的压力相关弯曲特性，并验证了以柔顺气动执行器替代刚性转动铰链的可行性；该方案有望降低机械复杂度、降低声学特征并增强适应性与翼形变能力。

Conclusion: 用柔顺气动执行器替代刚性转动关节可行，可为更敏捷、更安静且更仿生的扑翼空中机器人提供设计基础。

Abstract: This paper describes the development of a bio-inspired ornithopter wing mechanism that uses soft pneumatic actuators, such as PneuNet structures, in place of revolute joints, inspired by the complicated kinematics of bat flight. The proposed design employs pneumatically driven air pockets within a flexible wing skeleton to achieve smooth, lifelike flapping motion. The actuation system is controlled by 3/2-way solenoid valves and an air pump, which manipulate airflow and actuator deformation. We demonstrate, using ANSYS FEA software, the bending behavior of the PneuNet actuators under varying pressures to inform the wing's structural design. The implementation of soft robotics in the flapping-wing system offers potential advantages over traditional mechanisms, such as reduced mechanical complexity, lower acoustic signatures, enhanced adaptability, and wing morphing. This work demonstrates the feasibility of replacing rigid revolute joints with compliant pneumatic actuators, enabling more agile, silent, and biomimetic aerial robots.

</details>


### [126] [A Reproducible Reconstruction and Evaluation Framework for Photorealistic Lunar Analog Scene Reconstruction Toward Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00870)
*Antar Mazumder,Ryan Hartzell,Luke Boyd,Alexander Robinson,Neha Sharma,Chance Carnival,Frances Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套以人控参考为基准的月球模拟场景光真实感重建评估流程，量化月面遍历效应下3DGS单目重建精度，并指出仅靠splat训练指标不足以预测跨重建新视角合成保真度，试点覆盖分数作为几何诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球探测数字孪生需要高保真场景重建与标准化评估，但运动模糊、扬尘雾霾、轨迹偏差和稀疏视角等遍历效应会影响重建质量，且缺乏以人控参考为基准、可量化跨重建光真实感保真度的评估方法。

Method: 构建重建管线，对比人控与巡视器桅杆单目相机输入，并以物理真值评估；在三个月球模拟场景上基准六种3DGS变体，使用标准光度指标在留出测试集评估新视角合成；分析遍历效应对数字孪生重建的影响，并试点基于COLMAP位姿和点云结构的覆盖分数Sc诊断。

Result: 实验表明，测试集上的NVS性能不会按比例迁移到重建数字孪生中的光度指标；splat训练指标不足以预测跨重建光真实感NVS保真度；巡视器轨迹偏差和几何场景覆盖是决定重建光真实感质量的关键因素；Sc可在无人控参考时作为最高可达结构保真度的有意义上界启发式。

Conclusion: 研究建立了标准化、人控参考的光真实感场景重建评估流程，为月球模拟数字孪生开发和未来行星探测中的重建质量评估提供依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a reconstruction pipeline that empirically quantifies the photorealistic scene reconstruction accuracy from both human-controlled and rover mast camera monocular feeds against physical ground truth as a function of Lunar traversal effects (e.g., motion blur, haze due to lofted regolith, trajectory bias, and sparse views). First, we benchmark six variants of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) across three Lunar analog scenes on held-out test sets using standard photometric metrics. We then establish how under traversal effects, this test-set-specific Novel View Synthesis (NVS) performance does not proportionally scale to the same photometric metrics in the reconstructed DTs. Our results quantify that splat training metrics alone are insufficient predictors of Cross-Reconstruction Photorealistic NVS Fidelity (CRPNVS), and that factors such as rover trajectory bias and geometric scene coverage play a critical role in determining the photorealistic quality of the reconstructed scenes targeting Lunar analog Digital Twins. Additionally, we pilot study Coverage Score ($S_c$), a geometry-based diagnostic derived from COLMAP poses and point cloud structure that provides a meaningful heuristic about the highest achievable CRPNVS structural fidelity, independently of splat training performance in the absence of a human-controlled reference. These findings establish a standardized, human control-referenced evaluation pipeline for photorealistic scene reconstruction for Lunar-analog Digital Twin development and future planetary exploration. Code and project page: this https URL

</details>


### [127] [MOST: Memory of Object States and Task Progress for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00734)
*Diqi He,Jinbang Guo,Yijun Shen,Zhiwei Yu,Huan Yang,Dingwen Zhang,Junwei Han,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MOST：一种面向长时程具身操作的政策内部记忆框架，通过对象状态与子任务进度监督压缩多视图观测的历史/未来时序 token，并发布 RoboCasa-Long 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作中，目标物体可能离开视野，已完成子任务仍约束后续动作；现有递归状态更新、测试时训练或历史检索机制会积累冗长、冗余且依赖视角的视觉经验，使策略难以推断任务状态。作者认为长时程智能体应记住任务相关的世界状态变化与已完成进度，而非单纯增加记忆量。

Method: MOST 将多视图观测压缩为时序 token，形成策略内部记忆，并显式用对象状态和子任务进度监督历史 token 与预测未来 token；该记忆在单一端到端策略中直接条件化动作生成，无需外部规划器或检索阶段。同时提出多分钟、多阶段移动操作基准 RoboCasa-Long。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365、RoboCasa-Long 和真实机器人实验中，MOST 均优于先前 SOTA 模型 Xiaomi-Robotics-1（XR-1）：RoboCasa365 平均成功率由 65.9% 提升至 72.1%，RoboCasa-Long 平均最大任务进度由 20.83% 提升至 42.22%，真实世界成功率由 6.66% 提升至 43.33%；并优于匹配的视觉历史记忆变体。

Conclusion: 有效的长时程记忆不仅取决于保留多少经验，更取决于是否保留对决策相关的任务进度与世界状态变化；MOST 通过结构化对象状态和子任务进度记忆显著提升长时程操作性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied manipulation requires policies to act on information that is no longer visible. Modified objects may leave the field of view, while completed subtasks continue to constrain future actions. Existing approaches extend temporal context through recurrent state updates, test-time training, or storage and retrieval of past observations. As horizons grow, these mechanisms can still leave the policy to infer task state from long, redundant, and viewpoint-dependent visual experience. We argue that long-horizon agents benefit not merely from remembering more, but from remembering task-relevant state changes and progress. We propose Memory of Object States and Task Progress (MOST), a policy-internal memory framework that compresses multi-view observations into temporal tokens. Historical and predictive future tokens are explicitly supervised with object states and subtask progress, structuring memory around what has changed in the world and what has already been accomplished. The resulting memory directly conditions action generation within a single end-to-end policy, without an external planner or retrieval stage. We further introduce RoboCasa-Long, a benchmark of multi-minute, multi-stage mobile manipulation tasks. Across RoboCasa365, RoboCasa-Long, and real-world robot experiments, MOST consistently outperforms Xiaomi-Robotics-1 (XR-1), the previous state-of-the-art model. It raises average success from 65.9% to 72.1% on RoboCasa365, average maximum task progress from 20.83% to 42.22% on RoboCasa-Long, and real-world success from 6.66% to 43.33%. MOST also outperforms a matched visual-history memory variant, showing that effective long-horizon memory depends not only on how much experience is retained, but on whether it preserves decision-relevant progress and world-state changes.

</details>


### [128] [STEER: Route-Aware Adaptive Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00718)
*Yangang Zou,Pei Liu,Nan Song,Bozhou Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Jun Ma,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STEER，一种无需场景难度标注的通用过度推理缓解策略，通过不确定性感知路由rollout与跨路由优势信用分配，实现VLA中CoT与直接响应的显式自适应路由优化；在NAVSIM v1/v2上于规划质量和推理效率Pareto前沿均具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在端到端自动驾驶中易过度调用CoT推理，造成不必要计算开销并可能反而降低规划性能；现有显式自适应路由方法依赖劳动密集、难扩展且因驾驶复杂度主观性而易出错的场景难度标注，本文首次探索无场景难度标注下实现显式路由优化的自适应推理。

Method: STEER包含两部分：(i) routing uncertainty-aware rollout，校准采样使路由多样性与模型不确定性对齐；(ii) cross-route advantage credit assignment，引入基于环境奖励的差异化度量，而非主观标签，以强化最优路由决策。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1/v2上的大量实验表明，STEER在规划质量及推理效率Pareto前沿上均与最先进方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: STEER证明无需场景难度标注也可实现显式路由优化的自适应推理，从而缓解VLA过度CoT问题，并在规划性能与推理效率之间取得有竞争力的平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language action (VLA) models excel for end-to-end autonomous driving, yet their tendency to over-invoke chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning mirrors a human cognitive pitfall: overthinking. Just as deliberate reasoning can slow or mislead human judgment in routine tasks, excessive CoT in VLA incurs unnecessary compute overhead and can paradoxically degrade planning performance. Recent research tackles this via adaptive reasoning, which dynamically modulates inference complexity by selectively triggering CoT for complex scenes while responding directly to routine ones. While prior adaptive methods rely on implicit adaptation, recent work shows that explicitly optimizing the route between CoT and direct responses yields superior performance, assuming scene-difficulty annotations which are labor-intensive, poorly scalable, and prone to error due to the subjectivity of judging driving complexity. We explore, for the first time, whether adaptive reasoning with explicit route optimization can be achieved without any scene-difficulty labeling. We introduce STEER, a generic over-reasoning mitigation strategy featuring two key components: (i) routing uncertainty-aware rollout, which calibrates sampling to align routing diversity with model uncertainty; and (ii) cross-route advantage credit assignment, which introduces a differential metric to reinforce optimal routing decisions based on environmental rewards rather than subjective labels. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM v1/v2 demonstrate that STEER is competitive with state-of-the-art methods in both planning quality and the Pareto front of inference efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [PREM: Prediction-Regulated Memory for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00634)
*Xiao Huang,Ya Zhang,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Prediction-Regulated Memory（PREM），用动作条件未来视觉预测监督记忆学习并调节视觉状态更新，从而为视觉-语言-动作策略提供时序上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人下一步动作常依赖近期运动或当前观测中缺失的早期视觉证据；循环视觉记忆是否有用取决于视觉状态表示什么以及如何随新观测更新。

Method: PREM 在训练时通过预测未来视觉特征为记忆学习提供时序监督；在执行时，将当前视觉特征与上一决策时刻的预测比较，并用空间归一化的预测差异逐空间位置调节视觉状态更新；更新后的视觉状态与当前观测一起为动作生成提供时序上下文。方法基于 pi_0.5 构建。

Result: 在 DOMINO 动态操作任务上平均成功率从 9.63% 提升到 20.74%；在 RMBench M(1) 记忆依赖设置上从 14.4% 提升到 83.8%；在 LIBERO 和 LIBERO-Pro 上提升通用操作表现及对空间扰动的鲁棒性；单张 NVIDIA H800 GPU 上平均推理开销为 7.7 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以未来预测调节记忆学习与更新能显著提升动态操作和记忆依赖任务表现，并以较低推理开销增强通用操作与空间扰动鲁棒性。

Abstract: A robot's next action can depend on recent motion or earlier visual evidence absent from its current observation. A recurrent visual-memory architecture can retain this history in a visual state, but its usefulness depends on what the state represents and how it is updated with new observations. We introduce Prediction-Regulated Memory (PREM), a framework that uses action-conditioned future prediction to guide both memory learning and updating in vision-language-action policies. During training, predicting future visual features provides temporal supervision for memory learning. During execution, PREM compares current visual features with the prediction made at the preceding decision and uses the spatially normalized prediction discrepancy to regulate updates to the visual state at each spatial location. The updated visual state supplies temporal context for action generation alongside current observations. Built on $\pi_{0.5}$, PREM improves average success rate from 9.63% to 20.74% on DOMINO dynamic manipulation tasks and from 14.4% to 83.8% on the RMBench M(1) memory-dependent setting. Further evaluations on LIBERO and LIBERO-Pro show improvements in general manipulation and robustness to spatial perturbations. PREM incurs a mean inference overhead of 7.7 ms on a single NVIDIA H800 GPU.

</details>


### [130] [Learning from an Imitable Teacher: Competition Solution for the MetaFine Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00615)
*Yanze Zhang,Hao Liu,Haochang Hao,Zhixiang Shen,Chunyu Miao,Yuyao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Team Friday 在 MetaFine 开放竞赛中提出面向精细操作的分任务小型模仿学习方案，以 0.947 总分获第二名，并总结演示数据与评估经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛旨在超越二值成功指标，评估光照与相机变化下的感知、指令理解及精细操作行为；任务包括按指定部位抓瓶、以 4 mm 间隙插入字母形 peg 等，对精确操作和模仿学习提出挑战。

Method: 不微调大型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，而为每个任务训练一个 19M 参数策略，并使用独立指令分类器；训练数据全部由仿真器中的特权脚本教师生成，并包含教师对学生错误的纠正。作者分析失败回合，指出近乎总成功的教师仍可能教坏无记忆学生：暂停变成停滞、固定习惯变成捷径、且不展示学生自身错误；据此使教师行为更“可模仿”。

Result: 总体得分 0.947，为 π0 基线的两倍以上并获第二名；在最难的字母插入任务上，官方分数由 0.44 提升至 0.82（数据增加 32%、架构不变），最终达到 0.88。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在精细操作模仿学习中，演示数据的可模仿性与失败分析至关重要；作者总结了演示数据和评估的实践教训，可助力精确操作模仿学习。

Abstract: The MetaFine Open Competition (RoboFineMani workshop, CoRL 2026) evaluates fine-grained manipulation beyond binary success. Its five simulated tasks, from grasping a bottle by an instructed part to inserting letter-shaped pegs with 4 mm clearance, are scored on perception under lighting and camera changes, instruction understanding, and behavior. This report presents Team Friday's solution, which got second place with an overall score of 0.947, more than twice the $\pi_0$ baseline. Instead of fine-tuning a large vision-language-action (VLA) model, we train one 19M-parameter policy per task with a separate instruction classifier and focus on the data, all generated in the simulator by a privileged scripted teacher, including its corrections of the student's mistakes. By analyzing failed episodes, we find that a teacher that almost always succeeds can still teach a memoryless student badly: its pauses become stalls, its fixed habits become shortcuts, and it never shows the errors the student makes. Making the teacher \emph{imitable} raised the official score on letter insertion, the hardest task, from 0.44 to 0.82 with 32\% more data and the same architecture, and finally to 0.88. We summarize practical lessons on demonstration data and evaluation, which we hope will help in imitation learning for precise manipulation.

</details>


### [131] [Roz: A Taste Test for World Model Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00612)
*Kenneth Ge,Dhruv Sheth,Kunvar Thaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文质疑当前潜空间动力学世界模型是否真正编码物理规律，指出其在图像相似性任务上表现强，却会遗忘质量、形变、摩擦和弹性等关键物理属性；为此提出可扩展的物理属性评测基准 Roz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在机器人规划等任务上表现优异，但评测多依赖不透明、模型特定且高计算成本的探针或规划器，难以判断模型是否学到了真实物理，而非仅拟合视觉重建。

Method: 作者明确世界模型应编码的物理内容，并提出两类可直接探测物理属性且易扩展到新属性的评测方法；Roz 包含 10 个对比任务和 3 个连续任务，共 1734 个场景，仅需模型可输入静态帧或视频、可提取潜变量并提供潜空间距离函数，即可单次运行。

Result: 在 AnonWM 上，性能大致随训练步数提升，但不一定与重建 MSE 相关；Roz 在控制任务上得到验证，并揭示当前模型会忽略质量、形变、摩擦、弹性等物理细节。

Conclusion: 当前世界模型的失败在一定程度上源于评测策略的缺陷；Roz 提供透明、可扩展、低计算成本的物理属性评测方式，期望推动更快速、可比较的世界模型基准迭代。

Abstract: State of the art latent dynamics world models achieve impressive performance for robot planning. But do they actually encode the physics of the world? In this paper, we show that popular world models today achieve strong results on tasks that reduce to image similarity but forget key details about objects in the scene like mass, deformation, friction, and bounciness. We achieve this by defining precisely what ought to be encoded in such a world model and introducing two new evaluation methods that can directly tease physical properties and easily scale to new ones. Our evaluation, which we call Roz, includes 10 contrastive tasks and 3 continuous tasks, totaling 1734 individual scenarios. We validate our eval on control tasks. On our own model, AnonWM, we find that performance roughly improves with training steps, while not necessarily being correlated with reconstruction MSE. We argue current model failures are a symptom of evaluation strategies (probes and planners) that are opaque, model-specific, and high-compute. Roz requires only a model that can ingest still frames or video, a way to extract latents, and a distance function between them, and it can be run in one pass. We hope future evaluations will adopt Roz's evaluation techniques. More broadly, we hope Roz can be a case study for a future where benchmarks allow rapid iteration and comparison across models.

</details>


### [132] [SaddleSense: Saddle-Shaped Modules for 3D Shape Sensing with Single-Axis Off-the-Shelf Bend Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00582)
*Dong Heon Han,Runze Zuo,Chankyo Kim,Saima Jamal,Daniel Bruder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaddleSense 提出一种 3D 打印 TPU 鞍形晶格结构，将商用单轴弯曲传感器沿四条曲边布置，实现对连续体机器人多轴弯曲、收缩和伸长的局部感知，并结合学习模型与物理回退的混合标定映射到 PCC 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人精确控制需要准确姿态反馈，但现成弯曲传感器通常只能测量单轴曲率；若安装方式不当，还会限制机器人运动并导致姿态信息不完整。

Method: 设计 TPU 鞍形晶格，每条曲边通道内固定一个商用弯曲传感器，使传感器在自身通道内弯曲而非沿段轴向跨越，从而将多轴本体变形转换为传感器曲率且不将段长与传感器长度绑定。每个四传感器模块可感知双轴弯曲、收缩和伸长。采用混合标定，将传感器读数映射为分段常曲率（PCC）参数，使用学习模型并在分布外输入时回退到物理模型。在 430 mm 连续体上构建六模块、24 个现成弯曲传感器原型。

Result: 仅使用各模块四个局部读数和物理模型时，尖端位置 RMSE 为 29.9 mm（体长的 7.0%）。数据驱动模型在类似训练集的留出样本上将尖端 RMSE 降至 13.2 mm（3.1%），在单独记录的试验中为 15.8 mm（3.7%）。将每模块从四个传感器减少到一个，尖端误差几乎增至三倍。

Conclusion: SaddleSense 通过鞍形晶格安装和混合标定，使商用单轴弯曲传感器能够提供多模式、多轴连续体姿态信息；数据驱动标定显著提升精度，同时传感器数量对误差有显著影响。

Abstract: Accurate posture feedback is essential for precise control of continuum robots. Off-the-shelf bend sensors measure curvature about only one axis; without a suitable mounting arrangement, they can restrict robot motion and provide incomplete posture information. SaddleSense introduces a 3D-printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU) saddle-shaped lattice that holds one commercial bend sensor along each curved edge. Because each sensor flexes within its own channel rather than spanning the segment axially, the lattice converts multi-axis body deformation into sensor curvature without tying segment length to sensor length. Each four-sensor module accommodates two-axis bending, contraction, and extension while quantifying all three deformation modes across its four readings. A hybrid calibration maps the sensor readings to piecewise constant curvature (PCC) parameters using a learned model paired with a physics-based fallback for out-of-distribution inputs. We demonstrate a six-module prototype with 24 off-the-shelf bend sensors on a 430~mm continuum body. Using only each module's four local readings, the physics-based model alone achieves a tip position root mean square error (RMSE) of 29.9~mm (7.0\% of body length). The data-driven model reduces the tip RMSE to 13.2~mm (3.1\%) on held-out samples of motions similar to the training set and 15.8~mm (3.7\%) on separately recorded trials. Reducing each module from four sensors to one nearly triples the tip error.

</details>


### [133] [Faster Imitation by Re-timing Demonstrations Within the Controller Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00539)
*Zongtan Li,Tianming Liu,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出示范加速后策略崩溃的机械原因是低层控制器每控制周期位移有界，连续命令合并后可能超出可实现范围；提出按可实现性贪心合并命令的单参数重定时规则，在四个robomimic任务和humanoid全身跟踪器上提升成功率并缩短成功回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将重定时崩溃归因于分布偏移，并用更细的片段加速规则应对；作者要证明该崩溃有片段选择无法解决的机械性原因：训练对可能承诺执行器无法实现的转移。

Method: 定义exceed share为重定时数据中合并后位移超出控制器可实现范围的训练对比例；提出贪心合并规则：只要连续命令之和仍可实现就合并，以合并上限为唯一参数，使每条命令按构造可实现、保持示范几何，并只在示范者留有余量处加速。

Result: 在四个模拟robomimic任务、每格200个held-out seeds上，merge cap为2时四个任务成功率均不低于冻结策略，Square提升14点并缩短成功回合；匹配节奏下uniform re-timing在Can和ToolHang分别损失9和16点，另两个在噪声内，最难任务2x时降至0；shuffled、entropy-guided或speed-conditioned替代在三个任务上不如该规则，第四个在任务可吸收的节奏下同处一带；在模拟humanoid全身跟踪器中，429个参考运动在其速度包络内重定时，2.3x成功率为71%，uniform time scaling在2x为51%。

Conclusion: 重定时加速的关键是保证每条命令机械可实现；基于exceed share的可实现性约束合并规则优于片段选择与均匀缩放，并推广到humanoid全身跟踪。

Abstract: Imitation policies inherit the operator's pace, and re-timing the demonstrations to run faster works only until success collapses at two to three times demonstration speed. Recent methods attribute that collapse to distribution shift and answer it with finer rules for which segments may be sped up; we show it has a mechanical cause that segment selection cannot address. A low-level controller realises only a bounded displacement per control period, so merging consecutive commands produces sums outside that bound: training pairs that promise a transition the plant cannot deliver. The share of such pairs in the re-timed data, which we call the exceed share, tracks where the retrained policy collapses. The rule that follows has a single parameter: merge greedily while the sum stays realisable. Every command is then realisable by construction, the demonstrated geometry is preserved, and the speed-up lands only where the demonstrator left slack. On four simulated robomimic tasks, 200 held-out seeds per cell, a merge cap of two is at or above the frozen policy's success on all four, by 14 points on Square, and shortens the successful episodes. At matched tempo, uniform re-timing loses 9 and 16 points on Can and ToolHang, is within noise on the other two, and reaches zero on the hardest at 2x. Placement also matters: no shuffled, entropy-guided or speed-conditioned alternative beats the rule on three of the four tasks, by margins the exceed share alone does not explain; on the fourth, at a tempo the task absorbs, every placement lands in the same band. The same principle carries to a simulated humanoid whole-body tracker: re-timing 429 reference motions within its velocity envelope succeeds on 71% of clips at 2.3x, against 51% for uniform time scaling at 2x.

</details>


### [134] [Reflex: Fast Robot Policy Serving by Reusing and Correcting Past Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00524)
*Ziyang Lin,Zixuan Sun,Rami Nasser,Jianli Jin,Xuanle Li,Jayanth Srinivasa,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Reflex 是一个机器人策略服务层，通过缓存完整动作块并配合两级缓存校正，绕过策略主干与动作头，从而显著降低推理延迟并提升吞吐，同时保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略生成动作块时推理开销大，成为实时控制延迟和多机器人服务吞吐瓶颈。现有复用去噪中间状态的缓存方法收益有限，且无法加速占 GPU 时间约70%的策略主干。

Method: Reflex 不重用中间计算，而是缓存完整动作块以绕过主干和动作头。针对相似观测可能需不同动作、复用误差会累积导致执行停滞，提出两级校正：动作级学习动作感知相似度、组装兼容动作块并做轻量残差校正；执行级捕捉任务进度并自适应刷新原策略以限制累积误差并平衡推理成本，且不修改底层策略。

Result: 在代表性负载和真实机器人上，Reflex 将推理延迟降低1.4倍至11倍，吞吐提升2.0倍至2.2倍，并保持可比的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 缓存完整动作块并结合动作级与执行级校正，是一种有效且无需修改原策略的机器人策略服务加速方案。

Abstract: Modern robot policies often perform expensive inference to generate action chunks, making serving a latency bottleneck for real-time control and a throughput bottleneck for multi-robot serving. Building on the success of caching in image, video, and audio generation, recent robot-policy advances have reported promising speedups by reusing intermediate denoising states to avoid redundant computation. Surprisingly, we find that such reuse often offers little advantage over simply running fewer denoising steps from fresh noise, without any cache. Moreover, accelerating denoising leaves the expensive policy backbone intact, which can account for 70% of GPU time. We present Reflex, a serving layer that turns past policy executions into a backbone-free path for future decisions. Rather than reusing intermediate computations, Reflex caches complete action chunks to bypass both the backbone and action head. This introduces two challenges: similar observations can demand different actions, and small action deviations (errors) in reuse can compound into execution stalls. Reflex addresses these through two-level cache correction. At the action level, it learns action-aware similarity from past policy decisions, assembles compatible chunks, and applies lightweight residual correction to limit action mismatch. At the execution level, it captures task progress and adaptively refreshes the original policy to limit accumulated errors and balance inference cost. Without modifying the underlying policy, Reflex improves inference latency by 1.4x to 11x and throughput by 2.0x to 2.2x on representative workloads and real robots, while preserving comparable task success.

</details>


### [135] [Behavioral Steering of Vision-Language-Action Models in the Low-Parameter Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00522)
*James Tcheng,Feiyu Gavin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究用极低维强化学习适配冻结的预训练VLA，实现行为风格引导；仅256个可训练参数即可显著提高搬运高度并保持较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究行为引导所需的最小可训练容量，以及如何在改变VLA执行风格的同时尽量保留预训练能力，并控制风格收益与能力保持之间的权衡。

Method: 冻结OpenVLA-OFT 7B策略，仅优化8至384个参数的TinyLoRA适配器，分别采用策略梯度强化学习GRPO或进化搜索CMA-ES；通过重缩放学习到的更新调节风格收益与能力保持的权衡，并比较适配器容量与优化器选择的影响。

Result: 仅用256个可训练参数，在训练任务上将搬运高度提高120 mm且成功率达92%，在留出任务上提高74 mm且成功率达80%。重缩放更新可在不重新训练的情况下调节权衡；增加适配器容量通常改善权衡，在相近风格收益下保留更多预训练能力；低参数时CMA-ES具有竞争力，容量增大时GRPO提供更好的权衡。

Conclusion: 极低维更新即可引导预训练VLA的行为；适配器容量、优化器选择和更新缩放为适配与能力保持提供了实用控制手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to adapt pretrained vision-language-action models (VLAs). We investigate the problem of behavioral steering: adapting a VLA's execution style to preferences such as carrying objects higher. To investigate how little trainable capacity such steering requires and how much pretrained capability can be retained, we freeze an OpenVLA-OFT 7B policy and optimize TinyLoRA adapters with 8 to 384 trainable parameters using either policy-gradient RL (GRPO) or evolutionary search (CMA-ES). With just 256 trainable parameters, we raise carry height by 120 mm on trained tasks at 92% success and by 74 mm on a held-out task at 80% success. By rescaling the learned update, we can tune the trade-off between style gains and capability retention without retraining. Increasing adapter capacity generally improves this trade-off, retaining more pretrained capability at comparable style gains. The choice of optimizer also depends on capacity, with CMA-ES being competitive at low parameter counts, while GRPO offers better trade-offs as capacity grows. These findings show that extremely low-dimensional updates can steer pretrained VLA behavior, with adapter capacity, optimizer choice, and update scaling offering practical control over adaptation and retention.

</details>


### [136] [WorldSketch: Sketching World Models for Zero-Shot Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00513)
*Jiaming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSketch让VLM不直接生成动作，而是构建并自我修正最小运动学世界模型，再由几何规划器联合优化接触与全身运动，从而在多任务上实现高零样本成功率并显著降低VLM token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动机器人系统要么依赖预定义技能库而受限于技能覆盖，要么需直接生成动作、约束或控制程序并对精确几何与接触进行推理，难以兼顾开放性与几何精度。

Method: VLM作为智能体主动调用感知工具，查询并视觉验证一个仅包含任务相关物体、交互几何和可能运动的最小运动学世界模型；几何规划器在该模型上联合优化接触与全身运动；执行反馈用于验证结果、更新不确定假设，并在预测被矛盾时修订世界模型。

Result: 在RoboCasa、LIBERO-PRO和R2R-CE导航上分别取得91%、97%和76%的零样本成功率；RoboCasa上比次优零样本基线高58个百分点且VLM输入token少10倍；LIBERO-PRO上比CaP-X高82个百分点；导航中匹配逐步VLM智能体的成功率且输入token少6倍。

Conclusion: 将VLM的角色从直接动作生成转变为世界模型构建与自改进，并由几何规划器完成接触与运动优化，可提升机器人智能体的泛化能力、几何精度和推理效率。

Abstract: Most agentic robotic systems use vision-language models (VLMs) either to orchestrate predefined skills or to directly generate actions, constraints, and control programs. The former is limited by the available skill library, while the latter requires VLMs to reason about precise geometry and contact. In this paper, we propose WorldSketch, a new paradigm for agentic robotics in which a VLM constructs a world model rather than generating robot actions. The VLM acts as an agent that actively queries perception tools to build and visually validate a minimal kinematic world model, capturing only task-relevant objects, their interaction geometry, and possible motions. A geometric planner then jointly optimizes contact and whole-body motion on the resulting model. Moreover, the world model is self-improving: execution feedback verifies outcomes, updates uncertain hypotheses, and enables the agent to revise the model when its predictions are contradicted. We demonstrate the generality of this paradigm across RoboCasa, LIBERO-PRO, and R2R-CE navigation, achieving zero-shot success rates of 91%, 97%, and 76%, respectively. On RoboCasa, WorldSketch outperforms the next-best zero-shot agent baseline by 58 percentage points while using 10 times fewer VLM input tokens; on LIBERO-PRO, it exceeds CaP-X by 82 percentage points. In navigation, it matches the success rate of step-by-step VLM agents while using 6 times fewer input tokens.

</details>


### [137] [MESSPa: A Multi-modal Embodiment Stimulation Shoulder Pad for Preserving the Sense of Agency in a Detachable Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00509)
*Vitvasin Vimolmongkolporn,Guillaume Gourmelen,Yukiko Iwasaki,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出MESSPa肩垫接口，将一对一运动映射与肩部空间分布式气动触觉反馈结合，以在超数机器人肢体附着与分离状态下维持操作者的能动感（SoA），并验证其可改善分离状态下的任务表现与能动感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人（如超数机器人肢体）增强人类能力的效果依赖操作者的具身感，包括能动感（SoA）和拥有感（SoO）；当肢体与身体分离以实现同时两地操作时，SoA和任务表现通常会下降。

Method: 设计Multi-modal Embodiment Stimulation Shoulder Pad（MESSPa），结合一对一运动传递与肩部空间分布式气动（气球）触觉反馈；开展2×2混合设计实验，组间为控制接口（MESSPa N=13 vs. 键盘 N=12），组内为附着状态（on-shoulder/detached），并设置单任务与双任务负荷。

Result: 与键盘控制在分离条件下相比，MESSPa显著缩短任务完成时间并显著提升分离状态下的能动感，同时缩小附着与分离条件间的表现差距；拥有感（SoO）无显著变化。

Conclusion: 将运动传递与位置性触觉反馈相结合，是在机器人肢体远程操作时恢复能动感的有效方式。

Abstract: Wearable robots such as supernumerary robotic limbs (SRLs) can augment human capability, but their effectiveness depends strongly on the operator's sense of embodiment, comprising the sense of agency (SoA) and the sense of ownership (SoO). When an SRL is detached from the body so that the operator can act in two places at once, both the sense of agency and task performance tend to degrade. This paper presents the Multi-modal Embodiment Stimulation Shoulder Pad (MESSPa), a wearable interface that combines one-to-one motion transfer with spatially distributed pneumatic (balloon) haptic feedback on the shoulder, with the goal of preserving the sense of agency across attached and detached states. We describe the system design, the control mapping, and a controlled experiment using a $2\times2$ mixed design that crosses the control interface between groups (MESSPa, $N=13$, vs. keyboard control, $N=12$ from our previous study) and attachment (on-shoulder/detached) within participants, under single- and dual-task loads. Compared with keyboard control in the detached condition, MESSPa significantly reduced task completion time and significantly improved the sense of agency in the detached state, while narrowing the performance gap between the attached and detached conditions. The sense of ownership did not change significantly. These results indicate that combining motion transfer with positional haptic feedback is an effective way to recover agency when a robotic limb is operated remotely.

</details>


### [138] [Knowing Where to Look: Interactive Object Search via Spatial-Semantic Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00499)
*Aditya Agarwal,Lucy Cai,Kelly Lu,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Tomás Lozano-Pérez,Leslie Pack Kaelbling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义合理性评分与几何占据证据通过贝叶斯对数几率滤波融合到统一3D体素信念场，以联合语义与几何推理驱动机器人交互式物体搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类搜索物体时会结合语义直觉与空间可见性/遮挡/未探索区域的几何感知；现有机器人方法仅依赖几何或仅依赖语言模型常识，缺乏对空间的显式信念或深度语义理解，因此需要联合推理。

Method: 构建端到端系统：对每个物体累积VLM合理性分数，并用贝叶斯log-odds滤波整合几何占据证据，形成统一3D体素信念场；根据高信念区域选择动作，包括从不同视角近距离观察、导航至未探索区域、以及物理交互打开关闭的柜子。

Result: 在双臂移动人形机器人上端到端部署，实验表明该方法优于仅几何搜索和基于LLM规划的方法。

Conclusion: 在显式3D信念中联合语义与几何证据，可实现类人的交互式物体搜索，并优于任一单一模态方法。

Abstract: When searching for an object, humans naturally combine semantic intuition - reasoning about where the object is likely to be stored - with geometric awareness of what parts of the space are visible, occluded, or unexplored. Effective robot object search requires the same joint reasoning, yet existing methods rely on only one: geometric methods maintain principled spatial beliefs but lack deep semantic understanding, while language-model based methods leverage commonsense knowledge but maintain no explicit belief over space. We argue that jointly reasoning over semantic and geometric evidence in an explicit 3D belief enables human-like interactive object search that outperforms either modality alone. To test this claim, we build a system that accumulates per-object VLM plausibility scores and geometric occupancy evidence via Bayesian log-odds filtering into a unified 3D voxel belief field. High-belief regions in the combined belief field drive action selection at each step: taking a closer look from a different viewpoint, navigating toward an unexplored region of the workspace, or physically interacting with a closed cabinet. We deploy this system end-to-end on a bimanual mobile humanoid and find that it outperforms geometry-only search and LLM-based planning methods. Project website - this https URL

</details>


### [139] [TacMind: Benchmarking and Modeling Tactile Interaction States for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00455)
*Jiaxin Shi,Zibin Wu,Yichi Zhang,Jianghua Duan,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个包含128个任务的仿真触觉操作基准，覆盖精确、识别和动态三类评估场景，并提出基于动作条件的潜在预测世界模型，将触觉历史建模为随时间演化的交互表示以提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉操作研究多在特定任务设定下开发和评估，难以系统比较触觉信息在不同交互条件下的贡献；同时触觉不应仅被视为瞬时观测，而应作为随时间演化的交互表示来建模。

Method: 作者构建仿真基准，包含128个任务、Precise/Recognition/Dynamic三类评估机制、受控静态与动态任务变体以及12种视觉和物理扰动设置。方法上提出动作条件潜在预测世界模型，融合触觉历史并通过预测未来触觉潜在表示学习触觉状态；基于该表示条件化动作生成，并利用预测的未来触觉信息通过预测性触觉反射细化候选动作。

Result: 在所提出的基准上，融入触觉信息在各类评估场景中均带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了系统评估触觉操作的可控基准，并表明将触觉建模为时间演化表示并结合预测性世界模型，可有效利用触觉反馈提升接触丰富操作任务的表现。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides robots with direct observations of contact physics, local geometry, and interaction dynamics, making it a vital source of feedback for contact-rich manipulation. However, existing tactile manipulation studies are often developed and evaluated within specific task settings, making it difficult to systematically compare how tactile information contributes under different interaction conditions. In this work, we develop a benchmark in simulation comprising 128 tasks organized around three complementary evaluation regimes: Precise, Recognition, and Dynamic. The benchmark further includes controlled static and dynamic task variants and 12 perturbation settings for evaluating robustness under changes in visual and physical conditions. These settings motivate studying tactile information as a temporally evolving interaction representation rather than only as an instantaneous observation. We therefore propose an action-conditioned latent predictive world model that integrates tactile history and learns a tactile state through prediction of future tactile latents. Building on this representation, we design a model that conditions action generation on the learned tactile state and uses predicted future tactile information to refine candidate actions through predictive tactile reflection. Experiments on the proposed benchmark show consistent gains from incorporating tactile information across the evaluated regimes. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [140] [Behavior Graphs: Unsupervised Graph Structures for Interpreting Robot Deployment Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00405)
*Erik Bauer,Jakob Thumm,Christopher Agia,Rohan Sinha,Chen Xu,Haruki Nishimura,Jeannette Bohg,Marco Pavone,Masha Itkina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出行为图（behavior graphs），将机器人策略部署产生的高维 rollout 压缩为可解释的有向图，用于分析成功/失败模式、指导数据收集，并提升进展预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据飞轮依赖部署数据来持续改进模型，但机器人部署产生大量高维 rollout 数据，难以理解且难以据此可靠地制定数据收集决策。

Method: 行为图通过学习识别跨 rollout 的相似行为模式、按时间连接这些行为，并关联到最终结果，从而把策略 rollout 压缩为单个可解释的有向图。

Result: 在三个真实任务的用户研究中，18 人中有 16 人更偏好行为图而非 rollout 视频；行为图达到相当结论的同时，感知时间需求降低 40.3%，努力程度降低 37.6%。在 43 个数据集上，行为图作为进展预测器，相比最强基线将预测进展与真实时间顺序的秩相关提高 22.9%，平均绝对误差降低 53.5%。

Conclusion: 行为图为高维策略 rollout 提供结构，使部署数据更易解释和推理，有助于指导数据收集，并随部署机器人数量增长而提升部署数据的可用性。

Abstract: Data flywheels promise continuous improvement via insights from deployment data. Understanding how the deployed model succeeds and fails can steer subsequent data collection towards patching failures and reinforcing successes. However, robot deployments create large volumes of high-dimensional rollout data, which is challenging to understand and confidently ground data collection decisions in. To make deployment data easier to analyze and act on, we propose behavior graphs, which compress policy rollouts into a single interpretable, directed graph. We construct the graphs by learning to identify similar behavior modes across rollouts, connecting the behaviors temporally, and finally linking them to outcomes. In a user study on three real-world tasks, where participants analyzed rollouts to choose a data collection strategy, 16 of 18 preferred behavior graphs to rollout videos, reaching comparable conclusions with 40.3% lower perceived temporal demand and 37.6% lower effort. Beyond interpretability for deployment data, behavior graphs are capable progress predictors: across 43 datasets, they improve the rank correlation between predicted progress and true temporal order by 22.9% and reduce mean absolute error by 53.5% relative to the strongest baseline. As the number of deployed robots grows, behavior graphs lend structure to high-dimensional policy rollouts and make deployment data easier to interpret and reason about.

</details>


### [141] [Emergent Control of Ant Learning Walks in a Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00392)
*Thomas Misiek,Andrew Philippides,Paul Graham,James Knight*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将基于路径整合（PI）与蘑菇体启发视觉罗盘（VC）角度失配的学习行走模型部署到实体机器人上，在真实室外环境中验证其能否复现沙漠蚂蚁学习行走的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙漠蚂蚁在觅食前会进行学习行走以获取巢周围视觉记忆，其轨迹随经验扩展、变直且视觉扫描减少；作者希望检验其提出的PI–VC角度失配机制能否在真实机器人的传感与运动噪声下产生这些行为。

Method: 作者在配备全景相机、IMU和轮式编码器的四轮机器人上实现该模型，并在复刻沙漠蚂蚁实验的室外环境中测试；PI–VC失配调控行走速度、转向、旋转行为（pirouettes）和归巢，PI为视觉学习提供教学信号，机器人自主决定何时获取视觉记忆。

Result: 机器人复现了沙漠蚂蚁学习行走的特征，包括轨迹逐渐扩展和变直、旋转行为频率下降以及视觉归巢性能提高；其行为随视觉学习进展从探索逐渐转向觅食。

Conclusion: 结果表明，简单的误差驱动机制可在真实世界条件下产生学习行走的关键特征，支持其作为沙漠蚂蚁主动视觉学习解释的合理性，并说明实体机器人可用于检验生物学模型。

Abstract: Before foraging, desert ants perform learning walks (LWs) to acquire visual memories around their nest. With experience, these walks expand, become straighter, and contain fewer visual scans. We previously proposed a model in which these behaviours emerge from the angular mismatch between path integration (PI) and a mushroom-body-inspired visual compass (VC). Here, we test whether this mechanism can generate learning walks on a physical robot, despite the sensory and motor noise inherent to real-world conditions. We implement the model on a four-wheeled robot equipped with a panoramic camera, an IMU and wheel encoders providing compass and stride information for PI, and test it outdoors using a replicated desert-ant experimental setup. The PI--VC mismatch regulates walking speed, turning, pirouettes, and returns to the nest, while PI provides a teaching signal for visual learning. The robot autonomously decides when to acquire visual memories, while its behaviour gradually shifts from exploration towards foraging as visual learning progresses. The robot reproduces desert-ant LW features, including expanding and straightening trajectories, decreasing pirouette frequency, and improved visual homing. These results demonstrate that a simple error-driven mechanism can generate key features of learning walks under real-world conditions, supporting its plausibility as an explanation for active visual learning in desert ants. More broadly, this work illustrates how physical robots can be used to test biological models.

</details>


### [142] [SPARK: Self-Correcting Embodied Memory via Physical Re-Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00344)
*Dmytro Kurdydyk,Aadi Palnitkar,Sebastian Scherer,Seungchan Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARK 是一个面向具身 RAG 的多回合纠错框架：通过语义批评器定位知识库问题区域、定向规划重访，并选择性合并新观测以增删改知识库信息，从而提升具身问答性能并减少冗余存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身 RAG 方法通常假设单回合知识库可靠，或在多回合中仅累积观测而不纠正先前错误，导致语义标签错误、物体位置不准和空间关系不一致等问题持续存在。

Method: 提出 SPARK：首回合后，语义批评器标记知识库中需检查的区域，定向规划器生成重访路径，选择性合并模块依据新观测对知识库进行添加、删除或替换。在 AirSim 四个户外地图、两种退化视角条件下评估，使用感知构建的知识库和仅用于评估的可见性约束 oracle，对固定 158 个具身问答问题在纠正前后评分。

Result: 单次纠错回合在两种条件下均提升所有地图的下游 EQA，且相比仅追加更新少存储 13-41% 的物体，某一地图-条件组合提升达 2.7 倍；纠错扩展至第 4 回合后，所有地图 EQA 均超过其未退化参考。消融显示批评器三类信号均有贡献；同等预算下定向重访平均增益高于非定向重访；选择性合并以更小知识库达到与仅追加更新相当的增益。

Conclusion: SPARK 表明，通过定向再观测纠正具身记忆具有重要价值，尤其随着具身系统规模扩大、累积错误风险增加时更为关键。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems have seen growing adoption in embodied settings as language-queryable memories of the environment. However, existing approaches often assume a reliable single-episode knowledge base (KB) or accumulate observations across episodes without correcting prior errors. Such errors can persist through incorrect semantic labels, inaccurate object positions, and inconsistent spatial relationships. To address this, we present SPARK, a corrective multi-episode framework for embodied RAG. After the first episode, a semantic critic flags regions of the KB, a targeted planner generates revisits, and a selective merge adds, removes, or replaces KB information based on the new observations. We evaluate SPARK on four outdoor AirSim maps under two degraded viewpoint conditions, using perception-built KBs, a visibility-constrained oracle used only for evaluation, and a fixed set of 158 embodied question answering (EQA) questions scored before and after correction. A single corrective episode improves downstream EQA on every map under both conditions while storing 13-41% fewer objects than append-only updating, with gains up to 2.7x on one map-condition pair. Extending correction through Episode 4 brings every map above the EQA performance of its undegraded reference. Ablations show that the critic's three signals each contribute to performance, that targeted revisits achieve a larger mean gain than untargeted ones under the same budget, and that selective merging achieves gains comparable to append-only updating with a smaller KB. Overall, SPARK demonstrates the value of correcting embodied memory through targeted re-observation, particularly as embodied systems scale and the risk of accumulated errors increases.

</details>


### [143] [Teach, Abstract, Apply: A Framework for Dialogue-Driven Task Learning with Collaborative Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00297)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sherzod Hakimov,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Teach-Abstract-Apply（TAA）框架，使操作者可用自然语言教会协作机器人新工作流，将其抽象为可复用技能，并在不同场景中应用；在结构搭建任务上验证了技能抽象能提升成功率并降低交互成本，初步人机实验表明也可处理人类指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人需在共享工作空间中辅助托盘化、联合装配、零件检测等任务，这些任务常含重复流程且会随生产环境和任务规格变化；因此需要直观接口，让现场工人修改工作流并将例程泛化复用。

Method: 提出 TAA 框架：人类操作者以自然语言教授新工作流，系统将教授的工作流抽象为技能以供后续使用，并在不同设置中应用这些技能。案例研究为协作结构搭建任务，使用开源权重模型 Qwen3-Coder 与闭源模型 GPT5.2-Codex，在自博弈和跨模型配置下评估，并开展初步 human-in-the-loop 研究。

Result: 结果表明，技能抽象提高了任务成功率并降低了交互成本；除自动化评估外，初步人在回路研究表明该流程也能在人类指令下运行。

Conclusion: TAA 为协作机器人提供了一种自然语言驱动、可抽象复用工作流的直观编程与泛化框架，并在结构搭建案例及初步人类交互中显示出有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative robots (cobots) are designed to be safer and more interactive than traditional industrial robots, share the same workspace as humans, and assist them in a range of shop-floor tasks such as palletization, joint assembly, and part inspection. Many of these tasks involve repetitive workflows that may evolve with changes in production environments and task specifications. These requirements motivate the need for intuitive interfaces that allow workers to modify workflows on the shop floor and generalise these routines for future use. This work introduces the Teach-Abstract-Apply (TAA) framework, which enables human operators to (a) teach new workflows to cobots in natural language, (b) abstract the taught workflows into skills for later use, and (c) apply these skills in different settings. As a case study, we apply the TAA framework to a collaborative structure-building task and evaluate it with both open-weight (Qwen3-Coder) and closed (GPT5.2-Codex) models in self-play and cross-model configurations. Our results show that skill abstraction improves task success rates and reduces interaction cost. Beyond automated evaluation, a preliminary human-in-the-loop study suggests that the pipeline also works with human instructions.

</details>


### [144] [Does Validation Loss Predict Real-Robot Success? Evidence from Four Imitation-Learning Policies on a Low-Cost Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00283)
*Simon Cowling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统测量离线 held-out 验证损失/动作误差与 SO-101 真机闭环成功率的关系，发现离线指标能诊断学习但跨策略排序会反转，不能替代实机评测，并建议将“未启动失败”和“已启动后的成功率”分开报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: held-out 验证损失是选择 checkpoint 和比较模型的常用离线信号，但其与真实机器人闭环任务成功率的关系很少被定量验证，作者希望判断离线指标是否可靠、何时会误导模型选择。

Method: 在 SO-101 机械臂的 pick-and-place 任务上，用 12 到 200 条演示的嵌套子集训练 ACT、Diffusion Policy、SmolVLA 和 π0.5（仅 action expert）。对 82 个 checkpoint 在 25 条 held-out 演示上计算训练损失和输出动作误差，并与 930 次 bench rollout 比较；还评估 70 个训练 run 的全部 700 个中间 checkpoint，并从前置相机恢复所有训练、held-out 和 bench episode 的球位置。

Result: 策略内，held-out 动作误差与 bench 成功率相关（Spearman ρ 为 -0.23 到 -0.88），但不强于仅用训练 episode 数，且在相同数据的不同 seed 间不相关。跨策略排序反转（200 episodes 时 ρ=+0.90）：held-out 误差最低的策略仅 110 次 rollout 成功 3 次。最常见失败是机械臂从未离开 rest pose，占 Diffusion Policy 未裁剪演示训练的 96% rollout 和 SmolVLA 的 47%，而该失败不会出现在由演示构成的 held-out 集中。在机械臂已移动的 rollout 中，SmolVLA 的 held-out 动作误差能较好预测成功（ρ=-0.81）。对 diffusion 和 flow-matching 策略，训练中 held-out loss 上升 2 到 5 倍，而 held-out 动作误差最多下降 29%。

Conclusion: Held-out 指标是判断学习是否发生的有效诊断，但不能替代真实机器人评测；benchmark 应分别报告 initiation failure 与已启动 rollout 中的成功率，以避免离线指标和闭环表现之间的错配。

Abstract: Held-out validation loss is the standard offline signal for choosing checkpoints and comparing models, but its relation to closed-loop success on a real robot is rarely measured. We measure it for ACT, Diffusion Policy, SmolVLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ (action expert only), trained on nested subsets of 12 to 200 demonstrations of a pick-and-place task on an SO-101 arm. We score 82 checkpoints on 25 held-out demonstrations, with each policy's training loss and with the error of the actions it outputs, and compare the scores with 930 bench rollouts. We also score all 700 intermediate checkpoints of 70 training runs, and recover the ball positions of every training, held-out and bench episode from the front camera. Within each policy, hold-out action error correlates with bench success (Spearman $\rho$ from -0.23 to -0.88), but no more strongly than the number of training episodes alone, and not at all among seeds trained on the same data. Across policies the ranking is inverted ($\rho$ = +0.90 at 200 episodes): the policy with the lowest hold-out error succeeded in 3 of 110 rollouts. The most common failure, the arm never leaving its rest pose, ended 96% of rollouts for Diffusion Policy trained on untrimmed demonstrations and 47% for SmolVLA, and cannot occur in a hold-out set made of demonstrations. Among rollouts in which the arm moved, SmolVLA's hold-out action error predicts success well ($\rho$ = -0.81). For the diffusion and flow-matching policies, hold-out loss rises two- to five-fold during training while hold-out action error falls by up to 29%. Held-out metrics are a sound diagnostic of learning but no substitute for evaluation on the robot, and we recommend reporting initiation failure and success among started rollouts as separate bench outcomes.

</details>


### [145] [Supervised Autonomous Bimanual Suction and Cauterization for Bleeding Control in Low Grade Splenic Trauma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00237)
*Francis Fan,Mackenzie A Eagleson,Yvonne Zhang,Hongting Pan,Lujia Yang,Ethan Kilmer,Jiawei Ge,Nural Yilmaz,Avinash Dhimal,Ahmed Ghazi,Jeffrey Jopling,Laura Connolly,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种监督自主双臂机器人框架，用于低级别脾创伤的出血点电外科凝血，并在体外脾出血模型上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失控性出血是可预防性创伤死亡的主要原因之一，及时干预并非总能获得，因此能够辅助止血的自主系统具有潜力；鉴于任务安全关键，采用人类监督下由系统执行技术步骤的监督自主是必要过渡。

Method: 系统集成实时出血分割、无标记工具关键点跟踪和深度工具-组织接触检测，协调两个机械臂同步完成吸引与电外科凝血；在新颖的插管体外脾出血模型上进行评估。

Result: 在七种不同损伤中，系统实现平均82.9%的出血表面凝血覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了使用监督自主双臂系统在损伤部位实施靶向凝血的可行性。

Abstract: Uncontrolled hemorrhage remains a leading cause of preventable trauma mortality. Timely intervention is not always available, making autonomous systems that can aid in bleeding control a promising alternative. Given the safety-critical nature of the task, supervised autonomy, in which the system executes the technical steps while a human retains oversight, represents a necessary intermediate step toward this goal. In this work, we present a supervised autonomous bimanual robotic framework for injury cauterization (eletrosurgical coagulation) in low-grade splenic trauma. The system integrates real-time bleeding segmentation, markerless tool keypoint tracking, and deep tool-tissue contact detection to coordinate two robotic arms for synchronized suction and cauterization. Evaluated on a novel, cannulated ex vivo splenic hemorrhage model, the system achieved an average bleeding surface cauterization coverage of 82.9% across seven distinct injuries. This work demonstrates the feasibility of using a supervised autonomous bimanual system to deliver targeted coagulation across an injury site.

</details>


### [146] [Anchor 1: Precise and Stable Humanoid Control for Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00193)
*Archon Robotics,Caojun Wang,Checheng Yu,Gangcheng Jiang,Guangjun Zeng,Haoran Jiang,Hongyang Li,Huibin Yan,Huijie Wang,Jialong Zeng,Jin Yang,Kai Cheng,Li Chen,Liang Chen,Lirui Zhao,Modi Shi,Penglin Fu,Shijia Peng,Tianyu Li,Wencong Zhang,Xuechong Lv,Yixuan Pan,Yong Zhang,Yu Qiao,Yu Zhang,Ziyan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Anchor 1 是一个面向精确、稳定全身操作的人形机器人全身控制器，并配套覆盖多种协调需求的基准测试；通过校准稀疏运动参考、离线筛选训练分布和解耦显式误差反馈，在仿真与实物上提升跟踪精度、稳定性及下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形控制器已具备多样化运动技能，但将其转化为精确且稳定的全身操作仍困难；操作任务对执行偏差敏感，微小误差或非预期动作即可导致失败，因此需要兼顾精度、稳定性与全身协调的控制方法及系统评测。

Method: 提出 Anchor 1 全身控制器与基准。控制器采用校准稀疏运动参考，保留任务相关人体运动结构且不做在线关节空间重定向，从而保留运动学自由度用于精确控制；通过离线筛选与这些校准稀疏参考配对的机器人运动来塑造训练分布，引导稳定且物理合理的全身协调；引入与运动编码解耦的显式误差反馈，提高控制精度并支持灵活命令接口。基准评估精度、持续执行稳定性和可达性，覆盖多样全身协调需求。

Result: 仿真与硬件评估显示在所有评测轨道上精度和稳定性更强；在最严格的真实世界全局评分设置下，相比现有控制器，Anchor 1 在更少前瞻输入且无需重定向条件下将跟踪误差降低 30% 至 80%；下游实验表明同一部署流程下遥操作数据采集更高效、任务成功率更高。

Conclusion: Anchor 1 可作为精确且稳定全身控制的实用基础，支撑人形机器人任务执行。

Abstract: Humanoid controllers have acquired increasingly diverse motor skills, yet translating these capabilities into precise and stable whole-body manipulation remains challenging. Manipulation tasks are sensitive to execution deviations -- even small errors or unintended steps can cause failure. Introducing Anchor 1, a whole-body controller for precise and stable whole-body manipulation, together with a benchmark spanning diverse coordination demands. Anchor 1 adopts calibrated sparse motion references to preserve task-relevant human motion structure without online joint-space retargeting, leaving kinematic freedom for precise control. To guide this freedom towards stable, physically plausible whole-body coordination, we shape the training distribution through offline curation of robot motions paired with these calibrated sparse references. We further introduce explicit error feedback decoupled from motion encoding, improving control precision while supporting flexible command interfaces. To achieve a systematic evaluation of these capabilities, a benchmark is proposed to assess precision, sustained execution stability, and reachability across diverse whole-body coordination demands. Evaluations in simulation and on hardware show stronger precision and stability across all evaluation tracks. Under the most strict real-world global scoring setting, Anchor 1 achieves a 30\% to 80\% decrease in tracking error with fewer look-ahead inputs, and without retargeting compared to all existing controllers. Downstream experiments demonstrate more efficient teleoperated data collection and higher task success using the same deployment pipeline as well. We believe Anchor 1 supports precise and stable whole-body control as a practical basis for humanoid task execution.

</details>


### [147] [Learning to Live: A Step Toward Lifelong Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00180)
*Lirong Che,Peidong Wang,Yimeng Su,Jiong Wang,Yang Chen,Wenbo Fu,Zhenyu Huang,Tiancheng Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Dongli Xie,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Daqi Liu,Jian Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIFE 是一个让机器人在持续变化的环境中通过观察、选择性记忆并将经验转化为新能力的终身学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体通常围绕单个任务组织，但物理世界在任务之间和任务执行过程中持续变化，因此机器人需要能够从长期经验中积累记忆与能力。

Method: LIFE 框架包含：RoboBeats 轻量级流式门控，用于跟踪智能体设定的条件、发现新事件并决定何时响应或写入记忆；Plastic Memory 以扁平结构存储图像和结构化记录并保留源证据，支持后续按需添加字段和索引；Explore-Distill-Mount（EDM）在现有工具不足时，将问题求解中发现的方案转化为经验证、可复用的工具和技能。

Result: 在匹配协议下，RoboBeats 相比基座模型显著提升条件跟踪能力；在相同写入预算下，其记忆问答保留的有用证据多于均匀采样；小规模案例研究通过回放真实机器人连续录制的24小时视频，展示了集成系统如何从一天经验中构建记忆并回答日常问题。

Conclusion: LIFE 朝着让机器人更熟悉周围环境、并能通过经验变得更 capable 的方向迈进一步：不仅学会完成任务，也学会在真实世界中生活。

Abstract: Embodied agents are usually organized around individual tasks, yet the physical world keeps changing between tasks and during execution. We introduce LIFE (Lifelong Intelligence from Experience), a framework in which a robot observes continuously, remembers selectively, and turns experience into new capabilities. RoboBeats, a family of lightweight streaming gates, tracks conditions set by the agent and discovers new events, deciding when an observation warrants a response or a memory write. Selected observations enter Plastic Memory, a flat store of images and structured records that keeps the source evidence, so new fields and indexes can be added when later questions need them. When existing tools fall short, Explore-Distill-Mount (EDM) turns solutions found during problem solving into validated, reusable tools and skills. Under matched protocols, RoboBeats substantially improves condition tracking over its base models and, at the same writing budget, retains more useful evidence for memory question answering than uniform sampling. A case study of limited scale, replaying 24 hours of continuous video recorded by a real robot, illustrates how the integrated system builds memory from a day of experience and uses it to answer everyday questions. LIFE takes a step toward robots that become more familiar with their surroundings and more capable through experience: learning not only to complete tasks, but to live in the world.

</details>


### [148] [Scaling Trusted Autonomy: Towards Generally Capable Verifiable Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00158)
*Benedict Quartey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经符号方法，将基础模型/学习策略的泛化能力与符号规划的显式结构结合，以构建可验证、可解释且可信的机器人自主系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型驱动机器人虽能理解开放语言、识别新物体并适应多样环境，但无法保证其真正理解请求、动作产生预期效果或内部表示与现实一致；经典符号规划结构显式但依赖手工技能与表示，学习策略泛化强但缺乏行为保证。

Method: 论文探索三个互补方向：LIMP利用基础模型与空间推理，将自然语言指令落地为显式任务约束和指令特定语义地图，支持任务与运动规划并暴露机器人解释；REALM维护可编辑的照片级真实3D高斯场景，预测规划操作后果并局部协调场景，使后续指令基于更新状态进行接地与规划；PACTS学习视觉运动技能，联合生成动作轨迹及其符号效果预测，支持技能组合、进度监控与重规划恢复。

Result: 三项工作分别在语言指令接地与可检查解释、场景状态更新与操作后果预测、技能组合与符号效果监控方面提供显式结构，同时扩展了结构的获取方式。

Conclusion: 该论文为通用能力机器人奠定神经符号基础，使机器人行为能够在其模型假设下被评估、推理和验证，从而提升可信自主性。

Abstract: Robots powered by foundation models are increasingly able to interpret open-ended language, recognize unfamiliar objects, and operate across diverse environments. Yet broader capability does not establish that a robot has understood a request, that its actions will produce the intended effects, or that its internal representation still corresponds to reality. Classical symbolic planning provides explicit structure for composition and verification, but traditionally depends on hand-engineered skills and representations. Learned policies can broaden the robot's repertoire, but provide weak guarantees about what will happen and why. This thesis argues that scaling trusted autonomy requires a neurosymbolic approach that unifies the generality of learned models with symbolic structure for robust robot behavior. It explores three complementary directions. First, LIMP uses foundation models and spatial reasoning to ground natural-language instructions in explicit task constraints and instruction-specific semantic maps, enabling task-and-motion planning while exposing the robot's interpretation for inspection. Second, REALM maintains an editable, photorealistic 3D Gaussian scene, predicts the consequences of planned manipulation, and locally reconciles the scene so that subsequent instructions obtain their grounding and planning geometry from an updated state. Third, PACTS learns visuomotor skills that jointly generate action trajectories and predictions of their symbolic effects, supporting skill composition, progress monitoring, and recovery through replanning. Together, these contributions preserve explicit structure for reasoning and verification while broadening how it is obtained, providing a foundation for generally capable robots whose behavior can be assessed under the assumptions of their models.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Touching Projected Agents through the DAT Screen -System Design and Questions of Interaction Boundaries-](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02525)
*Takaya Tamamoto,Tomoko Yonezawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出桌面式可形变触控屏 DAT：将空间投影、触摸传感与电机控制抗压阻力结合，使触摸虚拟代理时屏幕可形变并反馈阻力，同时提出触控虚拟代理是否应表现偏好或边界的设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触摸屏只能检测手指接触，缺乏接触面形变、抗压反馈以及虚拟代理对触摸行为的响应能力，因此需要探索更具物理交互感的触控虚拟代理系统。

Method: 构建 DAT 屏幕，将投影映射到半透明可形变网格，并进行触摸传感与压入阻力控制。初始原型通过柔性投影屏周围张力变化检测单点触摸并估计位置和深度；后续原型加入电机张力控制以调节屏幕抗压入阻力，并展示张力控制版 DAT。

Result: 初始原型实现了单点触摸的位置与深度估计；后续原型实现了可调的抗压阻力，并给出系统演示。论文区分了已观察到的系统能力与仍待研究的设计问题。

Conclusion: DAT 展示了可形变、可感知且可控制阻力的触控虚拟代理交互平台，并提出触控虚拟代理是否以及何时应表现出偏好或边界的设计问题；但本文未实证用户如何理解代理的拒绝或同意。

Abstract: Touching a virtual agent involves more than detecting a finger on a display: the contact surface can deform and resist the user's push, and the agent can respond to that action. We describe the Desktop Amid Touch (DAT) screen, which combines spatial projection onto a semi-transparent, deformable mesh with touch sensing and motorized control of resistance to indentation. An initial prototype detected a single touch and estimated its position and depth from tension changes around a flexible projection screen. A subsequent prototype added motorized tension control to vary the screen's resistance to indentation. We describe the tension-controlled DAT implementation and its demonstrations, distinguishing what has been observed from what remains an open design question. These capabilities raise a design question: should touchable virtual agents respond as though they had preferences or boundaries, and under what conditions should such behavior be implemented on systems like DAT. The present paper describes a system and frames this question; it does not establish how users interpret an agent's refusal or consent.

</details>
